## Canada Cooperatives Act {#canada-cooperatives-act .Title-of-Act}

[S.C.]{.abbr title="Statutes of Canada"} 1998, c. 1
:::

Assented to 1998-03-31

An Act respecting cooperatives
::::

::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
###### Preamble {#preamble .MarginalNote}

WHEREAS cooperatives in Canada carry on business in accordance with
internationally recognized cooperative principles;

WHEREAS cooperatives work for the social and economic development of
their communities through policies approved by their members;

WHEREAS the Government of Canada has determined that it is desirable to
modernize the law governing those cooperatives;
:::

::: Enacts
NOW, THEREFORE, Her Majesty, by and with the advice and consent of the
Senate and House of Commons of Canada, enacts as follows:
:::

## [Short Title]{.HTitleText1} {#h-44933 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Short title

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** This Act may be cited as
the [Canada Cooperatives Act](/eng/acts/C-1.7).

## [PART 1]{.HLabel1}[Interpretation and Application]{.HTitleText1} {#h-44936 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-44937 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  definitions in this subsection apply in this Act.

  [[affairs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[affairs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the relationship between a
      cooperative and its affiliates and their members, shareholders,
      directors and officers, but does not include the business carried
      on by those bodies corporate. ([affaires
      internes]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[affiliate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[affiliate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an affiliated body
      corporate within the meaning of subsection
      (2). ([groupe]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[articles]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[articles]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the original or restated
      articles of incorporation, articles of amendment, articles of
      amalgamation, articles of continuance, articles of reorganization,
      articles of arrangement, articles of dissolution, articles of
      revival and articles of correction, and includes any amendments to
      any of them. ([statuts]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[associate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[associate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a relationship
      with a person, means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a body corporate of which the person
        beneficially owns or controls, directly or indirectly, shares or
        securities currently convertible into shares carrying more than
        ten per cent of the voting rights under all circumstances or by
        reason of the occurrence of an event that has occurred and is
        continuing, or a currently exercisable option or right to
        purchase such shares or such convertible securities;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a cooperative entity of which the person
        beneficially owns more than ten per cent of the voting rights
        that can be cast at a meeting of the cooperative entity;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an unincorporated entity of which the person
        beneficially owns more than ten per cent of the ownership
        interests;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a partner of the person acting on behalf of the
        partnership of which they are partners;

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a trust or an estate or succession in which the
        person has a substantial beneficial interest or serves as a
        trustee, administrator, executor or liquidator of a succession
        or in a similar capacity;

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a spouse of the person or an individual who is
        cohabiting with the person in a conjugal relationship, having so
        cohabited for a period of at least one year;

      - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a child of the person or of the spouse or
        individual referred to in paragraph (f); and

      - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a relative of the person or of the spouse or
        individual referred to in paragraph (f), if that relative has
        the same residence as the person. ([liens]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[auditor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[auditor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a firm of
      accountants. ([vérificateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[bearer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[bearer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a security, means
      the person who is in possession of a security that is payable to
      bearer or endorsed in blank. ([porteur]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[beneficial ownership]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[beneficial ownership]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in Quebec, the rights of a beneficiary of a
        trust that owns property, and ownership of property through one
        or more administrators of the property of others, mandataries or
        other legal representatives; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the rest of Canada, ownership of property
        through one or more trustees, legal representatives, agents or
        other intermediaries. ([véritable propriétaire]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[body corporate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[body corporate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a cooperative, a
      cooperative entity, a company or any other body corporate wherever
      or however incorporated. ([personne morale]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[call]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[call]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an option transferable by
      delivery, to demand delivery of a specified number or amount of
      securities at a fixed price within a specified time but does not
      include an option or right to acquire securities of the
      cooperative that granted the option or right to acquire. ([option
      d'achat]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[cooperative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[cooperative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a body corporate that is
      incorporated under this Act and not discontinued under this
      Act. ([coopérative]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[cooperative entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[cooperative entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a body corporate
      that, by the law under which it is organized and operated, must be
      organized and operated on --- and is organized and operated on ---
      cooperative principles. ([entité coopérative]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[court]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[court]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the court of first instance of
      superior jurisdiction of a province. ([tribunal]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[debt obligation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[debt obligation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a bond, debenture,
      note or other evidence of indebtedness of an entity, whether
      secured or unsecured. ([titre de créance]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[delegate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[delegate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual who is
      appointed or elected to represent a member at a meeting of
      members. ([délégué]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Director]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Director]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Director appointed
      under section 371. ([directeur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[director]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[director]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual occupying the
      position of director, by whatever name called, of a cooperative,
      and "board of directors" or "directors" refers to the directors as
      a body. ([administrateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[distributing cooperative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[distributing cooperative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, subject to
      subsections 4(4) and (5), a distributing cooperative as defined in
      the regulations. ([coopérative ayant fait appel au
      public]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a body corporate, a trust, a
      partnership, a fund or an unincorporated
      organization. ([entité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[federation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[federation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a cooperative whose
      membership is composed substantially of other cooperative
      entities, federations or leagues of cooperative
      entities. ([fédération]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[firm of accountants]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[firm of accountants]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a partnership
      whose partners and accountant employees are accountants engaged in
      the practice of accounting, or a body corporate that is
      incorporated by or under an Act of the legislature of a province
      and engaged in the practice of accounting. ([cabinet de
      comptables]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[former Act cooperative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[former Act cooperative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a cooperative
      that was incorporated under the Canada Cooperative Associations
      Act. ([coopérative sous le régime de l'ancienne
      loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[holder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[holder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a security certificate, the
        person in possession of the certificate issued or endorsed to
        the person or to bearer or in blank;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the ownership of a membership
        share, the person referred to in section 5; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the ownership of an investment
        share, the person referred to in section
        6. ([détenteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[incapable]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[incapable]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an individual,
      means that the individual is found, under the laws of a province,
      to be unable, other than by reason of minority, to manage their
      property or is declared to be incapable by any court in a
      jurisdiction outside Canada. ([incapable]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[incorporated]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[incorporated]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, when used with reference to
      a body corporate that is incorporated by or under an Act of
      Parliament or of the legislature of a province, includes a body
      corporate that is continued by or under any such
      Act. ([constitué]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[incorporator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[incorporator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who signs
      articles of incorporation. ([fondateur]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[individual]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[individual]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a natural
      person. ([particulier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[issuer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[issuer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a security, means
      the entity that issues the security. ([émetteur]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[investment share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[investment share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a share in the
      capital of a cooperative that is not a membership share. ([part de
      placement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[meeting of a cooperative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[meeting of a cooperative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a meeting of members of a cooperative, or of a
        class of members of a cooperative, or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a meeting of the holders of investment shares
        of a cooperative, or a class or series of investment shares,

      as the context permits. ([assemblée d'une
      coopérative]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[member]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[member]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a member of a cooperative,
      but does not include an auxiliary
      member. ([membre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[member loan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[member loan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a loan required by the
      cooperative from its members as a condition of membership or to
      continue membership in the cooperative, and, for the purpose of
      Parts 8, 16, 17, 18.1 and 19 and subsection 163(2), a member loan
      is deemed to be a membership share issued at par value. ([prêt de
      membre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[membership share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[membership share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a share described
      in section 118. ([part de membre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the member of the Queen's
      Privy Council for Canada that is designated by the Governor in
      Council as the Minister for the purposes of this
      Act. ([ministre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes the chairperson of the
      board of directors, a vice-chairperson of the board of directors,
      the president, a vice-president, the secretary, an assistant
      secretary, the treasurer, an assistant treasurer and the general
      manager of a cooperative, and any other individual designated as
      an officer of the cooperative by by-law or by resolution of the
      directors, or any other individual who performs functions for the
      cooperative similar to those normally performed by an individual
      occupying any such office. ([dirigeant]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[ordinary resolution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ordinary resolution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a resolution
      passed by a majority --- or any greater number set out in the
      articles or a unanimous agreement --- of the votes cast by or on
      behalf of the persons who are entitled to vote in respect of the
      resolution. ([résolution ordinaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[patronage return]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[patronage return]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an amount that the
      cooperative allocates among and credits or pays to its members or
      to its member and non-member patrons based on the business done by
      them with or through the
      cooperative. ([ristourne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual or an entity,
      and includes a personal
      representative. ([personne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[personal representative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[personal representative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who
      stands in place of and represents another person including, but
      not limited to, a trustee, an executor, an administrator, a
      liquidator of a succession, an administrator of the property of
      others, a guardian or tutor, a curator, a receiver or
      sequestrator, an agent or mandatary or an attorney. ([représentant
      personnel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means prescribed by
      regulation. ([Version anglaise
      seulement]{.DefinitionOtherLangOnly})

  [[prior legislation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[prior legislation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Canada
      Cooperative Associations Act, chapter 6 of the Statutes of Canada,
      1970-71-72. ([législation antérieure]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[proxy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[proxy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a completed and executed or,
      in Quebec, signed form of proxy by means of which a shareholder
      appoints a proxyholder to attend and act on the shareholder's
      behalf at a meeting of the
      shareholders. ([procuration]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[put]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[put]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an option transferable by
      delivery, to deliver a specified number or amount of securities at
      a fixed price within a specified time. ([option de
      vente]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[redeemable]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[redeemable]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, with respect to an investment
      share issued by a cooperative, means that the share is one

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that the cooperative may acquire or redeem on
        the demand of the cooperative; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that the cooperative is required by its
        articles to acquire or redeem at a specified time or on the
        demand of the holder. ([rachetable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[security]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[security]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes an investment share, a
      debt obligation of a cooperative and a certificate evidencing such
      a share or debt obligation and, for the purposes of section 173
      and Parts 18.1 and 19, includes a membership share. ([valeur
      mobilière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[security interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[security interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an interest or
      right in or charge on property of a cooperative to secure payment
      of a debt or the performance of an obligation of the
      cooperative. ([sûreté]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[send]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[send]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes
      deliver. ([envoyer]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[series]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[series]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of investment shares,
      means a division of a class of those
      shares. ([série]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a membership share or an
      investment share. ([part]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[shareholder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[shareholder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person described in
      section 6. ([détenteur de parts de placement]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[special resolution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[special resolution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a resolution
      passed by not less than two thirds --- or any greater number set
      out in the articles or a unanimous agreement --- of the votes cast
      by or on behalf of the persons who are entitled to vote in respect
      of the resolution. ([résolution spéciale]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[unanimous agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[unanimous agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an agreement in
      writing among all members and all shareholders, if any, that
      contains a provision referred to in subsection
      115(1). ([convention unanime]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Affiliates

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a body corporate is affiliated with another body
    corporate if one of them is a subsidiary of the other, if both are
    subsidiaries of the same body corporate or if each of them is
    controlled by the same person; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if two bodies corporate are affiliated with the
    same body corporate at the same time, they are deemed to be
    affiliated with each other.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minors

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, the word
  [[minor]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} has the same meaning as in the
  applicable provincial law and, in the absence of any such law, has the
  same meaning as the word [[child]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in the United
  Nations Convention on the Rights of the Child, adopted in the United
  Nations General Assembly on November 20, 1989.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 2
- 2000, c. 12, s. 28
- 2001, c. 14, s. 137
- 2011, c. 21, s. 72
- 2018, c. 8, s. 47
:::

### [Purpose and Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45018 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Purposes of Act

- **[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  purposes of this Act are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to set out the law applicable to the business
    endeavours of persons who have associated themselves in a democratic
    manner to carry on a common purpose; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to advance the cause of uniformity of cooperative
    business law in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Carrying on business in more than one
  place

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No cooperative may be incorporated under this Act
  unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it will carry on its undertaking in two or more
    provinces; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it will have a fixed place of business in more than
    one province.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} This Act applies to cooperatives incorporated under
  this Act that have not been discontinued.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of certain Acts

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} No provision of the [Canada Business Corporations
  Act](/eng/acts/C-44), the [Canada Not-for-profit Corporations
  Act](/eng/acts/C-7.75) or the [Winding-up and Restructuring
  Act](/eng/acts/W-11) applies to a cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation on business that can be
  carried on

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} No cooperative may carry on the business of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a bank;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a company to which the [Insurance Companies
    Act](/eng/acts/I-11.8) applies;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a company to which the [Trust and Loan Companies
    Act](/eng/acts/T-19.8) applies; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an association to which the [Cooperative Credit
    Associations Act](/eng/acts/C-41.01) applies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition on granting degrees

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} No cooperative may carry on business as a
  degree-granting educational institution unless expressly authorized to
  do so by a federal or provincial agent that by law has the power to
  confer degree-granting authority on an educational institution.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 3
- 2009, c. 23, ss. 312, 347
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-3-20111017.html "Link to previous version of section 3"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Control

- **[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of this Act,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person controls a body corporate if securities of
    the body corporate to which are attached more than fifty per cent of
    the votes that may be cast to elect directors of the body corporate
    are beneficially owned by the person and the votes attached to those
    securities are sufficient, if exercised, to elect a majority of the
    directors of the body corporate; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person controls a cooperative entity if the
    person has the right to exercise more than fifty per cent of the
    votes that may be cast at an annual meeting or to appoint or elect a
    majority of the directors.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Holding body corporate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A body corporate is the holding body corporate of
  another if that other body corporate is its subsidiary.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsidiary

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A body corporate is a subsidiary of another body
  corporate if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is controlled by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that other body corporate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} that other body corporate and one or more bodies
      corporate each of which is controlled by that other body
      corporate, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} two or more bodies corporate each of which is
      controlled by that other body corporate; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is a subsidiary of a body corporate that is a
    subsidiary of that other body corporate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemptions --- on application by
  cooperative

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} On the application of a cooperative, the Director may
  determine that the cooperative is not or was not a distributing
  cooperative if the Director is satisfied that the determination would
  not be prejudicial to the public interest.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemptions --- classes of cooperatives

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Director may determine that a class of
  cooperatives are not or were not distributing cooperatives if the
  Director is satisfied that the determination would not be prejudicial
  to the public interest.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2001, c. 14, s. 138\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 4
- 2001, c. 14, s. 138
:::

### [Share Ownership]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45063 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Membership share ownership

**[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** A member is a holder of a
membership share of a cooperative when, according to the members'
register of the cooperative, the member is the owner of the membership
share or is entitled to be entered in the members' register or like
record of the cooperative as the owner of the membership share.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Investment share ownership

**[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** A person is a shareholder
of a cooperative when, according to the securities register of the
cooperative, the person is the owner of an investment share or is
entitled to be entered in the securities register or like record of the
cooperative as the owner of the investment share.

### [Cooperative Basis]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45068 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cooperative basis

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of this Act, a cooperative is organized and operated, and
  carries on business, on a cooperative basis if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} membership in the cooperative is open, in a
    non-discriminatory manner, to persons who can use the services of
    the cooperative and who are willing and able to accept the
    responsibilities of membership;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} each member or delegate has only one vote;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} no member or delegate may vote by proxy;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} interest on any member loan is limited to a maximum
    percentage fixed in the articles;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} dividends on any membership share are limited to
    the maximum percentage fixed in the articles;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} to the extent feasible, members provide the capital
    required by the cooperative, with the return paid on member capital
    not to exceed the maximum percentage specified in the articles;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} surplus funds arising from the cooperative's
    operations are used

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to develop its business,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to provide or improve common services to
      members,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} to provide for reserves or the payment of
      interest on member loans or dividends on membership shares and
      investment shares,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} for community welfare or the propagation of
      cooperative enterprises, or

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} as a distribution among its members as a
      patronage return; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} it educates its members, officers, employees and
    the public on the principles and techniques of cooperative
    enterprise.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restrictions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(a) is subject to any restrictions on
  the classes of persons to which membership may be available that may
  reasonably relate to any business restriction set out in the articles
  of the cooperative and to the reasonable commercial ability of the
  cooperative to provide services to prospective members, as long as the
  restrictions are consistent with applicable laws with respect to human
  rights.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(b),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the articles of a federation may provide that the
    members or delegates of the federation have more than one vote;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if, before the coming into force of this
    subsection, the articles of association or charter by-laws of a
    former Act cooperative provide that a member or delegate of the
    former Act cooperative has more than one vote, the articles filed
    under subsection 379(2) may contain the same provisions with respect
    to voting rights of members or delegates;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any provision referred to in paragraph (b) is
    deemed to be contained in the articles of the cooperative until it
    complies with subsection 379(2); and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the articles of a cooperative may provide that
    subject to the conditions stated in the articles, a cooperative
    entity may have more than one vote in a cooperative.

## [PART 2]{.HLabel1}[Incorporation, Structure and Organization]{.HTitleText1} {#h-45093 .Part}

### [Incorporators]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45094 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incorporators

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application for incorporation of a cooperative may be made by a
  minimum of three persons, or by one or more cooperative entities, who
  intend to be members of the cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Persons not to be incorporators

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An application under subsection (1) is not to be made
  by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an individual who is less than eighteen years of
    age;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an individual who is incapable; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an individual or body corporate that has the status
    of bankrupt.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 8
- 2001, c. 14, s. 139
- 2018, c. 8, s. 48
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-8-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 8"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Capital Structure]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45105 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Capital structure

**[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** A cooperative may be
incorporated with or without membership shares and with or without the
power to issue investment shares.

### [Application for Incorporation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45108 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

**[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** An application for
incorporation is made by sending the following to the Director:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} articles of incorporation;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a notice of registered office in accordance with
  section 30;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a notice of directors in accordance with section 81;
  and

- [(d) and (e)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2018, c. 8, s. 49\]]{.Repealed}

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} any other information that the Director may require
  to make a decision under section 12.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 10
- 2018, c. 8, s. 49
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 10"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Articles of Incorporation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45118 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Articles

- **[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Articles of incorporation must be in
  the form fixed by the Director and contain the following particulars:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the cooperative;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the place in Canada where its proposed registered
    office will be located;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2018, c. 8, s. 50\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the number of directors or the minimum and maximum
    number of directors;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any restriction on the businesses that the
    cooperative may carry on;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} any restriction on the class of membership in the
    cooperative;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the cooperative will be organized
    and operated and will carry on business on a cooperative basis;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the cooperative will carry on its
    undertaking in two or more provinces and will have a fixed place of
    business in more than one province;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} whether the cooperative is to be incorporated with
    or without membership share capital, and, if there is to be no
    membership share capital, a statement that the interest or right of
    each member as member is, subject to subsection 7(3), the same as
    that of every other member;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} if there is to be membership share capital, whether
    the number of membership shares to be issued is unlimited or
    limited, and if limited, the maximum number of membership shares
    that may be issued, and, if the membership shares are to have a par
    value, their par value and, if they are not to have par value,
    whether the membership shares are to be issued, purchased, redeemed
    or otherwise acquired at a fixed price or at a price determined in
    accordance with a formula, and if so, the particulars of the
    formula;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} any provision for a maximum rate of return that may
    be paid on member loans or membership shares;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} whether there is to be investment share capital
    and, if so, the particulars of it;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} any provisions for the distribution of the property
    of the cooperative on its dissolution and, in the case of a
    cooperative to which Part 20 or 21 applies, the provisions for that
    distribution of property in accordance with the applicable Part; and

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} any provision by which the members, other than by
    means of a unanimous agreement, restrict, in whole or in part, the
    powers of the directors to manage the business of the cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional provisions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The articles may set out any provisions that could be
  set out in the by-laws of the cooperative and if they do, any
  reference in this Act to the by-laws of the cooperative is also a
  reference to those provisions of the articles.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special majorities

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), the articles or a
  unanimous agreement may require a greater number of votes of
  directors, members or shareholders than is required by this Act to
  effect an action.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Voting number

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Neither the articles nor a unanimous agreement may
  require, in order to remove a director or delegate, that more than a
  majority of the votes cast by or on behalf of the persons who are
  entitled to vote be so cast.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2018, c. 8, s. 50\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 11
- 2011, c. 21, s. 73
- 2018, c. 8, s. 50
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11-20111129.html "Link to previous version of section 11"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Incorporation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45145 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance of certificate of incorporation

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Director must issue a certificate of incorporation for a cooperative
  if the Director is satisfied that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the articles are in accordance with section 11 and,
    if applicable, section 353 and subsection 359(2);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the cooperative will be organized and operated and
    will carry on business on a cooperative basis;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the cooperative will comply with Part 20 or 21, if
    applicable; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a notice that is required to be given under
    subsection 30(2) or 81(1) indicates that the cooperative, if it came
    into existence, would be in compliance with the Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reliance on articles

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraphs (1)(b) and (c), the
  Director may rely on the articles required by section 10.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 12
- 2001, c. 14, s. 140
- 2018, c. 8, s. 51
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-12-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 12"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date of existence and effect

**[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** On the day set out in its
certificate of incorporation, a cooperative comes into existence and the
incorporators become members of the cooperative.

### [By-laws]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45159 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adoption of by-laws

**[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** Without delay after the
issuance of the certificate of incorporation, a meeting of the members
must be held to make by-laws for the cooperative.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}By-laws --- mandatory provisions

- **[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  by-laws of a cooperative must provide for

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the qualifications of members and the procedures
    for acceptance of members;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the rights of joint members, if any;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the membership obligations of members, including
    any obligation to use the services of the cooperative and any fees
    to be paid by members;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the cooperative has auxiliary members, the
    rights and obligations of auxiliary members and the conditions for
    their acceptance by the cooperative as auxiliary members, including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the relationship an individual must have with the
      cooperative in order to be an auxiliary member, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the services of the cooperative that may be
      available to auxiliary members;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} whether the interest or right of a member in the
    cooperative may, subject to section 46, be transferred or assigned,
    and any conditions or restrictions that apply to such a transfer or
    assignment;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the selection, qualifications, term of office and
    removal of directors and members of committees of directors;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the distribution of any surplus earnings arising
    from the operations of the cooperative;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} if the cooperative is to act as an agent or
    mandatary for its members, a definition of that relationship;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} subject to sections 39 and 40 and Parts 20 and 21,
    the conditions on which membership is terminated, whether by
    withdrawal or by involuntary termination, if it may be terminated
    otherwise than as provided for in this Act, and the determination of
    the value and disposition of the member's interest or right in the
    cooperative on termination; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} if the cooperative wishes to permit members or
    shareholders to attend a meeting of the cooperative by means of a
    communication facility referred to in subsection 48(3), the ways in
    which votes must be held.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}By-laws --- other provisions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The by-laws of a cooperative may provide for

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the representation of members by delegates and, if
    so,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the designation of the classes or regional groups
      of members, if any, who may be represented by delegates,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the procedure for altering classes or regional
      groups of members, if applicable, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the powers, duties, selection, voting rights
      and procedures for the removal of delegates;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the division of members into classes or regional
    groups and, if so,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the qualifications for membership in each class
      or regional group,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the conditions precedent to membership in each
      class or regional group,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the method, time and manner of withdrawing from
      a class or regional group or transferring membership from one
      class or regional group to another and any applicable conditions
      on a transfer, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the conditions on which membership in a class or
      regional group ends;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the referral of disputes between a member and the
    cooperative to a process of dispute resolution; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any other matter that the members consider
    necessary or desirable.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 15
- 2001, c. 14, s. 141
- 2011, c. 21, s. 74
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-15-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 15"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Binding effect of by-laws and articles

**[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** The articles and by-laws
of a cooperative bind it and its members to the same extent as if they

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} had been signed by the cooperative and every member;
  and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} contained undertakings by every member and the
  successors, assigns and personal representatives of every member to
  observe all the provisions of the articles and by-laws.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 16
- 2001, c. 14, s. 142
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies

- **[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Each
  member and shareholder may on request, not more than once in each
  calendar year, receive free of charge one copy of the articles, the
  by-laws and any unanimous agreement, and one copy of any amendments to
  the articles, the by-laws and any unanimous agreement, of the
  cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The cooperative must provide each creditor, and,
  where the cooperative is a distributing cooperative, any other person,
  on payment of a reasonable fee, with a copy of the articles, the
  by-laws and any unanimous agreement of the cooperative.

### [Cooperative Basis]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45203 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cooperative basis

- **[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  cooperative must be organized and operated and must carry on business
  on a cooperative basis.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Complaint

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person may make an application to the court in
  accordance with section 329 if the person has a complaint that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a cooperative is not organized, operated or
    carrying on business on a cooperative basis; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a cooperative to which Part 20 or 21
    applies, the cooperative is not complying with the applicable Part.

### [Pre-incorporation Contracts]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45211 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pre-incorporation contracts

- **[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this section, a person
  who enters into, or purports to enter into, a written contract in the
  name of or on behalf of a cooperative before it comes into existence
  is personally bound by the contract and is entitled to its benefits,
  unless the contract expressly provides otherwise.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adoption by cooperative

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A cooperative may, within a reasonable time after it
  comes into existence, adopt a contract that was made in its name or on
  its behalf before it came into existence by any act or conduct that
  signifies the cooperative's intention to be bound by the contract.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of adoption

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a cooperative adopts a contract under this
  section,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cooperative is bound by the contract and
    entitled to its benefits as of the date of the contract; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person who originally entered into the contract
    ceases to be bound by the contract or to be entitled to its
    benefits, subject to an order made under subsection (4).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Court order

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Whether or not a cooperative has adopted a contract,
  the court may, on application by a party to the contract,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} make an order respecting the nature and extent of
    the obligations and liability under the contract of the cooperative
    and the person who entered into or purported to enter into the
    contract by or on behalf of the cooperative; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} make any other order that the court considers just
    in the circumstances.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 19
- 2001, c. 14, s. 143
:::

### [Name]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45227 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Required name

- **[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  cooperative must have the word "cooperative", "co-operative", "coop",
  "co-op", "coopérative", "united" or "pool", or another grammatical
  form of any of those words, as part of its name.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of abbreviation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A cooperative that has the word "cooperative",
  "co-operative", "coop", "co-op" or "coopérative", or another
  grammatical form of any of those words, as part of its name may use
  and be legally designated by any of those words or forms.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Business restriction

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the business of a cooperative is restricted by its
  articles or by a resolution of its members to a specific business
  purpose, the cooperative must have as part of its name one or more
  words that suggest the nature of the restriction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternate form

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The name of a cooperative may be set out in its
  articles in an English form, a French form, an English form and a
  French form, or a combined English and French form, so long as the
  combined form meets the prescribed criteria. The cooperative may use
  and be legally designated by any such form.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2001, c. 14, s. 144\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Identification outside Canada

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A cooperative may set out in its articles its name in
  any language form and, if it does, it may use and be legally
  designated outside Canada by its name in that form.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication of name

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} A cooperative must set out its name in legible
  characters in all contracts, invoices, negotiable instruments and
  orders for things or services made by or on behalf of the cooperative
  and in all documents filed with the Director under this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 20
- 2001, c. 14, s. 144
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other name

**[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** Subject to subsection
20(3) and section 23, a cooperative may carry on business or identify
itself by a name or style other than as set out in its articles.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reservation of name

**[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22 .sectionLabel}** The Director may, on
request, reserve for a prescribed period a name for an intended
cooperative or for a cooperative that intends to change its name.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 22
- [2018, c. 8, s. 52](#1037713-1373909){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037713-1373909.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-22-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 22"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibited names

**[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** A cooperative must not be
incorporated or continued as a cooperative under this Act with, change
its name to, or have, carry on business under or identify itself by a
name that is prohibited by the regulations or that does not meet the
prescribed requirements.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 23
- [2018, c. 8, s. 52](#1037713-1373914){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037713-1373914.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-23-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 23"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Direction to change name

- **[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Director may direct a cooperative to change its name in accordance
  with section 289 if, through inadvertence or otherwise, the
  cooperative acquires a name that is prohibited by the regulations or
  that does not meet the prescribed requirements.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation of name

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a cooperative has not followed a directive under
  subsection (1) or (3) within the prescribed period, the Director may
  revoke the name of the cooperative and assign a new name to it and,
  until changed in accordance with section 289, the name of the
  cooperative is the name assigned by the Director.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Undertaking to change name

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a cooperative acquires a name as a result of a
  person undertaking to dissolve or to change names, and the undertaking
  is not honoured, the Director may direct the cooperative to change its
  name in accordance with section 289, unless the undertaking is
  honoured within the period specified in subsection (2).

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2018, c. 8, s. 53\]]{.Repealed}

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2018, c. 8, s. 53\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 24
- [2018, c. 8, s. 53](#1037719-1373917){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037719-1373917.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-24-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 24"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of amendment

- **[[24.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the Director assigns a new name to a cooperative under subsection
  24(2), the Director must issue a certificate of amendment showing the
  new name of the cooperative and must publish a notice of the change of
  name as soon as practicable in a publication generally available to
  the public.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of certificate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The articles of the cooperative are amended
  accordingly on the date shown in the certificate of amendment.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 8, s. 54](#1037723-1373924){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037723-1373924.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition on use of name

**[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** Every entity, other than
a cooperative incorporated under this Act or a body corporate
incorporated by or under the authority of another Act of Parliament or
an Act of the legislature of a province, is guilty of an offence if the
entity uses or authorizes the use of the word "cooperative",
"co-operative", "co-op", "coop", "coopérative" or "pool", or another
grammatical form of any of those words, as part of its name or in any
manner in connection with the conduct of its business so that the entity
could reasonably be considered to be holding itself out as carrying on
business as a cooperative entity.

## [PART 3]{.HLabel1}[Capacity and Powers]{.HTitleText1} {#h-45265 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Legal capacity

- **[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Act, a cooperative

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has the capacity and the rights, powers and
    privileges of a natural person; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} may carry on business throughout Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}By-laws not required to confer powers

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} It is not necessary for a cooperative to pass a
  by-law in order to confer any particular power on a cooperative or its
  directors.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers outside the jurisdiction

- **[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Act, a cooperative
  has the capacity to carry on its business, conduct its affairs and
  exercise its powers in any jurisdiction outside Canada to the extent
  and in the manner that the laws of that jurisdiction permit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Business restriction

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No cooperative and no subsidiary of a cooperative may
  carry on any business contrary to a restriction set out in the
  articles of the cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Validity of acts

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No act of a cooperative, including a transfer of
  property, is invalid by reason only that the act is contrary to its
  articles or this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Constructive notice

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A member of a cooperative is deemed to have knowledge
  of the content of the articles and by-laws of the cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No constructive notice

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), no person is deemed to
  have knowledge of the content of a document by reason only that it is
  filed with the Director or is available for inspection at the
  cooperative.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assertions

- **[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  cooperative and no guarantor of an obligation of a cooperative may
  assert against a person dealing with the cooperative or against a
  person who acquired rights from the cooperative that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cooperative's articles, by-laws or any
    unanimous agreement have not been complied with;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the persons named in the most recent notice of
    directors filed with the Director are not the directors;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the place named in the most recent notice of
    registered office filed with the Director is not the registered
    office;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a person held out as a director, an officer, an
    agent or a mandatary of the cooperative has not been duly appointed
    or has no authority to exercise the powers and perform the duties
    that are customary in the business of the cooperative or usual for a
    director, officer, agent or mandatary;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a document issued by a director, officer, agent or
    mandatary of the cooperative with actual or usual authority to issue
    it is not valid or genuine; or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a sale, lease or exchange of all or substantially
    all of the property of the cooperative was not authorized.

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2001, c. 14, s. 145\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of a person
  who has, or ought to have, knowledge of a situation described in that
  subsection by virtue of their relationship to the cooperative.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 28
- 2001, c. 14, s. 145
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No personal liability

**[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** The members and
shareholders of a cooperative, by reason only of being members or
shareholders, are not liable for any liability, act or default of the
cooperative, except as provided in this Act.

## [PART 4]{.HLabel1}[Registered Office and Records]{.HTitleText1} {#h-45300 .Part}

### [Registered Office]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45301 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Place of registered office

- **[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  cooperative must maintain a registered office in the place set out in
  its articles.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of registered office

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A notice of registered office in the form that the
  Director fixes must be sent to the Director together with any articles
  that designate or change the place of the registered office of the
  cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Change of address

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The directors may change the address of the
  registered office within the place specified in the articles.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of change of address

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A cooperative must send to the Director, within
  fifteen days after any change of address of its registered office, a
  notice in the form that the Director fixes.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 30
- 2018, c. 8, s. 55(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-30-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 30"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Records]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45313 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record keeping

- **[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  cooperative must prepare and maintain the following records at its
  registered office or at any other place in Canada designated by the
  directors:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cooperative's articles and by-laws, including
    any amendments and a copy of any unanimous agreement;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the minutes of meetings of the members and
    shareholders;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} copies of all notices of directors and notices of
    change of directors;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a list of its members, setting out their names and
    addresses, the number of any membership shares owned and the amount
    of any member loans;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a list of its shareholders, setting out their names
    and addresses and the number of investment shares owned;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a register of its directors, setting out the names
    and addresses of the individuals who are or who have been directors
    and the dates on which they became or ceased to be directors; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} if the cooperative issues securities in registered
    form, a securities register that complies with section 186.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other records

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In addition to the records specified in subsection
  (1), a cooperative must prepare and maintain adequate

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} accounting records;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} records containing minutes of meetings and
    resolutions of directors and any committee of directors; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} records sufficient for the purpose of calculating
    patronage returns, that show for each member particulars of the
    transactions between the cooperative and the member.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When records or registers kept outside
  Canada

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), but subject to the [Income
  Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15), the
  [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) and any other Act administered by the
  Minister of National Revenue, a cooperative may keep all or any of its
  records mentioned in paragraphs (1)(a), (b), (c), (f) and (g) and
  (2)(a) and (b) at a place outside Canada, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the records are available for inspection, by means
    of a computer terminal or other technology, during regular office
    hours at the registered office or another office in Canada
    designated by the directors; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the cooperative provides the technical assistance
    to facilitate an inspection referred to in paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention of accounting records

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to any other Act of Parliament and to any Act
  of the legislature of a province that provides for a longer retention
  period, a cooperative must retain the accounting records referred to
  in paragraph (2)(a) for a period of six years after the end of the
  financial year to which they relate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Records of continued cooperatives

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (1)(b) and
  subsections (2) to (4), if a body corporate is continued under this
  Act, "records" includes similar records required by law to be
  maintained by the body corporate before it was so continued.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 31
- 2001, c. 14, s. 146
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form of records

- **[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  register or record required by this Act must be prepared and
  maintained in a form that is capable of reproducing any required
  information in intelligible written form within a reasonable time,
  including

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a bound or loose-leaf form;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a photographic form;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a system of mechanical or electronic data
    processing; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any other information storage device.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Precautions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A cooperative and its agents and mandataries must
  take reasonable precautions respecting the registers and records
  required by this Act to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prevent their loss or destruction;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prevent the falsification of entries in them; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} facilitate the detection and correction of
    inaccuracies in them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Records open to directors' inspection

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The records described in section 31, other than those
  described in paragraph 31(2)(c), must be open for inspection by the
  directors at any reasonable time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inspection and copying of records by
  members, creditors and shareholders

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Members, creditors and shareholders of the
  cooperative, their personal representatives and the Director may
  examine the records referred to in paragraphs 31(1)(a), (b), (c) (f)
  and (g) during the usual business hours of the cooperative and may
  take extracts from the records, free of charge, or have copies of them
  made after payment of a reasonable fee.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 32
- 2001, c. 14, s. 147
:::

### [Lists]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45357 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Lists

- **[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Members, shareholders and creditors
  of a cooperative and their personal representatives and, where the
  cooperative is a distributing cooperative, any other person, may
  request that the cooperative provide them with a list of members or
  shareholders, no later than ten days after the cooperative receives
  the affidavit referred to in subsection (2) and after payment of a
  reasonable fee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Affidavit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A request under subsection (1) must be accompanied by
  an affidavit containing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the applicant; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an undertaking that the list of members or
    shareholders will not be used except as permitted by subsection (5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request by Director

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Director may request that the cooperative provide
  him or her with a list of members or shareholders, no later than ten
  days after the cooperative receives the request and after payment of a
  reasonable fee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of list

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The list of members or shareholders provided under
  subsection (1) or (3) must set out in alphabetical order the names and
  addresses of the members or shareholders of the cooperative as of a
  date not more than ten days before the receipt of the affidavit
  referred to in subsection (2) or the request referred to in subsection
  (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permitted uses of list

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A list obtained under subsection (1) must not be used
  by any person except in connection with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an effort to influence voting at a meeting of the
    cooperative; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any other matter relating to the affairs of the
    cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-inclusion of name on request

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A member or shareholder may advise the cooperative in
  writing that their name is not to be included in a list prepared by
  the cooperative further to a request under subsection (1), in which
  case the cooperative must not include that name in the list but must
  mention on the list that the list is incomplete.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 33
- 2001, c. 14, s. 148
:::

### [Corporate Seal]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45377 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Corporate seal

- **[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  cooperative may but need not adopt a corporate seal and may change a
  corporate seal that is adopted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Validity of unsealed documents

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A document executed or, in Quebec, signed on behalf
  of a cooperative is not invalid merely because a corporate seal is not
  affixed to it.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 34
- 2011, c. 21, s. 75(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-34-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 34"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 5]{.HLabel1}[Membership]{.HTitleText1} {#h-45385 .Part}

### [Conditions for Membership]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45386 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}By-laws govern

**[[35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35 .sectionLabel}** Subject to this Act and
any provision in the articles, membership in a cooperative is governed
by its by-laws.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for membership

- **[[36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person may be admitted to membership in a cooperative until

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person has applied for membership in writing;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the application has been approved by the directors;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person has complied with the membership
    provisions required by the by-laws, including subscribing for any
    minimum number of membership shares, paying any minimum amount on
    account of the subscription price of the shares or paying any
    minimum amount on account of a member loan.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effective date of membership

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If all the conditions set out in subsection (1) have
  been met within six months after the date on which the cooperative
  receives the application for membership, the directors may make the
  admission of the member into membership effective as of the date of
  the application or as of any date after that date but before the end
  of the six months.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to vote

- **[[37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2) and
  subsection 7(3), a member has one vote on all matters to be decided by
  the members.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegates

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the by-laws provide that the voting rights of a
  member are vested in one or more delegates to be elected or appointed
  by the members, the delegates so elected or appointed may exercise all
  or any of those rights.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When delegation permitted by by-laws

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} When in this Act reference is made to a meeting of
  members and the cooperative has a by-law providing for the appointment
  of delegates, a reference in this Act to a meeting of members is to be
  construed as a reference to a meeting of delegates.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Members under eighteen years of age

- **[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the by-laws, a person
  less than eighteen years of age may be admitted to membership in a
  cooperative and may vote at meetings of the cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minors

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The articles and by-laws of a cooperative, and any
  unanimous agreement, are binding on a member who is less than eighteen
  years of age.

### [Withdrawal from Membership]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45409 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Withdrawal of membership

- **[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless
  the by-laws provide otherwise, this section applies to the voluntary
  withdrawal of a member from membership in a cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Written notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A member may withdraw from membership in a
  cooperative by written notice to the cooperative. Any such withdrawal
  is effective on the later of the date stated in the notice and the
  date on which the cooperative receives the notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Redemption of membership shares and
  repayment of amounts owing on withdrawal

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 149, the cooperative must, no
  later than one year after the effective date of a notice of
  withdrawal, redeem all membership shares held by the withdrawing
  member at the redemption price determined in accordance with section
  146 and repay to the member all member loans, all other amounts held
  to the member's credit and all amounts outstanding on loans made to
  the cooperative by the member, together with any interest accrued on
  those amounts up to the date of the payment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Redemption and repayment more than one
  year after withdrawal

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (3), if the directors determine
  that the redemption of membership shares, or the repayment of
  membership loans, of a withdrawing member would adversely affect the
  financial well-being of the cooperative, the directors may direct that
  the redemption and repayment referred to in subsection (3) take place
  after the end of the one year period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Withdrawal not to have certain effects

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Unless the directors determine otherwise,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the withdrawal of a member from the cooperative
    does not release the member from any debt or obligation to the
    cooperative or contract with the cooperative; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the cooperative need not, despite subsection (3),
    repay to the member amounts outstanding on loans made to the
    cooperative that have a fixed maturity date until that date has
    arrived.

### [Termination of Membership]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45423 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Termination of membership by directors

- **[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This
  section sets out the rights and procedures that apply to termination
  of membership. The by-laws of a cooperative may derogate from this
  section, but only in respect of the manner in which the membership of
  members may be terminated.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special resolution of directors

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The directors may by special resolution order the
  termination of the membership of a member but, if the cooperative is
  in breach of section 149 --- or if making the payment referred to in
  subsection (7) would put the cooperative in breach of section 149 ---
  the termination is not effective until the cooperative is no longer in
  breach.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Written notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Not more than ten days after the date on which a
  special resolution is made, the secretary of the cooperative must give
  written notice to the member of the termination and the reasons for
  it. Subject to subsections (4) and (5), the effective date of the
  termination is the later of the date specified in the written notice
  and thirty days after the member receives the notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A member whose membership has been terminated may
  appeal from the decision of the directors to the next meeting of
  members by giving written notice to the secretary of the member's
  intention to appeal no later than thirty days after receiving notice
  of the special resolution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of notice of appeal

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a member gives a notice of appeal, the effect of
  the special resolution is suspended until the vote of the members
  under subsection (6).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Resolution of members

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a member appeals the termination of membership, a
  vote of members must be taken at the next meeting of members as to
  whether the member's membership should be terminated as of the
  effective date referred to in subsection (3). The vote is by majority
  of the members present at the meeting, unless a greater proportion is
  specified in the articles, the by-laws or a unanimous agreement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of termination of membership

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 149, if a member's membership is
  terminated, a cooperative must, no later than one year after the date
  of the special resolution, redeem all membership shares held by the
  member at the redemption price determined in accordance with section
  146, and repay to the member all member loans and all other amounts
  held to the member's credit and all amounts outstanding on loans made
  to the cooperative by the member, together with any interest accrued
  on those amounts up to the date of the payment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Termination of membership not to have
  certain effects

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} Unless the directors determine otherwise, the
  termination of the membership of a member does not release the member
  from any debt or obligation to the cooperative or contract with the
  cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If address of member unknown

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} If the address of a member whose membership has been
  terminated by the directors is unknown to the cooperative after all
  reasonable efforts have been made to ascertain it and two years have
  elapsed since the effective date referred to in subsection (3), the
  cooperative must transfer all amounts owing under subsection (7) to a
  reserve fund, but those amounts do not, despite subsection (7),
  include any interest that would have accrued after the end of the two
  years.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amounts paid to entitled persons

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} If any amounts are transferred to a reserve fund
  under subsection (9), the cooperative must pay those amounts to any
  person who, no later than ten years after the transfer, shows evidence
  of entitlement satisfactory to the cooperative. If no person appears
  to show that evidence within the ten years, the amounts become the
  property of the cooperative.

### [Limitation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45445 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation on by-law

**[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41 .sectionLabel}** No by-law governing the
withdrawal of a member from membership or the termination of the
membership of a member may authorize a redemption of membership shares
or member loans in contravention of section 149.

### [Termination of Membership by Members]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45448 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Termination by members

**[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42 .sectionLabel}** Unless the by-laws
provide otherwise, the membership of a member may be terminated by a
special resolution of the members. Section 40 applies, with any
modifications that the circumstances require, to a termination by the
members.

### [Other Terminations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45451 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Termination of inactive membership

- **[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  cooperative may, by written notice to a member, terminate the
  membership if the member

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is a body corporate and winding-up proceedings have
    commenced with respect to it; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} failed, during a period of two consecutive years,
    to transact any business with the cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provisions not to apply

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Section 40, other than subsections 40(7) to (10),
  does not apply to a termination under this section.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-profit housing cooperatives

**[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44 .sectionLabel}** If the membership of a
person in a non-profit housing cooperative is terminated, any right of
the person to possession or occupancy of residential premises acquired
by virtue of membership in the cooperative is subject to Part 20.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Re-admission

**[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45 .sectionLabel}** A person whose membership
has been terminated under section 40 or 42 may be re-admitted to
membership only by special resolution of the members.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transfer

**[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46 .sectionLabel}** No transfer of a
membership, a member loan or a membership share in a cooperative is
valid for any purpose unless it is approved by the directors and the
transferee has otherwise complied with the articles and by-laws of the
cooperative and, if applicable, become a party to a unanimous agreement.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If membership too low

**[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47 .sectionLabel}** If the membership of a
cooperative is reduced to a number less than the number of members
required for incorporation, and if after thirty days notice remains at
less than that number, the Director may require the cooperative

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} to apply for a certificate of continuance under the
  [Canada Business Corporations Act](/eng/acts/C-44), if it was
  incorporated with membership capital; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} to be liquidated or dissolved under Part 17.

## [PART 6]{.HLabel1}[Corporate Governance]{.HTitleText1} {#h-45469 .Part}

### [Meetings]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45470 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Place of members' meetings

- **[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Meetings of the members of a
  cooperative are to be held at the place in Canada provided for in the
  by-laws or, in the absence of such a provision, at any place in Canada
  that the directors may determine.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Place of shareholders' meetings

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Meetings of the shareholders must be held at the
  place set out in the articles. If the articles do not set out such a
  place, the meetings are to be held at the place in Canada that the
  directors determine, unless all the shareholders entitled to vote at
  the meeting agree that the meeting is to be held at another place that
  is not in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Participation in meeting by electronic
  means

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Unless the by-laws provide otherwise, a member or
  shareholder may participate in a meeting of the cooperative, in
  accordance with the regulations, if any, by means of a telephonic,
  electronic or other communication facility that permits all
  participants to communicate adequately with each other during the
  meeting, if the cooperative makes available such a communication
  facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Meeting held by electronic means

  [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} If the directors of a cooperative, or any other
  person, call a meeting of the cooperative pursuant to this Act, those
  directors or that person, as the case may be, may determine that the
  meeting shall be held, in accordance with the regulations, if any,
  entirely by means of a telephonic, electronic or other communication
  facility that permits all participants to communicate adequately with
  each other during the meeting, if the by-laws so provide.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presence

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A person participating in a meeting referred to in
  subsection (3) is deemed to be present at the meeting.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 48
- 2001, c. 14, s. 149
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calling members' meetings

- **[[49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} After
  holding the organizational meeting referred to in section 82, the
  directors must call a meeting of the members without delay.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Business at first meeting

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The members, at their first meeting, must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} adopt by-laws for the cooperative;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} elect directors in accordance with subsection
    81(3); and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection 254(1), appoint an auditor to
    hold office until the close of the first annual meeting of members.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calling meetings of the members

- **[[50]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-50 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  directors must call the first annual meeting of members not later than
