  eighteen months after the cooperative comes into existence and an
  annual meeting of members must be held not later than the earlier of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} fifteen months after holding the last annual
    meeting, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} six months after the end of the preceding financial
    year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special meetings

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The directors may at any time call a special meeting
  of the members or of the shareholders.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order to delay calling of annual
  meeting

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), the cooperative may apply to
  the court for an order extending the time for calling an annual
  meeting.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 50
- 2001, c. 14, s. 150
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record date

- **[[51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  directors may, within the prescribed period, fix in advance a date as
  the record date for the determination of the members or shareholders
  who are entitled to receive payment of a dividend or for any other
  purpose except the right to receive notice of, or to vote at, a
  meeting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record date --- members' meetings

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of determining the persons who are
  entitled to receive notice of, or to vote at, a meeting of members,
  the record date is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the day before the day on which the notice is
    given; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if no notice is given, the day of the meeting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record date --- notice of shareholders'
  meetings

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of determining the shareholders who
  are entitled to receive notice of a meeting of the shareholders, the
  directors may, within the prescribed period, fix in advance a date as
  the record date for that determination.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record date --- voting at shareholders'
  meetings

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of determining the shareholders who
  are entitled to vote at a meeting of shareholders, the directors may,
  within the prescribed period, fix in advance a date as the record date
  for that determination.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If no record date fixed

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If no record date is fixed under subsection (1) or
  (3), the record date

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the determination of members or shareholders
    for any purpose, other than to establish the right of a member or
    shareholder to receive notice of a meeting or to vote, is the day on
    which the directors pass the resolution relating to the particular
    purpose; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the determination of shareholders who are
    entitled to receive notice of a meeting is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the day before the day on which the notice is
      given, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if no notice is given, the day of the meeting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If record date fixed

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a record date with respect to shareholders is
  fixed under this section, unless notice of the date is waived by each
  shareholder whose name is set out in the securities register at the
  close of business on the day the directors fix the record date, notice
  of the record date must be given within the prescribed period

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} by advertisement in a newspaper published or
    distributed in a place where the cooperative has its registered
    office and in each place in Canada where it has a transfer agent or
    where a transfer of its investment shares may be recorded; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} by written notice to each stock exchange in Canada
    on which the investment shares of the cooperative are listed for
    trading.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 51
- 2001, c. 14, s. 151
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of meetings

- **[[52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Notice
  of the time and place of a meeting of a cooperative must be sent
  within the prescribed period

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to each person who is entitled to vote at the
    meeting;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to each director; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to the auditor of the cooperative, if any.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a cooperative that is not a
  distributing cooperative, the notice may be sent within a shorter
  period if so specified in the articles or the by-laws.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notice of the time and place of a meeting of the
  holders of investment shares of any class that is publicly traded on a
  recognized stock exchange in Canada may be published once a week for
  at least four consecutive weeks before the date of the meeting in a
  newspaper in general circulation in the place where the registered
  office of the cooperative is situated and in each place in Canada
  where the cooperative has a transfer agent or where a transfer of the
  investment shares may be recorded.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}By-laws may specify manner of giving
  notice to members

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The by-laws of a cooperative may derogate from this
  section, but only in respect of the manner in which notice of a
  meeting of members may be given to members.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 52
- 2001, c. 14, s. 152
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice not required

**[[53]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53 .sectionLabel}** A notice of a meeting of
a cooperative need not be sent to a shareholder who was not registered
on the records of the cooperative or its transfer agent on the record
date fixed or determined under section 51.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Failure to receive notice

**[[54]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-54 .sectionLabel}** Failure to receive notice
of a meeting does not deprive a person of a right to vote at the meeting
to which the person is otherwise entitled.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice when adjournment

- **[[55]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-55 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  meeting of a cooperative is adjourned for less than thirty days, it is
  not necessary, unless the by-laws provide otherwise, to give notice of
  the adjourned meeting, other than by announcement at the earliest
  meeting that is adjourned.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of adjournment --- meetings of
  members

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a meeting of members is adjourned by one or more
  adjournments for a total of thirty days or more, notice of the
  adjourned meeting must be given as for the original meeting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of adjournment --- meetings of
  shareholders

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a meeting of shareholders is adjourned by one or
  more adjournments for a total of thirty days or more, notice of the
  adjourned meeting must be given as for the original meeting but,
  unless the meeting is adjourned by one or more adjournments for a
  total of more than ninety days, subsection 165(1) does not apply.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special business

- **[[56]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-56 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} All
  matters dealt with at a special meeting of a cooperative and all
  matters dealt with at an annual meeting, except consideration of the
  financial statements, the auditor's report, the business of the
  cooperative, the election of directors, the remuneration of directors
  and the re-appointment of the incumbent auditor, are special business.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice if special business is to be
  transacted

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notice of a meeting of a cooperative at which special
  business is to be transacted must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} state the nature of the special business in
    sufficient detail to permit the recipient to form a reasoned
    judgement with respect to the special business; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} contain the text of any special resolution to be
    submitted to the meeting.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Waiver of notice

- **[[57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who is entitled to attend a meeting of a cooperative may waive
  notice of the meeting in any manner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Attendance is waiver

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Attendance at a meeting of a cooperative is a waiver
  of notice of the meeting, except when a person attends the meeting for
  the express purpose of objecting to the transaction of any business on
  the ground that the meeting is not lawfully called.

### [Proposals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45563 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proposals

- **[[58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  member may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} submit to the cooperative notice of any matter that
    the member proposes to raise at an annual meeting; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} discuss at the meeting any matter in respect of
    which the member would have been entitled to submit a proposal.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proposals by members or directors to
  amend articles

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any member or director may, in accordance with
  section 290, make a proposal to amend the articles.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proposals by other persons to amend
  articles

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} Any other person may, in accordance with section
  290, make a proposal to amend the articles if the person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has been, for at least the prescribed period, the
    registered holder or the beneficial owner of at least the prescribed
    number of outstanding investment shares of the cooperative; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has the support of persons who, in the aggregate,
    and including or not including the person that submits the proposal,
    have been, for at least the prescribed period, the registered
    holders, or the beneficial owners of, at least the prescribed number
    of outstanding investment shares of the cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information to be provided

  [(2.2)]{.lawlabel} A proposal submitted by a person described in
  paragraph (2.1)(a) must be accompanied by the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person and of the
    person's supporters, if applicable; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of investment shares held or owned by
    the person and by the person's supporters, if applicable, and the
    date the investment shares were acquired.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information not part of proposal

  [(2.3)]{.lawlabel} The information provided under subsection (2.2)
  does not form part of the proposal or of the supporting statement
  referred to in subsection (3) and is not included for the purposes of
  the prescribed maximum word limit set out in subsection (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof may be required

  [(2.4)]{.lawlabel} If requested by the cooperative within the
  prescribed period, a person who submits a proposal must provide proof,
  within the prescribed period, that the person meets the requirements
  of subsection (2.1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proposal and statement to accompany
  notice of meeting

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A proposal submitted for consideration at a meeting
  must be attached to the notice of the meeting, together with, if
  requested by the person making the proposal, a statement in support of
  the proposal and the name and address of person making the proposal.
  The statement and the proposal must together not exceed the prescribed
  maximum number of words.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A cooperative need not comply with subsection (3) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the proposal is not submitted to the cooperative
    within the prescribed period;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it clearly appears that the primary purpose of the
    proposal is to enforce a personal claim or redress a personal
    grievance against the cooperative or its directors, officers,
    members or security holders;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} not more than the prescribed period before the
    receipt of a proposal, a person failed to present, at a meeting, a
    proposal that, at the person's request, had been attached by the
    cooperative to the notice of the meeting;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} substantially the same proposal was attached to a
    notice of meeting relating to a meeting of the cooperative held not
    more than the prescribed period before the receipt of the proposal
    and the proposal did not receive the prescribed minimum amount of
    support at the meeting; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the rights conferred by subsections (1) and (2) are
    being abused to secure publicity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cooperative may refuse to include
  proposal

  [(4.1)]{.lawlabel} If

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person described in subsection (2.1) makes a
    proposal and fails to continue to hold or own the number of
    investment shares referred to in that subsection up to and including
    the day of the meeting, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a member makes a proposal and, prior to the
    meeting, withdraws from membership in accordance with section 39,

  the cooperative is not required to include in the notice of a meeting,
  or attach to it, any proposal submitted by that person for any meeting
  held within the prescribed period following the date of the meeting.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 58
- 2001, c. 14, s. 153
- [2018, c. 8, s. 56](#1037725-1373752){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037725-1373752.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-58-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 58"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability for circulation

**[[59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59 .sectionLabel}** No cooperative or person
acting on behalf of a cooperative incurs any liability by reason only of
circulating a proposal or statement in accordance with section 58.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal to include proposal

- **[[60]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  cooperative refuses to include a proposal in a notice of a meeting
  referred to in section 52, the cooperative must, within the prescribed
  period after the day on which it receives the proposal or the day on
  which it receives the proof of ownership under subsection 58(2.4), as
  the case may be, notify in writing the person submitting the proposal
  of its intention to omit the proposal from the notice and of the
  reasons for the refusal.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restraining order by court

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On the application of a person submitting a proposal
  who claims to be aggrieved by a cooperative's refusal under subsection
  (1), a court may restrain the holding of the meeting at which the
  proposal is sought to be presented and make any further order it
  thinks fit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order to omit proposal from notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A cooperative or any person claiming to be aggrieved
  by a proposal may apply to a court for an order permitting the
  cooperative to omit the proposal from a notice of meeting, and the
  court, if it is satisfied that subsection 58(4) applies, may make any
  order that it thinks fit.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 60
- 2001, c. 14, s. 154
:::

### [Lists]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45610 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}List of persons entitled to receive
notice

- **[[61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  cooperative must prepare an alphabetical list of its members as of the
  record date established under subsection 51(2) or, if the by-laws
  provide for delegates, of the delegates, who are entitled to receive
  notice of and vote at a meeting of members.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entitlement to vote --- members'
  meetings

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection 7(3), a member or delegate
  whose name appears on the list referred to in subsection (1) is
  entitled to one vote at a meeting of members.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}List of shareholders entitled to vote

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a record date for voting is fixed under subsection
  51(4), a cooperative must prepare, no later than ten days after the
  record date, an alphabetical list of shareholders who are entitled to
  vote as of the record date at a meeting of shareholders that shows the
  number of investment shares held by each shareholder.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entitlement to vote

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A shareholder named in the list referred to in
  subsection (3) is entitled to vote the investment shares opposite
  their name at the meeting to which the list relates.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}List of shareholders entitled to
  receive notice

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a record date for voting is not fixed under
  subsection 51(4), a cooperative must prepare, not later than 10 days
  after the record date for notice of a meeting that is fixed under
  subsection 51(3) or not later than the record date that is referred to
  in subsection 51(5), as the case may be, an alphabetical list of
  shareholders who are entitled to receive notice of a meeting of
  shareholders as of the record date that shows the number of shares
  held by each shareholder.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entitlement to vote

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A shareholder whose name appears on the list referred
  to in subsection (5) is entitled to vote the investment shares shown
  opposite their name at the meeting to which the list relates, except
  to the extent that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the shareholder has transferred the ownership of
    any of those investment shares after the record date, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the transferee of those investment shares demands,
    not later than ten days before the meeting, or any shorter period
    that the by-laws of the cooperative provide, that the transferee's
    name be included in the list before the meeting and produces
    properly endorsed investment share certificates or otherwise
    establishes that the transferee owns the investment shares,

  in which case the transferee may vote the shares at the meeting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Examination of list

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} A person who is entitled to vote at a meeting of a
  cooperative may examine a list that relates to the meeting

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} during usual business hours at the registered
    office of the cooperative or at the place where its records of
    members and shareholders are maintained; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at the meeting for which the list was prepared.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 61
- 2018, c. 8, s. 57(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-61-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 61"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Procedure]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45633 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quorum

- **[[62]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless
  the by-laws provide otherwise, a quorum is present at a meeting of the
  cooperative if persons holding a majority of the voting rights that
  may be exercised at the meeting are present in person or represented
  in a manner provided for by this Act or permitted by the by-laws.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Opening quorum sufficient

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a quorum is present at the opening of a meeting,
  the persons who are present and entitled to vote may, unless the
  by-laws provide otherwise, proceed with the business of the meeting
  even though a quorum is not present throughout the meeting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adjournment

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a quorum is not present at the opening of a
  meeting, the persons who are present and entitled to vote may adjourn
  the meeting to a fixed time and place but may not transact any other
  business.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representative

- **[[63]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-63 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If an
  entity is entitled to vote at a meeting of a cooperative, the
  cooperative must recognize any individual authorized by a resolution
  of the directors or governing body or similar authority of the entity
  to represent it at meetings of the cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of representative

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An individual who is authorized under subsection (1)
  to represent an entity may exercise on behalf of the entity all the
  powers the entity could exercise if it were an individual.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Joint voting --- members

- **[[64]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless
  the by-laws provide otherwise, each joint member may vote at a meeting
  of members.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Joint voting --- shareholders

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Unless the articles provide otherwise, if two or more
  persons hold investment shares jointly, one of those holders present
  at a meeting of shareholders may, in the absence of the others, vote
  the investment shares, but if two or more of those persons who are
  present vote, in person or by proxy, they vote as one on the
  investment shares jointly held by them.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Voting by show of hands

- **[[65]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless
  the by-laws provide otherwise, voting at a meeting of a cooperative
  takes place by a show of hands except when a ballot is demanded by a
  person who is entitled to vote at the meeting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ballot

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person who is entitled to vote at a meeting may
  demand a ballot either before or after a vote by show of hands.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Electronic voting

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), unless the by-laws provide
  otherwise, any vote referred to in subsection (1) may be held, in
  accordance with the regulations, if any, entirely by means of a
  telephonic, electronic or other communication facility, if the
  cooperative makes available such a communication facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Voting while participating
  electronically

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Unless the by-laws otherwise provide, a member or
  shareholder participating in a meeting of the cooperative under
  subsection 48(3) or (3.1) and entitled to vote at that meeting may
  vote, in accordance with the regulations, if any, by means of the
  telephonic, electronic or other communication facility that the
  cooperative has made available for that purpose.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 65
- 2001, c. 14, s. 155
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Resolution in lieu of meeting

- **[[66]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless
  the by-laws provide otherwise, and except when a written statement is
  submitted under section 89 or subsection 260(4), a resolution in
  writing signed by all the persons who are entitled to vote on that
  resolution at a meeting of the cooperative is as valid as if it had
  been passed at such a meeting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Resolution

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Unless the by-laws provide otherwise, and except when
  a written statement is submitted under section 89 or subsection
  260(4), a resolution in writing dealing with all matters required by
  this Act to be dealt with at a meeting of a cooperative, and signed by
  all the persons who are entitled to vote at the meeting, satisfies all
  the requirements of this Act relating to meetings.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies of resolutions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A copy of every resolution referred to in
  subsection (1) must be kept with the minutes of the meeting.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Evidence

**[[67]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-67 .sectionLabel}** Unless a ballot is
demanded, an entry in the minutes of a meeting to the effect that the
chairperson of the meeting declared a resolution to be carried or
defeated is, in the absence of evidence to the contrary, proof of the
fact without proof of the number or proportion of the votes recorded in
favour of or against the resolution.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 67
- 2001, c. 14, s. 156
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}One shareholder meeting

**[[68]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-68 .sectionLabel}** If a cooperative has only
one shareholder, or only one holder of any class or series of investment
shares, the shareholder present in person or represented by proxy
constitutes a meeting of the shareholders or a meeting of shareholders
of that class or series.

### [Requisitions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45675 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requisition of meeting

- **[[69]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-69 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Two or
  more persons who together hold not less than five per cent of the
  voting rights that could be exercised at a meeting of a cooperative
  may requisition the directors to call such a meeting for the purposes
  stated in the requisition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The requisition

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} must state the business to be transacted at the
    meeting and be sent to each director and to the registered office of
    the cooperative; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} may consist of several documents of like form, each
    signed by one or more persons who are entitled to vote at the
    meeting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Directors calling meeting

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of the requisition, the directors must
  call a meeting to transact the business stated in the requisition
  unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the directors have called a meeting and given
    notice of it under section 52;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the business of the meeting as stated in the
    requisition includes matters described in any of paragraphs 58(4)(b)
    to (e); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the business of the meeting as stated in the
    requisition includes a matter

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a requisition by a member, outside
      the powers of the members, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a requisition by a shareholder,
      outside the powers of the shareholders.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Member or shareholder calling meeting

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the directors do not call a meeting within
  twenty-one days after receiving the requisition, any person who signed
  the requisition may call the meeting unless any of paragraphs (3)(a)
  to (c) applies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Procedure

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A meeting called under this section must be called as
  nearly as possible in the manner in which meetings are to be called
  pursuant to the by-laws, a unanimous agreement and this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reimbursement

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Unless the persons who are present and entitled to
  vote at a meeting called under subsection (4) resolve otherwise, the
  cooperative must reimburse the persons who signed the requisition for
  the expenses reasonably incurred by them in requisitioning, calling
  and holding the meeting.

### [Other Methods of Calling Meetings]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45696 .Subheading}

**[[70]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-70 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2001, c. 14,
s. 157\]]{.Repealed}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Meeting called by court

- **[[71]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-71 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  court, on the application of a director or a person who is entitled to
  vote at a meeting, may order a meeting of a cooperative to be called,
  held and conducted within the time and in the manner that the court
  directs, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is not feasible to call the meeting within the
    time or in the manner in which those meetings are to be called;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is not feasible to conduct the meeting in the
    manner required by this Act or the by-laws; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the court thinks the meeting should be called, held
    and conducted in the manner it directs for any other reason.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Varying quorum

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Without restricting the generality of subsection (1),
  a court may order that the quorum required by the by-laws or this Act
  be varied or dispensed with at a meeting called, held and conducted
  under this section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Valid meeting

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A meeting called, held and conducted under this
  section is for all purposes a meeting duly called, held and conducted.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 71
- 2001, c. 14, s. 158
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Court review of election

- **[[72]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  cooperative, a director or any person who is entitled to vote in the
  election or appointment of a director or an auditor may apply to a
  court to resolve any dispute in respect of the election or appointment
  of a director or an auditor of the cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of court

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On an application under subsection (1), a court may
  make any order it thinks fit, including an order

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} restraining a director or auditor whose election or
    appointment is challenged from acting pending determination of the
    dispute;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} declaring the result of a disputed election or
    appointment;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} requiring a new election or appointment, and
    including in the order directions for the management of the business
    and affairs of the cooperative until a new election is held or a new
    appointment is made; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} determining the voting rights of persons claiming
    to be entitled to vote.

### [By-laws]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45719 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Making or amendment of by-law by members

- **[[73]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  members may, by special resolution, make, amend or repeal any by-law
  that regulates the business and affairs of the cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Making or amendment of by-law by
  directors

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Unless the by-laws of a cooperative provide
  otherwise, the directors may, by special resolution, make a by-law or
  amend a by-law of the cooperative, but only if the by-law or amendment
  is not contrary to a by-law made by the members.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The directors must present a by-law or an amendment
  to a by-law that is made under subsection (2) to the members at the
  next meeting of members and the members may, by special resolution,
  confirm or amend it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}By-law not confirmed

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a by-law or an amendment to a by-law made by the
  directors is not confirmed, with or without amendments, under
  subsection (3), the by-law or amendment is repealed as of the date of
  the meeting of members at which it was not confirmed.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proposal of by-law

**[[74]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-74 .sectionLabel}** A member may, in
accordance with section 58, make a proposal to make, amend or repeal a
by-law.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effective date of by-law

- **[[75]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  by-law or an amendment to or repeal of a by-law made by the members is
  effective from the later of the date of the resolution made under
  subsection 73(1) and the date specified in the by-law, amendment or
  repeal.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effective date of by-law

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A by-law or an amendment to a by-law made by the
  directors is effective from the later of the date the by-law is made
  or amended by the directors and the date specified in the by-law,
  until it is confirmed, with or without amendment, under subsection
  73(3) or repealed under subsection 73(4) and, if the by-law is
  confirmed, or confirmed as amended, it is in effect in the form in
  which it was so confirmed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If by-law not approved

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a by-law or an amendment to a by-law made by the
  directors under subsection 73(2) is not submitted by the directors to
  the next meeting of the members as required under subsection 73(3),
  the by-law or amendment ceases to be effective from the date of that
  meeting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}New resolution of directors

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a by-law or an amendment to a by-law made by the
  directors under subsection 73(2) is repealed under subsection 73(4) or
  ceases to be effective under subsection (3), no subsequent resolution
  of the directors to make or amend a by-law that has substantially the
  same purpose or effect is effective until it is confirmed, or
  confirmed as amended, by the members.

## [PART 7]{.HLabel1}[Directors and Officers]{.HTitleText1} {#h-45740 .Part}

### [General Provisions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45741 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Number of directors

- **[[76]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  cooperative must have at least three directors or any greater minimum
  number that is set out in the articles.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of decrease

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the articles are amended to decrease the number of
  directors, the term of any incumbent director is not affected.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of increase

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} At a meeting to amend the articles to increase the
  number of directors, the persons who are entitled to do so may elect
  or appoint the additional number of directors.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Directors as members

**[[77]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77 .sectionLabel}** Not less than two thirds
of the directors, or any greater proportion that is provided for by the
articles, must be members of the cooperative, or representatives of
members that are entities or members of members that are cooperative
entities.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualifications

- **[[78]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-78 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person is not qualified to be a director if the person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is not an individual;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is less than eighteen years of age;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is incapable; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} has the status of bankrupt.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional qualifications

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A cooperative may provide in its by-laws for
  qualifications or disqualifications of directors in addition to those
  in subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Status of directors

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Unless Part 21 applies to a cooperative, a majority
  of the directors must be individuals who are not full-time officers or
  employees of the cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Resident in Canada

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} At least twenty-five per cent of the directors must
  be resident in Canada. However, if the cooperative has only three
  directors, at least one director must be resident in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Member election

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections 124(3) and (4), all directors
  are to be elected by the members.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 78
- 2001, c. 14, s. 159
- 2018, c. 8, s. 58
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-78-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 78"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Functions of directors

**[[79]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79 .sectionLabel}** Subject to this Act and
to the articles and any unanimous agreement, the directors manage or
supervise the management of the business and affairs of the cooperative.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duties

- **[[80]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-80 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  director and officer must, in exercising the powers and performing the
  duties of office,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} act honestly and in good faith with a view to the
    best interests of the cooperative; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} exercise the care, diligence and skill that a
    reasonably prudent person would exercise in comparable
    circumstances.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty of compliance

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every director and officer must comply with this Act,
  the articles, the by-laws and any unanimous agreement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No exculpation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection 115(5), no provision in a
  contract, the articles, the by-laws, a unanimous agreement or a
  resolution relieves a director or officer from complying with this Act
  and the regulations or from liability for non-compliance.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of directors

- **[[81]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-81 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} At the
  time of sending articles of incorporation, the incorporators must send
  the Director a notice of directors, in the form that the Director
  fixes.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}First directors

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When a cooperative comes into existence, the
  individuals identified in the notice have all the powers and duties of
  directors until the first meeting of members.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}First meeting

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} At the first meeting of members after a cooperative
  comes into existence, the directors are to be elected or appointed in
  accordance with this Act, the articles, the by-laws and any unanimous
  agreement.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Organizational meeting

- **[[82]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-82 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} After
  a cooperative comes into existence, a meeting of directors must be
  held at which the directors may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} adopt forms of security certificates and of
    cooperative records;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} admit persons to membership in the cooperative and
    issue or authorize the issuance of membership shares and member loan
    certificates;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} appoint officers;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} appoint an auditor to hold office until the first
    meeting of members;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} make arrangements with an appropriate financial
    institution; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} transact any other business necessary to organize
    the cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An incorporator or a director may call a meeting of
  directors referred to in subsection (1) by giving not less than five
  days notice of the meeting to each director, stating the time and
  place of the meeting.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rules for election of directors

- **[[83]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-83 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless
  the articles, the by-laws or a unanimous agreement provides otherwise,
  the election of the directors must be in accordance with this section,
  subsection 78(5) and sections 84 to 87 and 124.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Annual election

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Elections of directors are to be held annually at a
  meeting of the persons who are entitled to elect or appoint them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Term of office

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Directors hold office until the close of the meeting
  at which their successors are elected.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Staggered terms

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} It is not necessary that all directors elected at a
  meeting of the cooperative hold office for the same term.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Re-election

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Directors may be re-elected.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Election or appointment as director

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} An individual who is elected or appointed to hold
  office as a director is not a director and is deemed not to have been
  elected or appointed to hold office as a director unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} he or she was present at the meeting when the
    election or appointment took place and he or she did not refuse to
    hold office as a director; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} he or she was not present at the meeting when the
    election or appointment took place and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} he or she consented to hold office as a director
      in writing before the election or appointment or within ten days
      after it, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} he or she has acted as a director pursuant to
      the election or appointment.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2001, c. 14, s. 160\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Secret ballot

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} Directors are to be elected by secret ballot if the
  number of nominees exceeds the number of directors to be elected.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Casting ballot

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} A ballot that is cast for the election of more than
  the number of directors to be elected is null or void.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determining election of directors

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} The individual who receives the greatest number of
  votes at an election of directors is elected a director and the other
  individuals who receive, in descending order, the next greatest
  numbers of votes are also elected directors, until the number of
  directors to be elected has been elected. If two individuals receive
  an equal number of votes for the last vacancy on the board, the
  directors already elected to the board must determine which of the two
  individuals is to be elected.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Majority voting

  [(10.1)]{.lawlabel} If, at a meeting of persons who are entitled to
  elect or appoint directors of a distributing cooperative --- other
  than in the case of a prescribed class of distributing cooperatives
  --- at which an election of directors is required, there is only one
  candidate nominated for each position available on the board, each
  candidate is elected only if the number of votes cast in their favour
  represents a majority of the votes cast for and against them by those
  persons who are present in person or represented by proxy, unless the
  articles require a greater number of votes.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incumbent director not elected

  [(10.2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (3) and paragraph 84(1)(b), if
  an incumbent director who was a candidate in an election held in
  accordance with subsection (10.1) was not elected during the election,
  the director may continue in office until the earlier of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the 90th day after the day of the election; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the day on which their successor is appointed or
    elected.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Separate election

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} If shareholders have a right to elect one or more
  directors, they vote separately from the members.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Separate vote for each candidate

  [(12)]{.lawlabel} If the election of directors is for a prescribed
  cooperative, a separate vote by persons who are entitled to elect or
  appoint directors must be taken with respect to each candidate
  nominated for director.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(13)]{.lawlabel} If an individual who was a candidate in an election
  held in accordance with subsection (10.1) was not elected during that
  election, the individual is not to be appointed, except in prescribed
  circumstances, as a director under subsection 85(1) before the next
  meeting at which an election of directors is required by persons who
  are entitled to elect or appoint directors.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 83
- 2001, c. 14, s. 160
- [2018, c. 8, s. 59](#1037734-1373763){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037734-1373763.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-83-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 83"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum term

- **[[84]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-84
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 86, a director is
  entitled to hold office for a term expiring not later than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a cooperative that is not a
    distributing cooperative, the close of the third annual meeting of
    members following the director's election; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a distributing cooperative, the
    close of the next annual meeting of members following the director's
    election.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions --- certain distributing
  cooperatives

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(b), in the case of any
  prescribed class of distributing cooperatives or in any prescribed
  circumstances respecting distributing cooperatives or classes of
  distributing cooperatives, the directors are not entitled to hold
  office for a term that is longer than three years.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 84
- [2018, c. 8, s. 60](#1037739-1373934){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037739-1373934.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-84-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 84"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vacancy on board

- **[[85]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), if there
  is a vacancy on the board of directors, except a vacancy because of an
  increase in the number or the minimum or maximum number of directors
  provided for in the articles or because of a failure to elect or
  appoint the number or minimum number of directors provided for in the
  articles, and there is still a quorum on the board, the remaining
  directors may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} continue to fulfil their functions without filling
    the vacancy; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (8), appoint a director to
    fill the vacancy.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Failure to appoint or elect minimum

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, at the close of a meeting of a cooperative, the
  persons at the meeting have failed to appoint or elect the number or
  minimum number of directors required by this Act or the articles, the
  purported appointment or election of directors at the meeting

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is valid if the directors purported to be appointed
    or elected and the incumbent directors, if any, whose terms did not
    expire at the close of the meeting, together constitute a quorum; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is null or void if the directors purported to be
    appointed or elected and the incumbent directors, if any, whose
    terms did not expire at the close of the meeting, together do not
    constitute a quorum.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special meeting

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The articles may provide that if there is a vacancy
  on the board of directors, the remaining directors must call a special
  meeting of the persons who are entitled to vote for the purpose of
  electing or appointing directors to fill the vacancy.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Absence of quorum

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If there is not a quorum of directors, the directors
  must, without delay, call a special meeting of the persons who are
  entitled to vote to fill the vacancy, and if they fail to do so, any
  person who is entitled to vote at a meeting of the cooperative may
  call the meeting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vacant board

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the board of directors is vacant, any person who
  is entitled to vote at a meeting of the cooperative may call a special
  meeting to elect directors to fill the vacancies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed directors

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If all of the directors have resigned or been removed
  without replacement, a person who manages or supervises the management
  of the business and affairs of the cooperative is deemed to be a
  director for the purposes of this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (6) does not apply to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an officer who manages the business or affairs of
    the cooperative under the direction or control of a member,
    shareholder or other person;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a lawyer, notary, accountant or other professional
    who participates in the management of the cooperative solely for the
    purpose of providing professional services; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a trustee in bankruptcy, receiver,
    receiver-manager, sequestrator or secured creditor who participates
    in the management of the cooperative or exercises control over its
    property solely for the purpose of the realization of security or
    the administration of a bankrupt's estate, in the case of a trustee
    in bankruptcy.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Class director

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), if there is a vacancy in
  the position of a director who is to be elected by a class vote of
  either the members or the shareholders,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any remaining directors elected or appointed by
    that class may act under subsection (1); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if there are no such remaining directors, any
    member of the class may act under subsection (5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unexpired term

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} Unless the by-laws provide otherwise, a director who
  is elected or appointed to fill a vacancy holds office for the
  unexpired term of their predecessor in office.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 85
- 2001, c. 14, s. 161
- 2011, c. 21, s. 76(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-85-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 85"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuation in office

**[[86]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-86 .sectionLabel}** If the election of
directors does not occur at the time fixed by this Act, the by-laws or a
unanimous agreement, the directors then in office continue in office
until their successors are elected.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ceasing to hold office

- **[[87]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-87 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  director ceases to hold office when he or she dies, resigns, is
  removed from office or is no longer qualified to be a director.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Resignation date

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A resignation of a director becomes effective on the
  later of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the day a written letter of resignation is sent to
    the cooperative, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the day specified in the letter of resignation.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal of directors

- **[[88]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  director may be removed from office by ordinary resolution at a
  special meeting of the persons who are entitled to vote in the
  election or appointment of that director.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vacancy

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The vacancy created by the removal of a director may
  be filled at the meeting at which the director was removed.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Resignation statement

- **[[89]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-89 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  director who resigns is entitled to submit to the cooperative a
  written statement giving the reasons for the resignation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Opposition statement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A director who learns of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a meeting of the cooperative called for the purpose
    of removing the director, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a meeting of the cooperative, or of the directors,
    at which another person is to be appointed or elected to succeed or
    replace the director,

  is entitled to attend and address the meeting, or to submit a written
  statement to the cooperative, giving the reasons why the director
  opposes any proposed action or resolution at the meeting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Circulation of statement

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} When the cooperative receives a statement under
  subsection (1) or (2), it must ensure that a copy of it is sent
  without delay to the Director and to every person who is entitled to
  receive notice of the meeting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Immunity for statement

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} No cooperative or person acting on its behalf incurs
  any liability by reason only of circulating a director's statement in
  compliance with subsection (3).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to attend meetings

**[[90]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-90 .sectionLabel}** A director is entitled to
receive notice of, to attend and to be heard at every meeting of the
cooperative.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of change of director or
director's address

- **[[91]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-91 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  cooperative must, within fifteen days after

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a change is made among its directors, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it receives a notice of change of address of a
    director referred to in subsection (2),

  send to the Director a notice, in the form that the Director fixes,
  setting out the change.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Director's change of address

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A director must, within fifteen days after changing
  his or her address, send the cooperative a notice of that change.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to court

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Any interested person, or the Director, may apply to
  a court for an order to require a cooperative to comply with
  subsection (1), and the court may so order and make any further order
  it thinks fit.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 91
- 2001, c. 14, s. 162
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Place of meetings

- **[[92]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-92 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  directors may meet at any place unless the articles or by-laws provide
  otherwise.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of meeting

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Unless the by-laws provide otherwise, notice of the
  time and place of a meeting of directors must be given to every
  director by sending the notice not less than ten days before the date
  of the meeting to the latest address of the director as shown on the
  records of the cooperative.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of notice

**[[93]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-93 .sectionLabel}** Notice of a meeting of
directors must specify the time and place of the meeting but need not
specify the matter to be dealt with at the meeting, unless the matter is
one listed in subsection 109(3).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Waiver of notice

**[[94]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-94 .sectionLabel}** A director may, in any
manner, waive notice of a meeting of directors and attendance at a
meeting constitutes waiver of notice unless the director is attending
for the purpose of objecting to the meeting on the ground that it was
not lawfully called.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adjourned meeting

**[[95]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-95 .sectionLabel}** There is no need to give
notice for the continuation of a meeting of directors that is adjourned
or for a meeting of directors that immediately follows an annual
meeting.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quorum

**[[96]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-96 .sectionLabel}** Unless the articles, the
by-laws or a unanimous agreement provides for a greater proportion, a
majority of the number or minimum number of directors, subject to
section 97, constitutes a quorum at any meeting of directors or of a
committee of directors, and, despite any vacancy among the directors, a
quorum of directors may exercise all the powers of the directors.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quorum

- **[[97]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-97 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} To
  constitute a quorum,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at least twenty-five per cent of the directors at
    the meeting must be resident in Canada or, if the cooperative has
    only three directors, at least one of the directors at the meeting
    must be resident in Canada; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a majority of the directors at the meeting must be
    members of the cooperative, or representatives of members that are
    entities or members of members that are cooperative entities.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), a meeting of directors may be
  held without the number of directors resident in Canada required under
  that subsection if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a director who is resident in Canada and who is not
    present approves, in writing or by telephonic, electronic or other
    communication facilities, the business transacted at the meeting;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the required number would have been present had
    that director been present at the meeting.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 97
- 2001, c. 14, s. 163
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Participation

- **[[98]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-98
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the by-laws, a director
  may, in accordance with the regulations, if any, participate in a
  meeting of directors or of a committee of directors by means of a
  telephonic, electronic or other communication facility that permits
  all participants to communicate adequately with each other during the
  meeting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presence

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A director participating in a meeting by a means
  referred to in subsection (1) is deemed to be present at the meeting.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 98
- 2001, c. 14, s. 164
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Validity of acts

**[[99]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-99 .sectionLabel}** No act of a director or
officer is invalid by reason only of an irregularity in the person's
election or appointment or because the director or officer is not
qualified to be one.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Resolution in lieu of meeting

- **[[100]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-100 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  resolution in writing, signed by all the directors entitled to vote on
  that resolution at a meeting of directors or of a committee of
  directors, is as valid as if it had been passed at a meeting of
  directors or of a committee of directors.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copy of resolutions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A copy of every resolution referred to in
  subsection (1) must be kept with the minutes of the meetings of the
  directors or committee of directors.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Evidence

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Unless a ballot is demanded, an entry in the minutes
  of a meeting to the effect that the chairperson of the meeting
  declared a resolution to be carried or defeated is, in the absence of
  evidence to the contrary, proof of the fact without proof of the
  number or proportion of the votes recorded in favour of or against the
  resolution.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 100
- 2001, c. 14, s. 165
:::

### [Liability]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45939 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Directors' liability

- **[[101]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-101
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Directors who vote for or consent to
  a resolution authorizing the issue of a membership share or an
  investment share in exchange for a thing or service other than money
  are jointly and severally, or solidarily, liable to the cooperative to
  make good any amount by which the thing or service received is less
  than the fair equivalent of the money that the cooperative would have
  received if the membership share or investment share had been issued
  for money on the date of the resolution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A director is not liable under subsection (1) if the
  director proves that he or she did not know and could not reasonably
  have known that the membership share or investment share was issued
  for a thing or service less than the fair equivalent of the money that
  the cooperative would have received if the membership share or
  investment share had been issued for money on the date of the
  resolution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Further liability

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Directors who vote for or consent to resolutions
  authorizing any of the following matters are jointly and severally, or
  solidarily, liable to restore to the cooperative any amounts so
  distributed or paid and not otherwise recovered by the cooperative:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a redemption or other acquisition of shares or the
    repayment of member loans contrary to this Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a commission contrary to this Act;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a payment of a dividend, a patronage return or
    interest contrary to this Act;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2001, c. 14, s. 166\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a payment of an indemnity contrary to this Act; or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} any other payment contrary to this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contribution

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A director who satisfies a judgment for a debt owed
  under this section is entitled to contribution from the other
  directors who were liable for the debt.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recovery

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A director who is liable under subsection (3) may
  apply to a court for an order to recover any money or property
  referred to in paragraphs (3)(a) to (f).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order of court

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A court may, on application under subsection (5), if
  it is satisfied that it is equitable to do so,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} order any person to pay or deliver to the director
    any money or property referred to in paragraphs (3)(a) to (f) that
    was paid or distributed to that person;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} order a cooperative to return or issue membership
    shares or investment shares to a person from whom the cooperative
    redeemed or otherwise acquired membership shares or investment
    shares;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} order any person to repay to the cooperative the
    amount of a member loan that was repaid; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} make any further order that it sees fit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation period

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} An action to enforce a liability imposed by this
  section may not be commenced more than two years after the date of the
  resolution authorizing the action complained of.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 101
- 2001, c. 14, s. 166
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability for wages

- **[[102]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-102
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this section and any
  other applicable law, each director is jointly and severally, or
  solidarily, liable to the employees of the cooperative for all debts
  payable to them for services performed while the director held office.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2001, c. 14, s. 167\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount of liability

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The amount of the liability under this section may
  not exceed six months wages in the case of each employee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions precedent

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A director is not liable under this section unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cooperative was sued for the debt within six
    months after it became due and execution was returned unsatisfied in
    whole or in part;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the cooperative has commenced liquidation or
    dissolution proceedings or was dissolved and a claim for the debt
    was proved no later than six months after the earlier of the date of
    commencement of the proceedings and the date of the dissolution; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the cooperative made an assignment or was made
    subject to a bankruptcy order under the [Bankruptcy and Insolvency
    Act](/eng/acts/B-3) and a claim for the debt was proved no later
    than six months after the date of the assignment or bankruptcy
    order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation period

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A director is not liable under this section unless he
  or she is sued while holding office or no later than two years after
  ceasing to be a director.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount due after execution

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If execution referred to in paragraph (4)(a) has
  issued, the amount recoverable from the director is the amount
  remaining unsatisfied after execution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subrogation of director

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} A director who pays a debt owed under this section
  that is proven in liquidation and dissolution or bankruptcy
  proceedings is subrogated to any priority that the employee would have
  been entitled to and, if a judgment has been obtained, the director is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in Quebec, subrogated to the employee's rights as
    declared in the judgment; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} elsewhere in Canada, entitled to an assignment of
    the judgment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contribution

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} A director who pays a debt owed under this section is
  entitled to contribution from the other directors who were liable for
  the debt.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 102
- 2001, c. 14, s. 167
- 2004, c. 25, s. 188
- 2011, c. 21, s. 77
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-102-20041215.html "Link to previous version of section 102"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Conflict of Interests]{.HTitleText2} {#h-45990 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure of interest

- **[[103]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-103 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  director or officer must, in accordance with this section, disclose to
  the cooperative the nature and extent of any interest that the
  director or officer has in a material contract or transaction, or a
  proposed material contract or transaction, with the cooperative, and
  any material change to any such interest, if the director or officer

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is a party to the contract or transaction;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is a director or officer --- or an individual
    acting in a similar capacity --- of a party to the contract or
    transaction; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} has a material interest in a party to the contract
    or transaction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} This section does not require the disclosure of an
  interest in a contract or transaction that is available to and
  customarily entered into between the cooperative and its members, if
  the contract or transaction is on the same terms as are generally
  available to members.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of disclosure

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The director or officer must make the disclosure in
  writing to the cooperative or request to have it entered in the
  minutes of the meetings of directors.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time of disclosure for a director

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A director must make the disclosure

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the meeting of directors at which the proposed
    contract or transaction is first considered;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the director was not interested in the proposed
    contract or transaction at the time of the meeting referred to in
    paragraph (a), at the first meeting after the director acquires an
    interest in it;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if there is a material change in the director's
    interest in the contract, transaction, proposed contract or proposed
    transaction, at the first meeting after the change;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the director becomes interested in a contract or
    transaction after it is made, at the first meeting after the
    director acquires an interest in it;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the director had an interest in the contract or
    transaction before becoming a director, at the first meeting after
    becoming a director; or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if the contract or transaction is one that would,
    in the ordinary course of business, not require the approval of the
    directors, as soon as the director becomes aware of the contract or
    transaction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time of disclosure for an officer

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} An officer who is not a director must make the
  disclosure

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} immediately after becoming aware that the contract,
    transaction, proposed contract or proposed transaction is to be
    considered or has been considered at a meeting of directors;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the officer acquires an interest in a contract
    or transaction after it is made, immediately after the officer
    acquires an interest in it;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if there is a material change in the officer's
    interest in the contract, transaction, proposed contract or proposed
    transaction, immediately after the change;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the officer had an interest in the contract or
    transaction before becoming an officer, immediately after becoming
    an officer; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the contract or transaction is one that would,
    in the ordinary course of business, not require the approval of the
    directors, as soon as the officer becomes aware of the contract or
    transaction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The members and shareholders may examine the portions
  of minutes of meetings of directors, of other documents that contain
  disclosures under this section and of the general notice referred to
  in section 105 during the usual business hours of the cooperative.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2001, c. 14, s. 168\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 103
- 2001, c. 14, s. 168
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Voting

- **[[104]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  director who is interested in a contract or transaction referred to in
  subsection 103(1) may not vote on any resolution to approve the
  contract or transaction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a contract or transaction that relates primarily to
    the director's remuneration as a director, officer, employee or
    agent or mandatary of the cooperative or of one of its subsidiaries;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a contract for indemnity or insurance under section
    113.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 104
- 2001, c. 14, s. 169
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuing disclosure

**[[105]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-105 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
section 103, a general notice to the directors declaring that the
director or officer is a director or officer of an entity or acting in a
similar capacity, or has a material interest in an entity, or that there
has been a change in the nature of their interest in the entity, and
that the director or officer is therefore to be regarded as interested
in a contract or transaction made with that entity, as declared in the
notice, is a sufficient declaration of interest in a contract or
transaction so made.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of disclosure

**[[106]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-106 .sectionLabel}** A contract or
transaction for which disclosure is required under section 103 is not
invalid, and the director or officer is not accountable to the
cooperative, its members or its shareholders for any profit realized
from the contract or transaction, because of the director's or officer's
interest in the contract or transaction or because the director was
present or was counted to determine whether a quorum existed at the
meeting of directors or committee of directors that considered the
contract or transaction, if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} disclosure of the interest was made in accordance
  with sections 103 to 105;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the directors approved the contract or transaction;
  and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the contract or transaction was reasonable and fair
  to the cooperative when it was approved.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 106
- 2001, c. 14, s. 170
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Confirmation

**[[106.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-106.1 .sectionLabel}** Even if the
conditions of section 106 are not met, a director or officer, acting
honestly and in good faith, is not accountable to the cooperative, its
members or its shareholders for any profit realized from a contract or
transaction for which disclosure is required under section 103 and the
contract or transaction is not invalid by reason only of the interest of
the director or officer in the contract or transaction if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the contract or transaction is approved or confirmed
  by special resolution at a meeting of the members;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} disclosure of the interest was made to the members in
  a manner sufficient to indicate its nature before the contract or
  transaction was approved or confirmed; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the contract or transaction was reasonable and fair
  to the cooperative when it was approved or confirmed.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 170
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Court order

**[[107]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-107 .sectionLabel}** If a director or
officer of a cooperative fails to disclose an interest in a material
contract or transaction in accordance with section 103, or otherwise
fails to comply with sections 103 to 106.1, a court may, on the
application of the cooperative or a member or shareholder, set aside the
contract or transaction on any terms that it thinks fit or order that
the director or officer account to the cooperative, its members or its
shareholders for any profit realized from the contract or transaction.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 107
- 2001, c. 14, s. 170
:::

### [Officers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-46050 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Officers

**[[108]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-108 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the
articles, the by-laws and any unanimous agreement, the directors may

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} designate the offices of the cooperative;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} specify the powers and duties of each office;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} appoint any individual of full capacity, including a
  director, to be an officer;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} appoint one individual to hold more than one office;
  and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} delegate to the officers the power to manage the
  business and affairs of the cooperative, except a power referred to in
  subsection 109(3).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appointment of managing director or
committee

- **[[109]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-109 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  directors may appoint from among themselves a managing director or any
  committee they consider necessary.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Residency

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The managing director must be resident in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The directors may delegate to a managing director or
  a committee composed of at least three directors any powers of the
  directors, except the power to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} fill a vacancy among the directors or in the office
    of the auditor, or appoint additional directors;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} declare dividends on shares, interest on member
    loans or patronage returns;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} approve a financial statement of the cooperative;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} submit to the persons who are entitled to vote on
    them questions or matters required to be approved at a meeting of
    the cooperative;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} make decisions that by this Act, the articles or a
    unanimous agreement are required to be made by a vote of greater
    than a majority of the directors;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} redeem or otherwise acquire shares issued by the
    cooperative;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} pay a commission referred to in section 128, except
    as authorized by the directors;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} approve a management proxy circular referred to in
    Part 9;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} issue securities, except in the manner and on the
    terms authorized by the directors; or

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} issue investment shares of a series under section
    126 except as authorized by the directors.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A committee referred to in subsection (1) may
  exercise the powers that have been delegated to it subject to any
  restrictions imposed by the directors.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Membership on committee

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A director who is appointed to a committee may remain
  on the committee until the appointment is revoked or the person ceases
  to be a director.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duties

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A committee appointed under this section must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} fix its quorum at not less than a majority of its
    members;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} keep minutes of its proceedings; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} report, at each meeting of the directors, on the
    proceedings of the committee since the last meeting of the
    directors.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 109
- 2001, c. 14, s. 171
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed assent

- **[[110]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-110 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  director who is present at a meeting of directors is deemed to have
  consented to any resolution made or action taken at the meeting unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the director requests that a dissent be entered in
    the minutes of the meeting or the dissent is so entered;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the director sends a written dissent to the
    secretary of the meeting before it is adjourned; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the director sends a written dissent by confirmed
    delivery service or delivers it personally, to the registered office
    of the cooperative immediately after the meeting is adjourned.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Loss of right to dissent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A director who votes for or expressly consents to a
  resolution or action taken at a meeting is not entitled to later
  dissent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed assent of absent director

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A director who is not present at a meeting of
  directors is deemed to have consented to any resolution made or action
  taken at the meeting unless the director, within seven days after
  becoming aware of the resolution or action,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} causes a dissent to be entered in the minutes of
    the meeting; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} sends a written dissent by confirmed delivery
    service or delivers it personally, to the registered office of the
    cooperative.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Due diligence

**[[111]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-111 .sectionLabel}** A director is not
liable under this Part if the director exercised the care, diligence and
skill that a reasonably prudent person would have exercised in
comparable circumstances, including reliance in good faith on

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} financial statements of the cooperative represented
  to the director by an officer of the cooperative or in a written
  report of the auditor of the cooperative fairly to reflect the
  financial condition of the cooperative; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a report of a person whose profession lends
  credibility to a statement made by the professional person.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 111
- 2001, c. 14, s. 172
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Remuneration

**[[112]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-112 .sectionLabel}** Unless the articles,
the by-laws or a unanimous agreement provides otherwise, the directors
may fix the remuneration of the directors, officers and employees of the
cooperative.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Indemnification

- **[[113]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-113 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  cooperative may indemnify an individual who is or was a director or
  officer of the cooperative, or who acts or acted at the cooperative's
  request as a director or officer, or an individual acting in a similar
  capacity, of another entity, against all costs, charges and expenses,
  including an amount paid to settle an action or satisfy a claim,
  reasonably incurred by the individual in respect of any civil,
  criminal, administrative, investigative or other proceeding in which
  the individual is involved by reason of their association with the
  cooperative or entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Advance of costs

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A cooperative may advance moneys to a director,
