  relation to any matter or group of matters is less than five per cent
  of all the votes that might be cast by shareholders present in person
  or represented by proxy at the meeting on the ballot, unless a
  shareholder or proxyholder demands a ballot,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the chairperson may conduct the vote in respect of
    that matter or group of matters by a show of hands; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a proxyholder or alternate proxyholder may vote in
    respect of that matter or group of matters by a show of hands.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty of intermediary

- **[[169]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-169
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Shares of a cooperative that are
  registered in the name of an intermediary or a nominee of an
  intermediary and not beneficially owned by the intermediary must not
  be voted unless the intermediary, without delay after receipt of the
  notice of the meeting, management proxy circular, dissident's proxy
  circular and any other documents other than the form of proxy sent to
  shareholders by or on behalf of any person for use in connection with
  the meeting, sends a copy of the document to the beneficial owner and,
  except when the intermediary has received written voting instructions
  from the beneficial owner, a written request for those instructions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on voting

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An intermediary, or a proxyholder appointed by an
  intermediary, may not vote shares that the intermediary does not
  beneficially own and that are registered in the name of the
  intermediary or in the name of a nominee of the intermediary unless
  the intermediary or proxyholder, as the case may be, receives written
  voting instructions from the beneficial owner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person by or on behalf of whom a solicitation is
  made must provide, at the request of an intermediary, without delay,
  to the intermediary at the person's expense the necessary number of
  copies of the documents referred to in subsection (1), other than
  copies of the document requesting voting instructions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Instructions to intermediary

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An intermediary shall vote or appoint a proxyholder
  to vote any shares referred to in subsection (1) in accordance with
  any written voting instructions received from the beneficial owner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Beneficial owner as proxyholder

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a beneficial owner so requests and provides an
  intermediary with appropriate documentation, the intermediary must
  appoint the beneficial owner or a nominee of the beneficial owner as
  proxyholder.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Validity

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The failure of an intermediary to comply with this
  section does not render null or void any meeting of shareholders or
  any action taken at the meeting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in this section gives an intermediary the
  right to vote shares that the intermediary is otherwise prohibited
  from voting.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 169
- 2001, c. 14, s. 190
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restraining order

- **[[170]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-170 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  form of proxy, management proxy circular or dissident's proxy circular
  contains an untrue statement of a material fact or omits to state a
  material fact required in it or necessary to make a statement
  contained in it not misleading in the light of the circumstances in
  which it was made, the Director or any other interested person may
  apply to a court and the court may make any order it thinks fit,
  including

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an order restraining the solicitation or the
    holding of the meeting, or restraining any person from implementing
    or acting on a resolution passed at the meeting to which the form of
    proxy, management proxy circular or dissident's proxy circular
    relates;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an order requiring correction of any form of proxy
    or proxy circular and a further solicitation; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an order adjourning the meeting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to Director

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant under this section must give the
  Director notice of the application and the Director is entitled to
  appear and to be heard in person or by counsel.

## [PART 10]{.HLabel1}[Insider Trading]{.HTitleText1} {#h-46702 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation

- **[[171]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-171 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  definitions in this subsection apply in this Part.

  [[business combination]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[business combination]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an acquisition
      of all or substantially all the property of one entity by another,
      or an amalgamation of two or more entities, or any similar
      reorganization between or among two or more
      entities. ([regroupement d'entreprises]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[insider]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[insider]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, except in section 173,

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a director or officer of a distributing
        cooperative;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a director or officer of a subsidiary of a
        distributing cooperative;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a director or officer of an entity that enters
        into a business combination with a distributing cooperative; and

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a person employed or retained by a distributing
        cooperative. ([initié]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the chairperson of the board
      of directors, president, vice-president, secretary, treasurer,
      comptroller, general counsel, general manager, managing director,
      or any other individual who performs functions for an entity
      similar to those normally performed by an individual occupying any
      of those offices. ([dirigeant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an investment share that
      carries voting rights under the articles or in the circumstances
      in which voting rights are exercisable with respect to it under
      this Act, and includes

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a security currently convertible into such an
        investment share; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} currently exercisable options and rights to
        acquire such an investment share or such a convertible
        security. ([part]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Further interpretation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a director or an officer of an entity that
    beneficially owns, directly or indirectly, shares of a distributing
    cooperative, or that exercises control or direction over shares of
    the distributing cooperative, or that has a combination of any such
    ownership, control and direction, carrying more than the prescribed
    percentage of voting rights attached to all of the outstanding
    shares of the distributing cooperative not including shares held by
    the entity as underwriter while those shares are in the course of a
    distribution to the public is deemed to be an insider of the
    distributing cooperative;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a director or an officer of an entity, or an
    individual acting in a similar capacity, that is a subsidiary is
    deemed to be an insider of its holding distributing cooperative;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a person is deemed to beneficially own shares
    beneficially owned by an entity controlled by them directly or
    indirectly;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an entity is deemed to beneficially own shares
    beneficially owned by its affiliates; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the acquisition or disposition by an insider of an
    option or right to acquire a share is deemed to be a change in the
    beneficial ownership of the share to which the option or right to
    acquire relates.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Membership shares

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, the sale of membership
  shares to members or the making of a member loan to a cooperative is
  not a distribution to the public.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2001, c. 14, s. 191\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 171
- 2001, c. 14, s. 191
- 2018, c. 8, s. 64(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-171-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 171"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition of short sale

- **[[172]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-172 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  insider shall knowingly sell, directly or indirectly, a security of a
  distributing cooperative or any of its affiliates if the insider
  selling the security does not own or has not fully paid for the
  security to be sold.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calls and puts

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No insider shall knowingly, directly or indirectly,
  buy a put or sell a call in respect of a security of the cooperative
  or any of its affiliates.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), an insider may sell a
  security that the insider does not own if the insider owns another
  security convertible into the security sold or an option or right to
  acquire the security sold and, no later than ten days after the sale,
  the insider

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} exercises the conversion privilege, option or right
    and delivers the security so acquired to the purchaser; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} transfers the convertible security, option or right
    to the purchaser.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 172
- 2001, c. 14, s. 192
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
[[insider]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[173]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-173 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, [[insider]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, with respect to a
  cooperative, means

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cooperative;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an affiliate of the cooperative;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a director or an officer of the cooperative or of
    any persons described in paragraphs (b), (e) or (g);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a member who controls more than ten per cent of the
    voting rights that may be exercised to elect or appoint a director
    of the cooperative;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a person who beneficially owns, directly or
    indirectly, shares of the cooperative or who exercises control or
    direction over shares of the cooperative, or a combination of any
    such ownership, control and direction, carrying more than the
    prescribed percentage of the voting rights attached to all of the
    outstanding shares of the cooperative other than shares held by the
    person as underwriter while those shares are in the course of a
    distribution to the public;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a person, other than a person described in
    paragraph (g), employed or retained by the cooperative or by a
    person described in paragraph (g);

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a person who engages in or proposes to engage in
    any business or professional activity with or on behalf of the
    cooperative;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a person who received, while they were a person
    described in any of paragraphs (a) to (g) material confidential
    information concerning the cooperative;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a person who receives material confidential
    information from a person described in this subsection or in
    subsection (2) or (2.1), including from a person described in this
    paragraph, and who knows or who ought reasonably to have known that
    the person giving the information is a person described in this
    subsection or in subsection (2) or (2.1), including a person
    described in this paragraph; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed insiders

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, a person who
  proposes to make a take-over bid (as defined in the regulations) for
  securities of a cooperative, or to enter into a business combination
  with a cooperative, is an insider of the cooperative with respect to
  material confidential information obtained from the cooperative and is
  an insider of the cooperative for the purposes of subsection (6).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed insiders

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} An insider of a person referred to in subsection
  (2), and an affiliate or associate of such a person, is an insider of
  the cooperative referred to in that subsection. Paragraphs (1)(b)
  to (j) apply in determining whether a person is such an insider except
  that references to "cooperative" in those paragraphs are to be read as
  references to "person described in subsection (2)".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expanded definition of "security"

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, the following are
  deemed to be a security of the cooperative:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a put, call or option or other right or obligation
    to purchase or sell a security of the cooperative; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a security of another entity whose market price
    varies materially with the market price of the securities of the
    cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Insider trading --- compensation to
  persons

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An insider who purchases or sells a security of the
  cooperative with knowledge of confidential information that, if
  generally known, might reasonably be expected to affect materially the
  value of any of the securities of the cooperative is liable to
  compensate the seller of the security or the purchaser of the
  security, as the case may be, for any damages suffered by the seller
  or purchaser as a result of the purchase or sale, unless the insider
  establishes that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the insider reasonably believed that the
    information had been generally disclosed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the information was known, or ought reasonably to
    have been known, by the seller or purchaser; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the purchase or sale of the security took place in
    the prescribed circumstances.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Insider trading --- compensation to
  cooperative

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The insider is accountable to the cooperative for any
  benefit or advantage received or receivable by the insider as a result
  of a purchase or sale described in subsection (4) unless the insider
  establishes the circumstances in paragraph 4(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tipping --- compensation to persons

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} An insider who discloses to another person
  confidential information with respect to the cooperative that has not
  been generally disclosed and that, if generally known, might
  reasonably be expected to affect materially the value of any of the
  securities of the cooperative is liable to compensate for damages any
  person who subsequently sells securities of the cooperative to, or
  purchases securities of the cooperative from, any person that received
  the information, unless the insider establishes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that the insider reasonably believed that the
    information had been generally disclosed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that the information was known, or ought reasonably
    to have been known, to the person who alleges to have suffered the
    damages;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that the disclosure of the information was
    necessary in the course of the business of the insider, except if
    the insider is a person described in subsection (2) or (2.1); or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the insider is a person described in
    subsection (2) or (2.1), that the disclosure of the information was
    necessary to effect the take-over bid or the business combination,
    as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tipping --- compensation to cooperative

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The insider is accountable to the cooperative for any
  benefit or advantage received or receivable by the insider as a result
  of a disclosure of the information as described in subsection (6)
  unless the insider establishes the circumstances in paragraph
  (6)(a), (c) or (d).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Measure of damages

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} The court may assess damages under subsection (4)
  or (6) in accordance with any measure of damages that it considers
  relevant in the circumstances. However, in assessing damages in a
  situation involving a security of a distributing cooperative, the
  court must consider the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the plaintiff is a purchaser, the price paid by
    the plaintiff for the security less the average market price of the
    security over the twenty trading days immediately following general
    disclosure of the information; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the plaintiff is a seller, the average market
    price of the security over the twenty trading days immediately
    following general disclosure of the information, less the price that
    the plaintiff received for the security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} If more than one insider is liable under
  subsection (4) or (6) with respect to the same transaction or series
  of transactions, their liability is joint and several, or solidary.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} An action to enforce a right created by
  subsections (4) to (7) may be commenced only within two years after
  discovery of the facts that gave rise to the cause of action.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 173
- 2001, c. 14, s. 193
:::

## [PART 11]{.HLabel1}[Compulsory Acquisition]{.HTitleText1} {#h-46785 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[174]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-174 .sectionLabel}** The definitions in this
section apply in this Part.

[[dissenting offeree]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[dissenting offeree]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a holder of a share
    of a class for which a take-over bid is made who does not accept the
    take-over bid, and includes a subsequent holder of that share who
    acquires it from the first-mentioned holder. ([pollicité
    dissident]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[offer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[offer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes an invitation to make an
    offer. ([pollicitation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[offeree]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[offeree]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person to whom a take-over
    bid is made. ([pollicité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[offeree cooperative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[offeree cooperative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a distributing
    cooperative whose shares are the object of a take-over
    bid. ([coopérative pollicitée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[offeror]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[offeror]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person, other than an agent
    or mandatary, who makes a take-over bid, and includes two or more
    persons who, directly or indirectly,

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} make take-over bids jointly or in concert; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} intend to exercise jointly or in concert voting
      rights attached to shares for which a take-over bid is
      made. ([pollicitant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an investment share, with or
    without voting rights, and includes

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a security currently convertible into such a
      share; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} currently exercisable options and rights to
      acquire such a share or such a convertible
      security. ([part]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[take-over bid]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[take-over bid]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an offer made by an
    offeror to shareholders of a distributing cooperative at
    approximately the same time to acquire all of the shares of a class
    of issued shares and includes an offer made by a distributing
    cooperative to repurchase all of the shares of a class of its
    shares. ([offre d'achat]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 174
- 2001, c. 14, s. 194
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to acquire

- **[[175]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-175 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  within one hundred and twenty days after the date of a take-over bid
  the bid is accepted by the holders of not less than ninety per cent of
  the shares of any class of shares to which the take-over bid relates,
  other than shares held at the date of the take-over bid by or on
  behalf of the offeror or an affiliate or associate of the offeror, the
  offeror is entitled, on complying with this section, to acquire the
  shares held by the dissenting offerees.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An offeror may acquire shares held by a dissenting
  offeree by sending, by confirmed delivery service within sixty days
  after the date of termination of the take-over bid and in any event
  within one hundred and eighty days after the date of the take-over
  bid, a notice to each dissenting offeree stating that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the offerees holding not less than ninety per cent
    of the shares to which the bid relates accepted the take-over bid;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the offeror is bound to take up and pay for or has
    taken up and paid for the shares of the offerees who accepted the
    take-over bid;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a dissenting offeree is required to elect

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to transfer their shares to the offeror on the
      terms on which the offeror acquired the shares of the offerees who
      accepted the take-over bid, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to demand payment of the fair value of their
      shares in accordance with subsections (10) to (19) by notifying
      the offeror within twenty days after the offeree receives the
      notice;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a dissenting offeree who does not notify the
    offeror in accordance with subparagraph (c)(ii) is deemed to have
    elected to transfer the shares to the offeror on the same terms on
    which the offeror acquired the shares from the offerees who accepted
    the take-over bid; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a dissenting offeree must send the shares to which
    the take-over bid relates to the offeree cooperative within twenty
    days after the offeree receives the offeror's notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of adverse claim

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Concurrently with sending the notice under subsection
  (2), the offeror must send to the offeree cooperative a notice of
  adverse claim in accordance with section 240 with respect to each
  share held by a dissenting offeree.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Share certificate

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A dissenting offeree to whom a notice is sent under
  subsection (2) must, within twenty days after receiving the notice,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} send the share certificates of the class of shares
    to which the take-over bid relates to the offeree cooperative; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} elect

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to transfer the shares to the offeror on the
      terms on which the offeror acquired the shares of the offerees who
      accepted the take-over bid, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to demand payment of the fair value of the
      shares in accordance with subsections (10) to (19) by notifying
      the offeror.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed election

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A dissenting offeree who does not notify the offeror
  in accordance with subparagraph (4)(b)(ii) is deemed to have elected
  to transfer the shares to the offeror on the same terms on which the
  offeror acquired the shares from the offerees who accepted the
  take-over bid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Within twenty days after the offeror sends a notice
  under subsection (2), the offeror must pay or transfer to the offeree
  cooperative the amount that the offeror would have had to pay to a
  dissenting offeree if the dissenting offeree had elected to accept the
  take-over bid under subparagraph (4)(b)(i).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amounts

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The offeree cooperative is deemed to hold in trust
  for the dissenting shareholders the amounts it receives under
  subsection (6), and the offeree cooperative must deposit the amounts
  in a separate account in a body corporate any of whose deposits are
  insured by the Canada Deposit Insurance Corporation or guaranteed by
  the Quebec Deposit Insurance Board or by any other similar entity
  created by the law of another province, and must place any thing
  received in lieu of money in the custody of such a body corporate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When cooperative is offeror

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} A cooperative that is an offeror making a take-over
  bid to repurchase all of the shares of a class of its shares is deemed
  to hold in trust for the dissenting shareholders the amounts that it
  would have had to pay or transfer to a dissenting offeree if the
  dissenting offeree had elected to accept the take-over bid under
  subparagraph (4)(b)(i), and the cooperative must deposit the amounts
  in a separate account in a body corporate any of whose deposits are
  insured by the Canada Deposit Insurance Corporation or guaranteed by
  the Quebec Deposit Insurance Board or by any other similar entity
  created by the law of another province, and must place any thing
  received in lieu of money in the custody of such a body corporate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty of offeree cooperative

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} Within thirty days after the offeror sends a notice
  under subsection (2), the offeree cooperative must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the payments required by subsection (6) are made
    and the money or things are deposited as required by subsection (7),
    issue to the offeror a share certificate in respect of the shares
    that were held by dissenting offerees;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} give to each dissenting offeree who elects to
    accept the take-over bid terms under subparagraph (4)(b)(i) and who
    sends share certificates as required by paragraph (4)(a) the money
    or thing to which the offeree is entitled, disregarding fractional
    shares, which may be paid for in money; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the payments required by subsection (6) are made
    and the money or things are deposited as required by subsection (7)
    or (8), send to each dissenting shareholder who has not sent share
    certificates as required by paragraph (4)(a) a notice stating that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the dissenting shareholder's shares have been
      cancelled,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the offeree cooperative or some designated
      person holds in trust for the dissenting shareholder the money or
      other things to which that shareholder is entitled as payment for
      or in exchange for the shares, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the offeree cooperative will, subject to
      subsections (10) to (19), send that money or thing to that
      shareholder without delay after receiving the shares.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to court

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} If a dissenting offeree has elected to demand
  payment of the fair value of the shares under subparagraph (4)(b)(ii),
  the offeror may, within twenty days after it has paid the money or
  transferred the things under subsection (6), apply to a court to fix
  the fair value of the shares of that dissenting offeree.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to court

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} If an offeror fails to apply to a court under
  subsection (10), a dissenting offeree may apply to a court for the
  same purpose within a further period of twenty days.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Status of dissenter if no court
  application

  [(12)]{.lawlabel} If no application is made to a court under
  subsection (11) within the period set out in that subsection, a
  dissenting offeree is deemed to have elected to transfer shares to the
  offeror on the same terms on which the offeror acquired the shares
  from the offerees who accepted the take-over bid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Venue

  [(13)]{.lawlabel} An application under subsection (10) or (11) must be
  made to a court having jurisdiction in the place where the cooperative
  has its registered office or in the province where the dissenting
  offeree resides if the cooperative carries on business in that
  province.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No security for costs

  [(14)]{.lawlabel} A dissenting offeree is not required to give
  security for costs in an application made under subsection (10) or
  (11).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Parties

  [(15)]{.lawlabel} On an application under subsection (10) or (11),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all dissenting offerees referred to in paragraph
    (4)(b) whose shares have not been acquired by the offeror must be
    joined as parties and are bound by the decision of the court; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the offeror must notify each affected dissenting
    offeree of the date, place and consequences of the application and
    of their right to appear and be heard in person or by counsel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of court

  [(16)]{.lawlabel} On an application to a court under subsection (10)
  or (11), the court may determine whether any other person is a
  dissenting offeree who should be joined as a party, and the court must
  then fix a fair value for the shares of all dissenting offerees.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appraisers

  [(17)]{.lawlabel} A court may in its discretion appoint one or more
  appraisers to assist the court to fix a fair value for the shares of a
  dissenting offeree.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Final order

  [(18)]{.lawlabel} The final order of the court must be made against
  the offeror in favour of each dissenting offeree and for the amount
  for shares as fixed by the court.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional powers

  [(19)]{.lawlabel} In connection with proceedings under this section, a
  court may make any order it thinks fit, including an order to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} fix the amount of money or things that are required
    to be held in trust under subsections (7) and (8);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} order that the money or thing be held in trust by a
    person other than the offeree cooperative;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} allow a reasonable rate of interest on the amount
    payable to each dissenting offeree from the date the offeree sends
    or delivers the share certificates under subsection (4) until the
    date of payment; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} order that any money payable to a shareholder who
    cannot be found be paid to the Receiver General, in which case
    subsection 327(3) applies.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 175
- 2001, c. 14, s. 195(E)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Distributing cooperative

- **[[176]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-176 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  shareholder holding shares of a distributing cooperative does not
  receive a notice under this Part, the shareholder may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} within ninety days after the date of the end of the
    take-over bid, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the shareholder did not receive an offer
    pursuant to the take-over bid, within ninety days after the later of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date of the end of the take-over bid, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the shareholder learned of the
      take-over bid,

  require the offeror to acquire those shares.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation to acquire

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a shareholder requires the offeror to acquire
  shares under subsection (1), the offeror must acquire the shares on
  the same terms under which the offeror acquired or will acquire the
  shares of the offerees who accepted the take-over bid.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 176
- 2001, c. 14, s. 196
:::

## [PART 12]{.HLabel1}[Security Certificates, Registers and Transfers]{.HTitleText1} {#h-46877 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-46878 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[177]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-177 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  definitions in this subsection apply in this Part.

  [[adverse claim]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[adverse claim]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a security,
      includes a claim that a transfer was or would be wrongful or that
      a particular adverse person is the owner of or has an interest or
      right in the security. ([opposition]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[bearer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[bearer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the person who is in
      possession of a security that is payable to bearer or endorsed in
      blank. ([porteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[broker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[broker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who is engaged in
      whole or in part in the business of buying and selling securities
      and who, in the transaction concerned, acts for, or buys a
      security from, or sells a security to a
      customer. ([courtier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[delivery]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[delivery]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means voluntary transfer of
      possession. ([livraison]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou
      [remise]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[fiduciary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fiduciary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any person who acts in a
      fiduciary capacity or as the administrator of the property of
      others and includes a personal representative of a deceased
      person. ([représentant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[fungible]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fungible]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to securities, means
      securities of which any unit is, by nature or usage of trade, the
      equivalent of any other like unit. ([fongibles]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[genuine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[genuine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means free of forgery or
      counterfeiting. ([authentique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[good faith]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[good faith]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, with respect to a transaction,
      means honesty in fact in the conduct of the transaction. ([bonne
      foi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[good faith purchaser]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[good faith purchaser]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, with respect to a
      security in bearer form or order form or a security in registered
      form issued to the purchaser or endorsed to the purchaser or
      endorsed in blank, means a purchaser for value in good faith and
      without notice of any adverse claim who takes delivery of the
      security. ([acquéreur de bonne foi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[holder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[holder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who is in possession
      of a security that is issued or endorsed to the person or to
      bearer or in blank. ([détenteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[issuer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[issuer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a cooperative that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is required by this Act to maintain a
        securities register;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in Quebec, issues securities that confer
        directly or indirectly rights in its property; or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} elsewhere in Canada, directly or indirectly
        creates fractional interests in its rights or property and
        issues securities as evidence of the fractional
        interests. ([émetteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[overissue]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[overissue]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the issue of securities in
      excess of any maximum number of securities that the issuer is
      authorized by its articles or a trust indenture to
      issue. ([émission excédentaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[purchaser]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[purchaser]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who takes an
      interest or right in a security by sale, mortgage, hypothec,
      pledge, issue, reissue, gift or any other voluntary
      transaction. ([acquéreur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[security]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[security]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or [[security
      certificate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a document issued by a
      cooperative that is

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in bearer, order or registered form,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} of a type commonly dealt in on securities
        exchanges or markets or commonly recognized in any area in which
        it is issued or dealt in as a medium for investment,

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} one of a class or series or by its terms
        divisible into a class or series of documents, and

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} evidence of an investment share, participation
        or other interest or right in or obligation of a cooperative,

      but does not include

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a membership share or a document evidencing a
        membership share, or

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a member loan or a document evidencing a member
        loan. ([valeur mobilière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou
        [certificat de valeurs mobilières]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[transfer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[transfer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes transmission by
      operation of law. ([transfert]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[trust indenture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[trust indenture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a trust indenture as
      defined in section 266. ([acte de fiducie]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[valid]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[valid]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means issued in accordance with the
      applicable law and the articles of the issuer, or validated under
      section 196. ([valide]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Negotiable instruments

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Except when a transfer is restricted and noted on a
  security in accordance with subsection 183(2), a security is a
  negotiable instrument.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registerable form

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A security is in registered form if it

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} specifies a person who is entitled to the security
    or to the rights it evidences, and its transfer is capable of being
    recorded in a securities register; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} bears a statement that it is in registered form.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order form

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A debt obligation is in order form if, by its terms,
  it is payable to the order of a person specified with reasonable
  certainty in it or to a person to whom it is assigned.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Bearer form

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A security is in bearer form if it is payable to
  bearer according to its terms and not by reason of an endorsement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Guarantor for issuer

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A guarantor for an issuer is deemed to be an issuer
  to the extent of the guarantee, whether or not the obligation is noted
  on the security.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 177
- 2011, c. 21, s. 88
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-177-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 177"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-46923 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

**[[178]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-178 .sectionLabel}** This Part governs the
transfer or transmission of a security.

### [Security Certificates]{.HTitleText2} {#h-46926 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security certificate

**[[179]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-179 .sectionLabel}** Every security holder
is entitled, at their option, to obtain from the cooperative

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a security certificate that complies with this Act;
  or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a non-transferable written acknowledgement of their
  right to obtain a security certificate.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fee

**[[180]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-180 .sectionLabel}** A cooperative may
charge a reasonable fee for a security certificate issued in respect of
a transfer.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Joint owners

**[[181]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-181 .sectionLabel}** If securities are held
jointly by more than one person,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a cooperative is not required to issue more than one
  security certificate in respect of those securities; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} delivery of a security certificate to one of the
  joint owners is sufficient delivery to them all.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Signatures

- **[[182]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-182 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  security certificate must be signed by at least one of the following
  persons, or a facsimile of the signature must be reproduced on the
  certificate:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a director, or an individual on their behalf, or an
    officer;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a transfer agent or branch transfer agent of the
    cooperative, or an individual on their behalf; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a trustee who certifies it in accordance with a
    trust indenture.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Former director

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A cooperative may issue security certificates that
  contain the signature of a person who is no longer a director or
  officer and the validity of the certificate is not adversely affected.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 182
- 2001, c. 14, s. 197
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of certificate

- **[[183]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-183 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following information must be stated on the face of each security
  certificate issued by a cooperative:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the cooperative;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the words "Incorporated under the [Canada
    Cooperatives Act](/eng/acts/C-1.7)", "constituée sous l'autorité de
    la [Loi canadienne sur les coopératives](/eng/acts/C-1.7)", "Subject
    to the [Canada Cooperatives Act](/eng/acts/C-1.7)" or "assujetti à
    la [Loi canadienne sur les coopératives](/eng/acts/C-1.7)";

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person to whom it was issued; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the number and class of shares and the designation
    of any series that the certificate represents.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notation of restrictions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No restriction, charge, lien, hypothec, agreement or
  endorsement described in subsection (3) is effective against a
  transferee of a security, issued by a cooperative or by a body
  corporate before it is continued under this Act, who has no actual
  knowledge of the restriction, charge, lien, hypothec, agreement or
  endorsement unless it or a reference to it is noted conspicuously on
  the security certificate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restrictions, etc.

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The restrictions, charges, liens, hypothecs,
  agreements and endorsements referred to in subsection (2) are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a restriction on transfer other than a constraint
    under section 130;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a charge, lien or hypothec in favour of the
    cooperative;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a unanimous agreement; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an endorsement under subsection 302(10).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restrictions

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the issued investment shares of a distributing
  cooperative remain outstanding and are held by more than one person,
  the cooperative must not restrict the transfer or ownership of its
  investment shares of any class or series except by way of a constraint
  under section 130.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conspicuous notation

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A reference to a constraint on the issue, transfer or
  ownership of any class or series of investment shares must be noted
  conspicuously on every security certificate evidencing such a share
  that is issued after the share becomes subject to the constraint under
  this Act, if the constraint is one

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the purpose of attaining or maintaining a level
    of Canadian ownership or control specified in its articles or
    required by law to carry on business or qualify for benefit; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to comply with any prescribed law.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of failure to note constraint

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A failure to note a constraint under subsection (5)
  does not invalidate the investment share or the security certificate
  and does not render the constraint ineffective.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 183
- 2001, c. 14, s. 198
- 2011, c. 21, s. 89
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-183-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 183"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of certificate

- **[[184]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-184
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every share certificate for an
  investment share of a cooperative that is authorized to issue shares
  of more than one class or series must clearly state

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the rights, privileges, restrictions and conditions
    attached to the shares of each class and series that exist when the
    share certificate is issued; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that the class or series of shares that it
    represents has rights, privileges, restrictions or conditions
    attached to it and that the cooperative will provide a shareholder,
    on demand and without charge, with a full copy of the text of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any rights, privileges, restrictions and
      conditions attached to each class authorized to be issued, and to
      each series, that have been fixed by the directors, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the authority of the directors to fix the
      rights, privileges, restrictions and conditions of subsequent
      series.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copy of information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a share certificate for an investment share
  contains the statement referred to in paragraph (1)(b), the
  cooperative must provide the shareholder, on demand and without
  charge, with a copy of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the rights, privileges, restrictions and conditions
    attached to each class or series of investment shares authorized to
    be issued; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the authority of the directors to fix the rights,
    privileges, restrictions and conditions of subsequent series of
    investment shares in the same class.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fractional shares

- **[[185]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-185 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  cooperative may issue for each fractional investment share, a
  certificate in registered form or scrip certificates in registered
  form that entitle the holder to receive a certificate for a full
  investment share in exchange for scrip certificates equalling a full
  investment share.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Replacement

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} A cooperative must, on the request of a holder of a
  certificate for a fractional investment share or scrip certificate
  that is in bearer form and that was issued before the coming into
  force of this subsection, issue in exchange to that holder, in
  registered form, a certificate for a fractional investment share or a
  scrip certificate, as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The directors may attach conditions to scrip
  certificates issued by the cooperative, including conditions that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the scrip certificates become void or, in Quebec,
    null if not exchanged for an investment share representing a full
    investment share before a specified date; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any investment shares for which the scrip
    certificates are exchangeable may, despite any pre-emptive right, be
    issued by the cooperative to any person and the proceeds distributed
    rateably to the holders of the scrip certificates.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Voting rights

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a fractional investment share is not
  entitled to exercise voting rights or to receive a dividend in respect
  of the investment share unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the fractional share results from a consolidation
    of investment shares; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the articles of the cooperative provide otherwise.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exercise of voting rights

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a scrip certificate is not entitled to
  exercise voting rights or to receive a dividend in respect of the
  scrip certificate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 185
- 2011, c. 21, s. 90(E)
- 2018, c. 8, s. 65
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-185-20111129.html "Link to previous version of section 185"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Registers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-47000 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Securities register

- **[[186]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-186 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  cooperative that issues securities must maintain a securities register
  in which it records the securities issued by it in registered form,
  showing with respect to each class or series

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the names, in alphabetical order, and the latest
    known address of each person who holds or previously held the
    security;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of securities held by each security
    holder; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date and particulars of the issue and transfer
    of each security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Location of register

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The securities register must be maintained at the
  cooperative's registered office or at any other place in Canada
  designated by the directors.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Branch registers

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A cooperative may maintain additional branch
  securities registers in other places designated by the directors.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of branch register

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A branch securities register only contains
  particulars of securities issued or transferred at the branch. The
  same information is also recorded in the central registry.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Destruction of certificates

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A cooperative, its agent or mandatary or a trustee as
  defined in section 266 is not required to produce

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a cancelled security certificate in registered
    form, an instrument referred to in any of subsections 142(1) to (3)
    that is cancelled or a like cancelled instrument in registered form
    six years after the date of its cancellation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a cancelled security certificate in bearer form or
    an instrument referred to in any of subsections 142(1) to (3) that
    is cancelled or a like cancelled instrument in bearer form after the
    date of its cancellation; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an instrument referred to in any of subsections
    142(1) to (3) or a like instrument, irrespective of its form, after
    the date of its expiration.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agent or mandatary

**[[187]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-187 .sectionLabel}** A cooperative may
appoint an agent or mandatary to maintain securities registers on its
behalf.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 187
- 2011, c. 21, s. 91(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-187-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 187"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration

**[[188]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-188 .sectionLabel}** The registration of the
issue or transfer of an investment share in a securities register
maintained by the cooperative is complete and valid registration for all
purposes.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Trustee

**[[189]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-189 .sectionLabel}** Subject to sections 51,
53 and 61, a cooperative or a trustee as defined in section 266 may
treat the person whose name appears on the securities register as the
owner of a security as its owner for all purposes.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof of ownership

**[[190]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-190 .sectionLabel}** If a cooperative
restricts the right to transfer its investment shares, the cooperative
may, despite section 189, treat a person as the registered holder of a
security if the person provides the cooperative with evidence that
reasonably meets the requirements of the cooperative that the person is

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the heir of a deceased security holder, or the
  fiduciary of the estate or succession of a deceased security holder,
  or of a registered security holder who is a minor, an incapable person
  or a missing person; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a liquidator of, or a trustee in bankruptcy for, a
  registered security holder.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 190
- 2018, c. 8, s. 66
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-190-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 190"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof of ownership

**[[191]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-191 .sectionLabel}** A cooperative must
treat a person, other than one described in section 190, as being
entitled to exercise the rights and privileges attached to a security if
the person provides proof that the person has acquired ownership of the
security by operation of law or has legal authority to exercise the
rights and privileges.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Joint owners

**[[192]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-192 .sectionLabel}** If satisfactory proof
of the death of a joint holder of a security is provided to a
cooperative, it may treat any surviving joint holders as the owner of
the security.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duties of cooperative

**[[193]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-193 .sectionLabel}** A cooperative is not
required to inquire into the existence of, or see to the performance of,
any duty owed to a third person by a registered holder or a person who
may be treated as a registered holder of a security.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minors

**[[194]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-194 .sectionLabel}** If an individual who is
less than 18 years of age exercises a right of ownership in a security
of a cooperative, no subsequent repudiation or avoidance or, in Quebec,
annulment or reduction of obligations is effective against the
cooperative.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 194
- 2011, c. 21, s. 92
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-194-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 194"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deceased owner

- **[[195]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-195
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to any applicable law
  relating to the collection of taxes, a person who is an heir or a
  fiduciary of an estate or succession of a deceased security holder is
  entitled to become the registered holder or to designate a registered
  holder if the person deposits the following information with the
  cooperative or its transfer agent, together with any reasonable
  assurances that the cooperative may require:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the security certificate or, in default of one, a
    document proving that the deceased was the security holder;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a document proving the death of the security
    holder; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a document proving that the heir or fiduciary has
    the right under the law of the place in which the deceased was
    domiciled immediately before their death to deal with the security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Endorsement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A security certificate referred to in paragraph
  (1)(a) must be endorsed

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a transfer to an heir or fiduciary,
    by that person; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, in a manner acceptable to the
    cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right of cooperative

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Deposit of the documents required by subsection (1)
  empowers a cooperative or its transfer agent to record in a securities
  register the transmission of a security from the deceased holder to
  the heir or fiduciary or to any person that the heir or fiduciary may
  designate and to treat the person who becomes a registered holder as
  the owner of the securities.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Overissue

- **[[196]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-196
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this section, the
  provisions of this Part that validate a security or compel its issue
  or reissue do not apply if the validation, issue or reissue of a
  security would result in overissue.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Identical security

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The person who is entitled to a validation or issue
  may, if there has been an overissue and if a valid security that is
  similar in all respects to the security involved in the overissue is
  reasonably available for purchase, compel the issuer to purchase and
  deliver that security against the surrender of the security that the
  person holds.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If identical security not available

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a valid security that is similar in all respects
  to the security involved in the overissue is not reasonably available
  for purchase, the person who is entitled to the validation or issue
  may recover from the issuer an amount equal to the price the last
  purchaser for value paid for the invalid security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Increase in capital

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The overissued securities are valid from the date
  they were issued only if the issuer increases the number of its
  authorized securities to a number equal to or greater than the number
  of securities previously authorized plus the number of the securities
  overissued.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemptions

**[[197]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-197 .sectionLabel}** Sections 147 to 149 and
152 do not apply to a payment or purchase by an issuer under section
196.

### [Proceedings]{.HTitleText2} {#h-47065 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rules of action

**[[198]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-198 .sectionLabel}** The following rules
apply in an action on a security:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} each signature on the security certificate or in a
  necessary endorsement is admitted unless specifically denied in the
  pleadings;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a signature on the security is presumed to be genuine
  and authorized but, if the effectiveness of the signature is in issue,
  the burden of establishing that it is genuine and authorized is on the
  party claiming under the signature;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if a signature is admitted or established, production
  of a certificate entitles the holder to recover on it unless the other
  party establishes a defence or defect going to the validity of the
  security; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the other party establishes the defence or defect,
  the plaintiff has the burden of establishing that the defect is
  ineffective against the plaintiff or some other person under whom the
  claim is made.

### [Delivery]{.HTitleText2} {#h-47072 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delivery of securities

- **[[199]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-199 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who is required to deliver securities may deliver any security
  of the specified issue

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in bearer form;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in registered form in the name of the transferee;
    or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} endorsed to the person or in blank.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) is subject to any agreement to the
  contrary, to the provisions of this Act, of any other applicable Act
  of Parliament, of any applicable Act of the legislature of a province
  or of any applicable regulation made under any such Act or to any
  applicable stock exchange rule.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 199
- 2018, c. 8, s. 67
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-199-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 199"){.PITLink}
:::

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-47083 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incorporation by reference

- **[[200]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-200 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  terms of a security include those stated on the security and those
  incorporated by reference to another instrument or act, Act of
  Parliament or the legislature of a province, regulation, rule or order
  to the extent that the incorporated terms do not conflict with those
  stated on the security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Purchaser without notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) applies to a good faith purchaser but
  the incorporation by reference is itself not notice of a defect to the
  purchaser even if the security expressly states that a person
  accepting it admits that notice.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 200
- 2011, c. 21, s. 93(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-200-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 200"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Validity of security

**[[201]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-201 .sectionLabel}** A security is valid in
the hands of a good faith purchaser.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Defence

**[[202]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section 205,
the fact that a security is not genuine is a complete defence even
against a good faith purchaser.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Defences

**[[203]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-203 .sectionLabel}** All other defences of
an issuer, including non-delivery and conditional delivery of a
security, are ineffective against a good faith purchaser.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed notice

- **[[204]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-204 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  purchaser is deemed to have notice of any defect in the issue of a
  security or any defence of the issuer if the security becomes stale
  within the meaning of subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Stale security

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A security becomes stale if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser takes the security more than two
    years after

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date on which performance of the principal
      obligation evidenced by the security was due, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the set date on or after which the security is
      to be presented or surrendered for redemption or exchange; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the payment of money or the delivery of securities
    is required in order to present or surrender the security, the money
    or securities are available on the day for the payment or delivery
    and the purchaser takes the security more than one year after that
    day.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unauthorized signature

- **[[205]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-205
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), an
  unauthorized signature on a security is ineffective.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limited effectiveness

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An unauthorized signature on a security is effective
  in favour of a good faith purchaser if the signature was made by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an authenticating trustee, transfer agent or other
    person entrusted by the issuer with the duty to sign the security,
    or similar securities, or to prepare them for signing; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an employee of the issuer or a person referred to
    in paragraph (a) who handles the security in the ordinary course of
    their duties.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Completion of form

**[[206]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-206 .sectionLabel}** If a security contains
the signatures necessary for its issue or transfer but is incomplete in
another respect, any person may complete it in accordance with their
authority.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Enforceability

**[[207]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-207 .sectionLabel}** A security that was
completed incorrectly is enforceable by a good faith purchaser.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fraud

**[[208]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-208 .sectionLabel}** A completed security
that was improperly altered, even if fraudulently altered, remains
enforceable but only according to its original terms.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Guarantees

- **[[209]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-209 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person signing a security as an authenticating trustee, transfer agent
  or other person entrusted by the issuer with the duty to sign the
  security guarantees to a good faith purchaser that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the security is genuine;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person's acts in connection with the security
    are within the person's authority; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person has reasonable grounds for believing
    that the security is in the form and within the amount the issuer is
    authorized to issue.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Unless agreed otherwise, a person referred to in
  subsection (1) does not assume any further liability for the validity
  of the security.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition of rights

- **[[210]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-210 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On
  delivery of a security, the purchaser of the security acquires the
  rights in it that the transferor had or had authority to convey.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Claim free

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A good faith purchaser of a security acquires it free
  from any adverse claim.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No better position

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A purchaser who was a party to a fraud or illegality
  affecting a security or who, as a prior holder, had notice of an
  adverse claim does not have a better position by taking from a later
  good faith purchaser.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation of the purchase

**[[211]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-211 .sectionLabel}** A purchaser acquires
rights only to the extent of the interest or right purchased.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 211
- 2011, c. 21, s. 94(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-211-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 211"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed notice

- **[[212]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-212 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  purchaser of a security, or a broker for a seller or purchaser, is
  deemed to have notice of an adverse claim if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the security has been endorsed "for collection" or
    "for surrender" or for some other purpose other than transfer; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the security is in bearer form and has a statement
    on it that it belongs to a person other than the transferor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Name

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The mere writing of a name on a security is not a
  statement for the purposes of paragraph (1)(b).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No duty to inquire

- **[[213]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-213 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  purchaser of a security, or a broker for a seller or purchaser, has no
  duty to inquire into the rightfulness of the transfer and, subject to
  section 214, has no notice of an adverse claim.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Third party holding

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) applies even if the purchaser or
  broker has notice that the security is held by a third person or is
  registered in the name of or endorsed by a fiduciary.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed notice

**[[214]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-214 .sectionLabel}** A purchaser or broker
who knows that the transaction is for the personal benefit of the
fiduciary or is otherwise in breach of the fiduciary's duty is deemed to
have notice of an adverse claim.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Staleness

- **[[215]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-215 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following events do not constitute notice of an adverse claim except
  if the security becomes stale within the meaning of subsection (2):

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an event that creates a right to performance of the
    principal obligation evidenced by the security; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an event that sets the date on or after which the
    security is to be presented or surrendered for redemption or
    exchange.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Staleness of security

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A security becomes stale if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser takes the security more than one year
    after

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date on which performance of the principal
      obligation evidenced by the security was due, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the date on or after which the security was to
      be presented or surrendered for redemption or exchange; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the payment of money or the delivery of securities
    is required in order to present or surrender the security, the money
    or securities are available on the day for the payment or delivery
    and the purchaser takes the security more than six months after that
    day.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Guarantee

- **[[216]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-216 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who presents a security for registration of transfer or for
  payment or exchange guarantees to the issuer that the person is
  entitled to do so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation on guarantee

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A good faith purchaser who receives a new, re-issued
  or re-registered security and who registers a transfer guarantees only
  that the purchaser has no knowledge of any unauthorized signature in a
  necessary endorsement.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of guarantee

**[[217]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-217 .sectionLabel}** A person who transfers
a security to a purchaser for value guarantees by the transfer only that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the transfer is effective and rightful;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the security is genuine and has not been materially
  altered; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person knows of nothing that might impair the
  validity of the security.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Guarantee of intermediary

**[[218]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-218 .sectionLabel}** An intermediary
delivering a security to a purchaser who knows that the intermediary is
an intermediary guarantees only good faith.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Guarantee of broker

**[[219]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-219 .sectionLabel}** A broker gives to a
customer, to the issuer and to a purchaser the guarantees provided in
sections 216 to 218 and has the rights and privileges of a purchaser
under those sections, and those guarantees of and in favour of the
broker acting as an agent or mandatary are in addition to guarantees
given by the customer and guarantees given in favour of the customer.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to compel endorsement

**[[220]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-220 .sectionLabel}** If a registered
security is delivered to a purchaser without a necessary endorsement,
the purchaser has the right to demand the endorsement. The purchaser
becomes a good faith purchaser after the endorsement.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
[[appropriate]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[221]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-221 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, section 222, subsections 229(1) and 237(1) and section
  241, [[appropriate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, with respect to a person,
  means that the person is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person who is specified by the security or by a
    special endorsement to be entitled to the security;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if a person described in paragraph (a) is described
    as a fiduciary but is no longer serving as one, either that person
    or their successor;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the security or endorsement mentioned in
    paragraph (a) specifies more than one person as fiduciaries and one
    or more of those persons is no longer a fiduciary, the remaining
    fiduciary or fiduciaries, whether or not a successor has been
    appointed or qualified;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if a person described in paragraph (a) is an
    individual and is without capacity to act by reason of death,
    minority or incapability, the person's fiduciary;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the security or endorsement mentioned in
    paragraph (a) specifies more than one person with right of
    survivorship and by reason of death not all of the persons can sign,
    the survivor or survivors;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a person who has the legal power to sign; or

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} to the extent that a person described in any of
    paragraphs (a) to (f) may act through an agent or mandatary, the
    person's authorized agent or mandatary.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time for determination

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The authority of a person signing is determined as of
  the time of signing.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 221
- 2018, c. 8, s. 68
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-221-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 221"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Endorsement

- **[[222]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-222 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  endorsement of a security in registered form for the purposes of
  assignment or transfer is made when an appropriate person signs either
  the security or a separate document, or when the signature of an
  appropriate person is written without more on the back of the
  security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Blank or special

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An endorsement may be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in blank; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} special.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Blank endorsement

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An endorsement in blank includes an endorsement to
  bearer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special endorsement

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A special endorsement specifies the person to whom
  the security is to be transferred, or who has power to transfer it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right of holder

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A holder may convert an endorsement in blank into a
  special endorsement.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Immunity of endorser

**[[223]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-223 .sectionLabel}** Unless agreed
otherwise, the endorser does not, by the endorsement, assume any
obligation that the security will be honoured by the issuer.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Partial endorsement

**[[224]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-224 .sectionLabel}** An endorsement
purporting to be an endorsement of only part of a security representing
units intended by the issuer to be separately transferable is effective
to the extent of the endorsement.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of failure by fiduciary to comply

**[[225]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-225 .sectionLabel}** Failure of a fiduciary
to comply with the instrument or act that is the source of the
fiduciary's power or with the law of the jurisdiction governing the
fiduciary relationship does not render the fiduciary's endorsement
unauthorized for the purposes of this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 225
- 2011, c. 21, s. 95(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-225-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 225"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of endorsement without delivery

**[[226]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-226 .sectionLabel}** An endorsement of a
security does not constitute a transfer until delivery of the security
on which it appears or, if the endorsement is on a separate document,
until delivery of both the security and the document.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Endorsement in bearer form

**[[227]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-227 .sectionLabel}** An endorsement of a
security in bearer form may give notice of an adverse claim under
section 212 but does not otherwise affect any of the holder's rights.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of unauthorized endorsement

- **[[228]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-228 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  owner of a security may assert the ineffectiveness of an endorsement
  against the issuer or a purchaser, other than a good faith purchaser
  who has in good faith received a new, re-issued or re-registered
  security on registration of transfer, unless the owner

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has ratified an unauthorized endorsement of the
    security; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is otherwise precluded from impugning the
    effectiveness of an unauthorized endorsement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability of issuer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An issuer who registers the transfer of a security on
  an unauthorized endorsement is liable for improper registration.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Warranties of guarantor of signature

- **[[229]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-229 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who guarantees the signature of an endorser of a security
  warrants that, at the time of signing, the signer was an appropriate
  person to endorse and the signature was genuine.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation of liability

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person who guarantees the signature of an endorser
  does not otherwise warrant the rightfulness of the transfer to which
  the signature relates.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Warranties of guarantor of endorsement

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person who guarantees the endorsement of a security
  warrants both the signature and the rightfulness, in all respects, of
  the transfer to which the signature relates, but an issuer may not
  require a guarantee of endorsement as a condition to registration of
  transfer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extent of liability

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The guarantees referred to in subsections (1) to (3)
  are made to any person who, relying on the guarantee, takes or deals
  with the security, and the guarantor is liable to the person for any
  loss resulting from breach of warranty.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presumption of delivery

**[[230]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-230 .sectionLabel}** Delivery of a security
to a purchaser occurs when

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser or a person designated by the purchaser
  acquires possession of it;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser's securities broker acquires possession
  of a security specially endorsed to or issued in the name of the
  purchaser;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser's securities broker sends the purchaser
  confirmation of the purchase and the broker in the broker's records
  identifies a specific security as belonging to the purchaser; or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} in respect of an identified security to be delivered
  while still in the possession of a third person, that person
  acknowledges that it is held for the purchaser.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presumption of ownership

- **[[231]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-231 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  purchaser is the owner of a security held for the purchaser by a
  securities broker, but a purchaser is not a holder except in the cases
  described in paragraphs 230(b) and (c).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ownership of part of fungible bulk

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a security is part of a fungible bulk, a purchaser
  of the security is the owner of the proportionate interest or right in
  the fungible bulk.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to securities broker of adverse
  claim

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Notice of an adverse claim received by a securities
  broker or by a purchaser after the broker takes delivery as a holder
  for value is not effective against the broker or the purchaser, except
  that, as between the broker and the purchaser, the purchaser may
  demand delivery of an equivalent security in respect of which no
  notice of an adverse claim has been received.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 231
- 2011, c. 21, s. 96
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-231-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 231"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delivery of security

- **[[232]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-232
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless agreed otherwise, if a sale
  of a security is made on a stock exchange or otherwise through
  securities brokers,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the selling customer fulfils their duty to deliver
