  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No amendment to the articles affects an existing
  cause of action or claim or liability to prosecution in favour of or
  against the cooperative or its directors or officers, or any civil,
  criminal, administrative, investigative or other action or proceeding
  to which a cooperative or its directors or officers are a party.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restated articles

- **[[294]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-294 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  directors may at any time, and must when reasonably so directed by the
  Director, restate the articles of incorporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sent to Director

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Restated articles of incorporation in the form that
  the Director fixes must be sent to the Director.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restated certificate

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of restated articles of incorporation, the
  Director must issue a restated certificate of incorporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effective date

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Restated articles of incorporation are effective on
  the date shown in the restated certificate of incorporation.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 294
- 2001, c. 14, s. 207(E)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amalgamation

**[[295]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-295 .sectionLabel}** Two or more
cooperatives, including holding and subsidiary cooperatives, may
amalgamate and continue as one cooperative, provided that the
amalgamation agreement sets out a capital and corporate structure for
the amalgamated cooperative that is one that would meet the requirements
for a cooperative to be incorporated under this Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amalgamation agreement

- **[[296]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-296 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Each
  cooperative proposing to amalgamate must enter into an agreement
  setting out the terms and means of effecting the amalgamation and, in
  particular, setting out

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the provisions that are required to be included in
    articles of incorporation under section 11;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of each proposed director of
    the amalgamated cooperative;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the manner in which the shares of each amalgamating
    cooperative are to be converted into membership shares of the
    amalgamated cooperative and, if applicable, investment shares or
    other securities of the amalgamated cooperative;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if any share of an amalgamating cooperative is not
    to be converted into shares or securities of the amalgamated
    cooperative, the amount of money or securities of a cooperative that
    the holders of those shares are to receive in addition to or instead
    of shares or securities of the amalgamated cooperative;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the manner of payment of money instead of the issue
    of fractional shares of the amalgamated cooperative or of any other
    body corporate the securities of which are to be received in the
    amalgamation;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} whether the by-laws of the amalgamated cooperative
    are to be those of one of the amalgamating cooperatives, and if not,
    a copy of the proposed by-laws; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} details of any arrangements necessary to perfect
    the amalgamation and to provide for the subsequent management and
    operation of the amalgamated cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If shares of one of the amalgamating cooperatives are
  held by or on behalf of another of the amalgamating cooperatives, the
  amalgamation agreement must provide for the cancellation of the shares
  when the amalgamation becomes effective without any repayment of
  capital in respect of them, and no provision may be made in the
  agreement for the conversion of those shares into shares of the
  amalgamated cooperative.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval

- **[[297]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-297 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  directors of each amalgamating cooperative must submit the
  amalgamation agreement for approval to a meeting of the members of
  each amalgamating cooperative and to a meeting of the shareholders of
  each amalgamating cooperative and, subject to subsection (5), to the
  holders of each class or series of those investment shares.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of meeting

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A notice of a meeting of members or shareholders
  complying with section 52 must be sent in accordance with that section
  to each member and shareholder of each amalgamating cooperative and
  must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} include or be accompanied by a copy or summary of
    the amalgamation agreement; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} state that a dissenting member or shareholder is
    entitled to the benefit of section 302.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Failure to make statement

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Failure to make the statement referred to in
  paragraph (2)(b) does not invalidate an amalgamation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to vote

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Each investment share carries the right to vote with
  respect to an amalgamation agreement, whether or not it otherwise
  carries the right to vote.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Class vote

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The holders of investment shares of a class or series
  are entitled to vote separately as a class or series in respect of the
  amalgamation agreement if it contains a provision that, if contained
  in a proposed amendment to the articles, would entitle them to vote as
  a class or series under subsection 290(4).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (5), an amalgamation agreement
  is adopted when the members of each amalgamating cooperative and, if
  any of the amalgamating cooperatives has issued investment shares,
  their shareholders, have approved the amalgamation agreement by
  separate special resolutions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Termination

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} An amalgamation agreement may provide that at any
  time before the issue of a certificate of amalgamation the agreement
  may be terminated by the directors of an amalgamating cooperative,
  even if the agreement has been approved by the members and
  shareholders of all or any of the amalgamating cooperatives.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vertical short-form amalgamation

- **[[298]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-298 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  cooperative that is a holding cooperative may amalgamate with one or
  more of its wholly owned subsidiary cooperatives. The cooperative and
  subsidiaries continue as one cooperative without complying with
  sections 295 to 297 if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amalgamation is approved by a resolution of the
    directors of each amalgamating cooperative; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the resolutions provide that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the shares of each subsidiary be cancelled
      without any repayment of capital in respect of them,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} except as may be prescribed, the articles of
      amalgamation be the same as the articles of the amalgamating
      holding cooperative, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} no shares or securities may be issued by the
      amalgamated cooperative in connection with the amalgamation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Horizontal short-form amalgamation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Two or more wholly owned subsidiary cooperatives of a
  holding entity may amalgamate and continue as one cooperative without
  complying with sections 295 to 297 if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amalgamation is approved by a resolution of the
    directors of each amalgamating cooperative; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the resolutions provide that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the shares of all but one of the amalgamating
      subsidiaries be cancelled without any repayment of capital in
      respect of them,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} except as may be prescribed, the articles of
      amalgamation be the same as the articles of the amalgamating
      subsidiary cooperative whose shares are not cancelled, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the stated capital of the amalgamating
      subsidiaries whose shares are cancelled are added to the stated
      capital of the amalgamating subsidiary whose shares are not
      cancelled.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Change of name

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subparagraph (2)(b)(ii) and section 289, the
  directors of the holding entity of the subsidiaries referred to in
  subsection (2) may, by resolution, approve a new name for the
  amalgamated cooperative, so long as, in a case to which Part 20 or 21
  applies, the name would comply with the provisions of the applicable
  Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 298
- 2001, c. 14, s. 208
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sending of articles

- **[[299]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-299
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} After an amalgamation has been
  approved under section 297 or 298, articles of amalgamation in the
  form that the Director fixes must be sent to the Director, together
  with a notice of registered office and a notice of the directors of
  the amalgamated cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Attached statutory declarations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A statutory declaration of a director or officer of
  each amalgamating cooperative must be attached to the articles of
  amalgamation and must establish to the satisfaction of the Director

  - [(a) to (c)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2018, c. 8, s. 76\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} that there are reasonable grounds to believe that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} each amalgamating cooperative is, and the
      amalgamated cooperative will be, able to pay its liabilities as
      they become due, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the realizable value of the amalgamated
      cooperative's assets will not be less than the total of its
      liabilities and stated capital of all classes; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} that there are reasonable grounds to believe that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} no creditor will be prejudiced by the
      amalgamation, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} adequate notice has been given to all known
      creditors of the amalgamating cooperatives and no creditor objects
      to the amalgamation otherwise than on grounds that are frivolous
      or vexatious.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adequate notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subparagraph (2)(e)(ii), adequate
  notice is given if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a notice in writing is sent to each known creditor
    who has a claim against any of the amalgamating cooperatives that
    exceeds one thousand dollars;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a notice in writing is published once in a
    newspaper published or distributed in the place where each
    amalgamating cooperative has its registered office and reasonable
    notice is given in each province where the cooperative carries on
    business; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} each notice states that the cooperative intends to
    amalgamate with one or more specified cooperatives in accordance
    with this Act and that a creditor of the cooperative may object to
    the amalgamation not later than thirty days after the date of the
    notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of amalgamation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of articles of amalgamation and the
  declarations required by subsection (2), the Director must issue a
  certificate of amalgamation if the Director is satisfied that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the articles are in accordance with section 11 and,
    if applicable, section 353 and subsection 359(2);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the cooperative will be organized and operated and
    will carry on business on a cooperative basis;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the things described in paragraphs (2)(d) and (e)
    are true; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, Part 20 or 21 has been complied
    with.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reliance on certificate

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraphs (4)(b) to (d), the
  Director may rely on the articles and the declarations required by
  subsection (2).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 299
- 2018, c. 8, s. 76
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-299-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 299"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of certificate

**[[300]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-300 .sectionLabel}** On the date shown in a
certificate of amalgamation,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amalgamation of the amalgamating cooperatives and
  their continuance as one cooperative becomes effective;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the property of each amalgamating cooperative
  continues to be the property of the amalgamated cooperative;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the amalgamated cooperative continues to be liable
  for the obligations of each amalgamating cooperative;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} an existing cause of action, claim or liability to
  prosecute is unaffected;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} a civil, criminal, administrative, investigative or
  other action or proceeding pending by or against an amalgamating
  cooperative may be continued to be prosecuted by or against the
  amalgamated cooperative;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} a conviction against, or ruling, order or judgment in
  favour of or against, an amalgamating cooperative may be enforced by
  or against the amalgamated cooperative; and

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} the articles of amalgamation are deemed to be the
  articles of incorporation of the amalgamated cooperative and the
  certificate of amalgamation is deemed to be the certificate of
  incorporation of the amalgamated cooperative.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extraordinary disposition

- **[[301]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-301 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  sale, lease or exchange of all or substantially all of the property of
  a cooperative, other than in the ordinary course of business, requires
  the approval of the members and shareholders in accordance with
  subsections (2) to (7).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A notice of meeting complying with section 52 must be
  sent to all members and shareholders and must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} include a copy or summary of the proposed agreement
    of sale, lease or exchange; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} state that a dissenting member or shareholder is
    entitled to bring section 302 into effect.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Failure to make statement

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Failure to make the statement referred to in
  paragraph (2)(b) does not invalidate the disposition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to vote

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Each investment share carries the right to vote with
  respect to an extraordinary disposition, whether or not it otherwise
  carries the right to vote.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Class vote

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The holders of investment shares of a class or series
  are entitled to vote separately as a class or series if the class or
  series is affected differently from another class or series by the
  proposed disposition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (5), a disposition is
  authorized when approved by a special resolution of the members and,
  if the cooperative has issued investment shares, by a separate special
  resolution of the shareholders of each class or series. The special
  resolution may authorize the directors to fix any terms or conditions
  of a sale, lease or exchange.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Termination

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The directors, if authorized by the members and
  shareholders approving a proposed disposition, and subject to the
  rights of third parties, may abandon the disposition without further
  approval.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to dissent

- **[[302]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-302
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless section 303 or 340 applies, a
  member or shareholder may dissent if a cooperative resolves to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} amend its articles in a manner that adversely
    affects a member's membership rights or that affects the rights of a
    shareholder in respect of an investment share;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} amend its articles to add, change or remove a
    restriction on the business the cooperative may carry on;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} amalgamate other than under section 298;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} apply for continuance under section 286 or 287; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} sell, lease or exchange all or substantially all of
    its property under section 301.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Further right

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of investment shares of any class or series
  of shares entitled to vote under section 134 may dissent if the
  cooperative resolves to amend its articles in a manner described in
  that section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dissent

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A dissenting member or shareholder must send to the
  cooperative, at or before any meeting of members or meeting of
  shareholders at which a resolution referred to in subsection (1)
  or (2) is to be voted on, a written objection to the resolution,
  unless the cooperative did not give notice to the member or
  shareholder of the purpose of the meeting and of the right to dissent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of dissent

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A dissenting member is deemed to have given notice of
  intent to withdraw from the cooperative under this section if the
  resolution is passed. A dissenting shareholder is deemed to have
  claimed under this section on behalf of all investment shares in a
  class held by the shareholder if the resolution is passed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of resolution

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The cooperative must, not later than ten days after
  the members and shareholders have adopted the resolution, send to each
  dissenting member and shareholder notice that the resolution has been
  adopted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A dissenting member or shareholder may, no later than
  twenty-one days after receiving the notice under subsection (5), or if
  no notice is received, no later than twenty-one days after learning
  that the resolution was adopted, send to the cooperative a written
  notice that contains

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person's name and address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the person is a shareholder, the number of
    investment shares and the class or classes of the shares held; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a demand

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a dissenting member, for
      withdrawal from the cooperative, for payment of all membership
      shares at their fair value and for repayment of any other interest
      or right held by the member in the cooperative, fair value being
      determined on the day before the resolution was adopted, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a dissenting shareholder, for
      payment of the fair market value of all investment shares of each
      class held by the shareholder, fair market value being determined
      on the day before the resolution was adopted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rights of members

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Despite the articles, the by-laws or section 39, a
  dissenting member who has sent a demand under subsection (6) does not
  have the right to vote at a meeting of the cooperative after having
  sent the notice. Despite the articles, the by-laws or section 146, the
  member is entitled to be paid the value of their membership shares in
  the cooperative in accordance with this section or a court order under
  subsection 304(4).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Share certificates

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} A dissenting shareholder must, not later than thirty
  days after sending the notice under subsection (6), send the
  certificates representing the investment shares held in the
  cooperative to the cooperative or to its transfer agent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Forfeiture

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} A dissenting shareholder who fails to comply with
  subsection (8) has no right to claim under this section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Endorsing certificate

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} Each certificate sent under subsection (8) must be
  endorsed by the cooperative or its transfer agent with a notice that
  the holder is a dissenting shareholder and must be returned to the
  shareholder.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of rights

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} On sending a notice under subsection (6), a member's
  rights as a member, and a shareholder's rights as a shareholder, other
  than the right to be paid in accordance with subsection (6), are
  suspended.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reinstatement

  [(12)]{.lawlabel} The rights of the member or shareholder are
  reinstated as of the date of the notice referred to in subsection (6)
  if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the dissenting member or shareholder withdraws the
    demand made under paragraph (6)(c) before the cooperative makes an
    offer under subsection (13);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the cooperative fails to make an offer in
    accordance with subsection (13) and the dissenting member or
    shareholder withdraws their notice; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the directors revoke a resolution to amend the
    articles under subsection 289(2), terminate an amalgamation
    agreement under subsection 297(7) or an application for continuance
    under subsection 286(3) or 287(3), or abandon a sale, lease or
    exchange under subsection 301(7).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offer to pay

  [(13)]{.lawlabel} A cooperative must, not later than seven days after
  the later of the day on which the resolution under subsection (1)
  or (2) is effective or the day the cooperative receives the notice
  under subsection (6), send to each dissenting member or shareholder

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a written offer to pay the amount determined in
    accordance with subsection (6) and a statement showing how the
    amount was calculated; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a statement that subsection (23) or (24) applies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Same terms

  [(14)]{.lawlabel} Every offer for membership shares must be on the
  same terms and every offer for the same class or series of investment
  shares must be on the same terms.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment

  [(15)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (23) or (24), a cooperative
  must pay to the dissenting member or shareholder the amount offered
  under subsection (13) no later than ten days after acceptance, but the
  offer lapses if it is not accepted within thirty days after being
  made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to court

  [(16)]{.lawlabel} If the dissenting member or shareholder fails to
  accept the offer, the cooperative may, not later than fifty days after
  the resolution is approved or any later time that the court may allow,
  apply to the court to fix the amount to be paid under subsection (6).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If no application

  [(17)]{.lawlabel} If the cooperative fails to make an application
  under subsection (16) --- or fails to make an offer under
  subsection (13) within the time set out in subsection (16) --- a
  dissenting member or shareholder may, no later than twenty days after
  the end of that period, make an application for the same purpose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Venue

  [(18)]{.lawlabel} An application under subsection (16) or (17) may be
  made to the court having jurisdiction where the registered office of
  the cooperative is located or to a court having jurisdiction where the
  dissenting member or shareholder resides if the cooperative carries on
  business in that jurisdiction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Parties

  [(19)]{.lawlabel} On an application under subsection (16) or (17), all
  dissenting members and shareholders whose shares or other interests or
  rights have not been purchased are joined as parties and the
  cooperative must notify them, advising each of them of the right to
  participate in, and the consequences of, the application. No
  dissenting member or shareholder is required to give security for
  costs in the application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of court

  [(20)]{.lawlabel} On an application under subsection (16) or (17), the
  court must determine who is a dissenting member or shareholder and fix
  the amount to be paid under subsection (6) and may make any further
  order that the court thinks fit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice if subsection (23) or (24)
  applies

  [(21)]{.lawlabel} If subsection (23) or (24) applies, the cooperative
  must, no later than ten days after the determination under subsection
  (20), advise each dissenting member and shareholder that
  subsection (23) or (24) applies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect if subsection (23) or (24)
  applies

  [(22)]{.lawlabel} If subsection (23) or (24) applies,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a dissenting member or shareholder, not later than
    thirty days after the notice under subsection (21), may by notice to
    the cooperative withdraw the notice of demand, in which case the
    member is reinstated as a member or the shareholder is reinstated as
    a shareholder; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if no notice is given to the cooperative under
    paragraph (a), the dissenting member or shareholder retains the
    status of a claimant to be paid as soon as the cooperative may
    lawfully do so or, in liquidation, to be paid in priority to the
    remaining members and shareholders.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(23)]{.lawlabel} The cooperative may not make a payment to a
  dissenting member or shareholder under this section if there are
  reasonable grounds to believe that after that payment the cooperative
  would be in breach of section 149.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Installments

  [(24)]{.lawlabel} If the directors determine that the payment to a
  dissenting member as member would adversely affect the financial
  well-being of the cooperative, payment may be made at the times that
  the directors determine, over a period that begins on the day on which
  the resolution was adopted and ends not later than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} five years after that day; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any other day that is not more than ten years after
    the day on which the resolution was adopted and that is specified in
    the articles of the cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest

  [(25)]{.lawlabel} Payments under subsection (24) shall bear interest
  at the rates prescribed by or calculated in accordance with the
  regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 302
- 2011, c. 21, s. 107
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-302-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 302"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reorganization

- **[[303]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-303 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This
  section applies to a reorganization made pursuant to a court order
  under section 340, a court order approving a proposal under the
  [Bankruptcy and Insolvency Act](/eng/acts/B-3) or a court order that
  affects the rights among the cooperative, its members, shareholders
  and creditors made under any other Act of Parliament.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No court order for reorganization may result in a
  cooperative

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} no longer being organized or operating or carrying
    on business on a cooperative basis;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the cooperative is one to which Part 20 applies,
    not complying with Part 20; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the cooperative is one to which Part 21 applies,
    not complying with Part 21.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of court

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a cooperative is subject to an order referred to
  in subsection (1), its articles may be amended by the order to effect
  any changes that might lawfully be made by an amendment under this
  Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Further powers

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a court makes an order referred to in subsection
  (1), the court may also

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} authorize the issue of debt obligations that if
    held by members may be converted to membership shares or investment
    shares and otherwise may be converted to investment shares and fix
    the terms of them; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} appoint directors in place of or in addition to all
    or any of the directors then in office.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Articles of reorganization

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} After an order referred to in subsection (1) has been
  made, articles of reorganization in the form that the Director fixes,
  together with, if applicable, notice of registered office and notice
  of change of directors, must be sent to the Director.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of amendment

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of articles of reorganization, the
  Director must issue a certificate of amendment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of certificate

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} A reorganization becomes effective on the date shown
  on the certificate of amendment and the articles of incorporation are
  amended accordingly.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No dissent

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} No member or shareholder is entitled to dissent under
  section 302 if an amendment to the articles of incorporation is
  effected under this section.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
[[arrangement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[304]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-304 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, [[arrangement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a continuance for the purpose of amalgamation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an amendment to the articles of a cooperative;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an amalgamation of two or more cooperatives;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an amalgamation of a body corporate with a
    cooperative that results in an amalgamated cooperative;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} an amalgamation of two bodies corporate to become a
    cooperative;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a division of the business carried on by a
    cooperative;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a transfer of all or substantially all of the
    property of a cooperative to another body corporate in exchange for
    property, money or securities of the body corporate;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} an exchange of securities of a cooperative for
    property, money or other securities of the cooperative or property,
    money or securities of another body corporate, subject to the issue
    or transfer of membership shares to members;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a liquidation and dissolution of a cooperative; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} any combination of the events set out in
    paragraphs (a) to (i).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When cooperative insolvent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, a cooperative is
  insolvent

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} when it is unable to pay its liabilities as they
    become due; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the realizable value of its assets is less than
    the total of its liabilities and the stated capital of all
    membership shares and investment shares.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to court for approval of
  arrangement

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If it is not feasible for a cooperative that is not
  insolvent to effect a fundamental change in the nature of an
  arrangement under any other provision of this Act, the cooperative may
  apply to a court for an order approving an arrangement proposed by the
  cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of court

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In connection with an application under this section,
  the court may make any order it thinks fit, including an order

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the giving of notice of the application,
    subject to subsection (6);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting the representation of the interests of
    members or shareholders;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} requiring meetings of the cooperative to be held;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} permitting a member or shareholder to dissent under
    section 302; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} approving an arrangement in any manner the court
    may direct.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The court may not make an order that would result in
  the cooperative

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} no longer being organized or operating or carrying
    on business on a cooperative basis;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the cooperative is one to which Part 20 applies,
    not complying with Part 20; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the cooperative is one to which Part 21 applies,
    not complying with Part 21.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to Director

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Notice of an application under subsection (4) must be
  given to the Director and the Director is entitled to appear and be
  heard in person or by counsel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Articles of arrangement

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If an order is made under paragraph (4)(e), articles
  of arrangement in the form that the Director fixes, together with, if
  applicable, notice of registered office and notice of change of
  directors, must be sent to the Director.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of arrangement

- **[[305]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-305 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On
  receipt of articles of arrangement, the Director must issue a
  certificate of arrangement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of certificate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An arrangement becomes effective on the date shown in
  the certificate of arrangement.

## [PART 17]{.HLabel1}[Liquidation and Dissolution]{.HTitleText1} {#h-48067 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
[[court]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

**[[306]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-306 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,
[[court]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a court having jurisdiction in the
place where the cooperative has its registered office.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of Part

- **[[307]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-307 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This
  Part, other than sections 308 and 311, does not apply to a cooperative
  that is an [[insolvent person]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} or a
  [[bankrupt]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as those terms are defined in
  section 2 of the [Bankruptcy and Insolvency Act](/eng/acts/B-3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Staying of proceedings

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any proceedings taken under this Part to dissolve or
  to liquidate and dissolve a cooperative are stayed if the cooperative
  is at any time found, in a proceeding under the [Bankruptcy and
  Insolvency Act](/eng/acts/B-3), to be an [[insolvent
  person]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in section 2 of that Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 307
- 2001, c. 14, s. 209
- 2018, c. 8, s. 77
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-307-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 307"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revival

- **[[308]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-308 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  a cooperative is dissolved under this Part, any interested person, or
  any person who would be an interested person if a certificate of
  revival were issued under this section, may apply to the Director to
  have the dissolved cooperative revived as a cooperative under this
  Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Articles of revival

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Articles of revival in the form that the Director
  fixes must be sent to the Director.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of revival

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of articles of revival, the Director must
  issue a certificate of revival, unless the Director is of the opinion
  that issuing the certificate

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} would result in the dissolved cooperative

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} no longer being organized or operating or
      carrying on business on a cooperative basis,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the cooperative is one to which Part 20
      applies, not complying with Part 20, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if the cooperative is one to which Part 21
      applies, not complying with Part 21; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} would not be advisable for any other valid reason.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reliance on articles

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection (3), the Director may
  rely on the articles of revival.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date of revival

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A dissolved cooperative is revived as a cooperative
  under this Act on the date shown on the certificate of revival.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rights preserved

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} In the same manner and to the same extent as if it
  had not been dissolved, but subject to any reasonable terms that may
  be imposed by the Director, to the rights acquired by any person after
  its dissolution and to any changes to the internal affairs of the
  cooperative after its dissolution, the revived cooperative is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} restored to its previous position in law, including
    the restoration of any rights and privileges whether arising before
    its dissolution or after its dissolution and before its revival; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} liable for the obligations that it would have had
    if it had not been dissolved whether they arise before its
    dissolution or after its dissolution and before its revival.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Legal actions

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Any legal action respecting the affairs of a revived
  cooperative taken between the time of its dissolution and its revival
  is valid and effective.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[interested
  person]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[interested
  person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a member, a shareholder, a director, an officer, an
    employee and a creditor of the dissolved cooperative;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person who has a contract --- other than, in
    Quebec, a contract by gratuitous title --- with the dissolved
    cooperative; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a trustee in bankruptcy or liquidator for the
    dissolved cooperative.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 308
- 2001, c. 14, s. 210
- 2018, c. 8, s. 78
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-308-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 308"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dissolution if no property and no
liability

- **[[309]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-309 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  cooperative that has no property and no liabilities may be dissolved
  by a special resolution of the members and, if the cooperative has
  issued investment shares, by a separate special resolution of the
  shareholders of each class, whether or not they are otherwise entitled
  to vote.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dissolution if property disposed of

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A cooperative that has property or liabilities, or
  both, may be dissolved by a special resolution of the members and, if
  the cooperative has issued investment shares, by a separate special
  resolution of the shareholders of each class, whether or not they are
  otherwise entitled to vote, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} by the special resolution or resolutions they
    authorize the directors to cause the cooperative to distribute
    property and discharge liabilities; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the cooperative has distributed property and
    discharged liabilities before it sends articles of dissolution to
    the Director under subsection (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Articles of dissolution

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Articles of dissolution in the form that the Director
  fixes must be sent to the Director.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of dissolution

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of articles of dissolution, the Director
  must issue a certificate of dissolution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of certificate

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The cooperative ceases to exist on the date shown in
  the certificate of dissolution.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proposing liquidation and dissolution

- **[[310]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-310 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  directors may propose, or a member may, in accordance with section 58,
  make a proposal for, the voluntary liquidation and dissolution of a
  cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of meeting

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notice of any meeting of the cooperative at which
  voluntary liquidation and dissolution is to be proposed must set out
  the terms of the proposal.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A cooperative may liquidate and dissolve by a special
  resolution of the members and, if the cooperative has issued
  investment shares, by a separate special resolution of the
  shareholders of each class, whether or not they are otherwise entitled
  to vote.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statement of intent to dissolve

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A statement of intent to dissolve in the form that
  the Director fixes must be sent to the Director.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of intent to dissolve

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of a statement of intent to dissolve, the
  Director must issue a certificate of intent to dissolve.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of certificate

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} On the issue of a certificate of intent to dissolve,
  the cooperative must cease to carry on business except to the extent
  necessary for the liquidation, but its corporate existence continues
  until the Director issues a certificate of dissolution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liquidation

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} After the issue of a certificate of intent to
  dissolve, the cooperative must, without delay,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} cause a notice to be sent to each known creditor of
    the cooperative;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} proceed to collect its property, dispose of
    properties that are not to be distributed in kind to its members or
    shareholders, discharge all its obligations and do all other acts
    required to liquidate its business; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} after giving the notice required under
    paragraph (a) and adequately providing for the payment or discharge
    of all its obligations, but subject to the articles and Parts 20 and
    21, distribute its remaining property among its members and
    shareholders, if any, according to their respective rights.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Supervision by court

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} Any interested person may, at any time during the
  liquidation of a cooperative, apply to a court for an order that the
  liquidation be continued under the supervision of the court as
  provided in this Part, and on the application the court may so order
  and make any further order it thinks fit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to Director

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} An applicant under this section must give the
  Director notice of the application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} At any time after issue of a certificate of intent
  to dissolve and before issue of a certificate of dissolution, a
  certificate of intent to dissolve may be revoked by sending the
  Director a statement of revocation of intent to dissolve in the form
  that the Director fixes, if the revocation is approved in the same
  manner as the resolution under subsection (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of revocation of intent to
  dissolve

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of a statement of revocation of intent to
  dissolve, the Director must issue a certificate of revocation of
  intent to dissolve.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of certificate

  [(12)]{.lawlabel} On the date shown in the certificate of revocation
  of intent to dissolve, the revocation is effective and the cooperative
  may continue to carry on its business or businesses.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Articles of dissolution

  [(13)]{.lawlabel} If a certificate of intent to dissolve has not been
  revoked and the cooperative has complied with subsection (7), articles
  of dissolution in the form that the Director fixes must be sent to the
  Director.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of dissolution

  [(14)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of articles of dissolution, the Director
  must issue a certificate of dissolution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of certificate

  [(15)]{.lawlabel} The cooperative ceases to exist on the date shown in
  the certificate of dissolution.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dissolution by Director

- **[[311]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-311
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and (3),
  the Director may dissolve a cooperative by issuing a certificate of
  dissolution under this section if the cooperative

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has not commenced business within three years after
    the date shown in its certificate of incorporation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has not carried on its business for three
    consecutive years;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is in default for a period of one year in sending
    the Director any fee, notice or document required by this Act; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} does not have any directors or is in the situation
    described in subsection 85(6).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Director may not dissolve a cooperative under
  this section until

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} one hundred and twenty days have elapsed since
    notice of intent to dissolve has been given to the cooperative and
    to each of its directors; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} notice of intent to dissolve the cooperative has
    been published in a publication generally available to the public.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of dissolution

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Unless cause to the contrary has been shown or an
  order has been made by a court under section 315, the Director may,
  after the end of the one hundred and twenty days referred to in
  subsection (2), issue a certificate of dissolution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- non-payment of
  incorporation fee

  [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite anything in this section, the Director may
  dissolve a cooperative by issuing a certificate of dissolution if the
  fee for the issuance of a certificate of incorporation is not paid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of certificate

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The cooperative ceases to exist on the date shown in
  the certificate of dissolution.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 311
- 2001, c. 14, s. 211
- 2018, c. 8, s. 79(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-311-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 311"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Grounds for dissolution

- **[[312]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-312 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  interested person may apply to a court for an order dissolving a
  cooperative if the cooperative has

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} failed for two or more consecutive years to comply
    with the requirements of this Act with respect to the holding of
    annual meetings;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} contravened section 18, subsection 27(2) or section
    31, 247 or 249; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} procured any certificate under this Act by
    misrepresentation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to director

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant under this section must give the
  Director notice of the application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dissolution order

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On an application under this section, the court may
  order that the cooperative be dissolved or that it be liquidated and
  dissolved under the supervision of the court, and the court may make
  any other order it thinks fit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of an order under this section or section
  313, the Director must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the order is to dissolve the cooperative, issue
    a certificate of dissolution in the form that the Director fixes; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the order is to liquidate and dissolve the
    cooperative under the supervision of the court, issue a certificate
    of intent to dissolve in the form that the Director fixes and
    publish notice of the order in a publication generally available to
    the public.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of certificate

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The cooperative ceases to exist on the date shown in
  the certificate of dissolution.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 312
- 2001, c. 14, s. 212
- 2018, c. 8, s. 80(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-312-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 312"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Further grounds

- **[[313]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-313 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  court may order the liquidation and dissolution of a cooperative or
  any of its affiliates on the application of a member or a shareholder
  if the court is satisfied

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that the cooperative no longer carries on business
    or is no longer organized or operating on a cooperative basis;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that an act or omission of the cooperative or any
    of its affiliates effects a result, that the business or affairs of
    the cooperative or any of its affiliates are or have been carried on
    or conducted in a manner, or that the powers of the directors of the
    cooperative or any of its affiliates are or have been exercised in a
    manner, that is oppressive or unfairly prejudicial to, or that
    unfairly disregards the interests of, a member, shareholder,
    security holder, creditor, director or officer; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that events have occurred that entitle a member or
    shareholder, in accordance with a unanimous agreement, to demand
    that the cooperative be dissolved or that it is just and equitable
    that the cooperative be liquidated and dissolved.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternate order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On an application under this section, a court may
  make any order under this section or section 340 that it thinks fit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of section 341

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Section 341 applies to an application under this
  section.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 313
- 2001, c. 14, s. 213(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for supervision

- **[[314]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-314 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application to a court to supervise a voluntary liquidation and
  dissolution under subsection 310(8) must state the reasons, verified
  by an affidavit of the applicant, why the court should supervise the
  liquidation and dissolution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Court supervision

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a court makes an order applied for under
  subsection 310(8), the liquidation and dissolution of the cooperative
  continues under the supervision of the court in accordance with this
  Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to court

- **[[315]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application to a court under subsection 313(1) must state the reasons,
  verified by an affidavit of the applicant, why the cooperative should
  be liquidated and dissolved.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Show cause order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On an application under subsection 313(1), the court
  may make an order requiring the cooperative and any person who has an
  interest in it or claim against it to show cause, at a specified time
  and place, not less than four weeks after the date of the order, why
  the cooperative should not be liquidated and dissolved.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of court

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On an application under subsection 313(1), the court
  may order the directors and officers of the cooperative to provide the
  court with all material information known to or reasonably
  ascertainable by them, including

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} financial statements of the cooperative;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of each member and
    shareholder; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of each known creditor or
    claimant, including any creditor or claimant with unliquidated,
    future or contingent claims, and any person with whom the
    cooperative has a contract.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A copy of an order made under subsection (2) must be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} published as directed in the order, at least once
    in each week before the time appointed for the hearing, in a
    publication generally available to the public; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} served on the Director and each person named in the
    order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Person responsible

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Publication and service of an order under this
  section must be effected by the cooperative or by any other person and
  in any manner that the court may order.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 315
- 2018, c. 8, s. 81(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-315-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 315"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of court

**[[316]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-316 .sectionLabel}** In connection with the
liquidation and dissolution of a cooperative, the court may, if it is
satisfied that the cooperative is able to pay or adequately provide for
the discharge of all its obligations, make any order it thinks fit,
including an order

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} to liquidate;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} appointing a liquidator, with or without security,
  and fixing their remuneration, or replacing a liquidator;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} appointing inspectors or referees, specifying their
  powers and fixing their remuneration, or replacing inspectors or
  referees;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} determining the notice to be given to any interested
  person, or dispensing with notice to any person;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} determining the validity of any claim made against
  the cooperative;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} at any stage of the proceedings, restraining the
  directors and officers from

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} exercising any of their powers, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} collecting or receiving any debt or other property
    of the cooperative, and from paying out or transferring any property
    of the cooperative, except as permitted by the court;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} determining and enforcing the duty or liability of
  any present or former director, officer, member or shareholder

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to the cooperative, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for an obligation of the cooperative;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} approving the payment, satisfaction or compromise of
  claims against the cooperative and the retention of assets for those
  purposes, and determining the adequacy of provisions for the payment
  or discharge of obligations of the cooperative, whether liquidated,
  unliquidated, future or contingent;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} disposing of or destroying documents and records of
  the cooperative;

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} on the application of a creditor, the inspectors or
  the liquidator, giving directions on any matter arising on the
  liquidation;

- [(k)]{.lawlabel} after notice has been given to all interested
  parties, relieving a liquidator from an omission or default on any
  terms that the court thinks fit and confirming any act of the
  liquidator;

- [(l)]{.lawlabel} subject to section 322, approving any proposed
  interim or final distribution to members or shareholders in money or
  in property in accordance with their respective rights;

- [(m)]{.lawlabel} disposing of any property that belongs to creditors,
  members or shareholders who cannot be found;

- [(n)]{.lawlabel} on the application of a director, officer, member,
  shareholder or creditor or the liquidator,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} staying the liquidation on any terms and conditions
    that the court thinks fit,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} continuing or discontinuing the liquidation
    proceedings, or

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} to the liquidator, to restore to the cooperative
    all its remaining property; and

- [(o)]{.lawlabel} after the liquidator has rendered a final account to
  the court, dissolving the cooperative.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 316
- 2011, c. 21, s. 108(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-316-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 316"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of order

**[[317]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-317 .sectionLabel}** The liquidation of a
cooperative commences when a court makes an order for liquidation.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cessation of business and powers

- **[[318]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-318 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  court makes an order for liquidation of a cooperative,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cooperative continues in existence but must
    cease to carry on business, except the business that is in the
    liquidator's opinion required for an orderly liquidation; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the powers of the directors, members and
    shareholders cease and vest in the liquidator, except as
    specifically authorized by the court.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegation by liquidator

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The liquidator may delegate any of the powers vested
  in him or her by paragraph (1)(b) to the directors or members.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appointment of liquidator

- **[[319]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-319 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  making an order for the liquidation of a cooperative or at any time
  after making one, the court may appoint any person, including a
  director, officer, member or shareholder, or any other body corporate,
  as liquidator of the cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vacancy

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an order for the liquidation of a cooperative has
  been made and the office of liquidator is or becomes vacant, the
  property of the cooperative is under the control of the court until
  the office of liquidator is filled.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duties of liquidator

**[[320]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-320 .sectionLabel}** A liquidator must,
without delay after being appointed,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} give notice of appointment to each claimant and
  creditor known to the liquidator;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} publish notice in a publication generally available
  to the public and take reasonable steps to give notice of the
  appointment in each province or other jurisdiction where the
  cooperative carries on business, requiring

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any person who is indebted to the cooperative to
    render an account and pay any amount owing to the liquidator at the
    time and place specified,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any person who possesses property of the
    cooperative to deliver it to the liquidator at the time and place
    specified, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} any person who has a claim against the
    cooperative, whether liquidated, unliquidated, future or contingent,
    to present particulars of it in writing to the liquidator not later
    than two months after the first publication of the notice;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} take the property of the cooperative into custody and
  control;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} open and maintain a trust account for money received
  by the liquidator in the course of the liquidation;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} keep accounts of the moneys of the cooperative
  received and paid out in the course of the liquidation;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} maintain separate lists of the members, shareholders
  and creditors and other persons who have claims against the
  cooperative;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} if at any time the liquidator determines that the
  cooperative is unable to pay or adequately provide for the discharge
  of its obligations, apply to the court for directions;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} deliver to the court and to the Director, at least
  once in every twelve month period after appointment or more often as
  the court may require, financial statements of the cooperative in the
  form required by section 247, or in any other form that the liquidator
  may think proper or that the court may require; and

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} after the final accounts are approved by the court,
  distribute any remaining property of the cooperative among the members
  and shareholders according to their respective rights.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 320
- 2018, c. 8, s. 82(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-320-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 320"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of liquidator

- **[[321]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-321 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  liquidator may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} retain lawyers, notaries, accountants, engineers,
    appraisers and other professionals;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} bring, defend or take part in any civil, criminal,
    administrative, investigative or other action or proceeding in the
    name and on behalf of the cooperative;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} carry on the business of the cooperative as
    required for an orderly liquidation;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} sell any property of the cooperative by public
    auction or private sale;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} do all acts and execute or, in Quebec, sign any
    documents in the name and on behalf of the cooperative;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} borrow money on the security of the property of the
    cooperative;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} settle or compromise any claims by or against the
    cooperative; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} do all other things necessary for the liquidation
    of the cooperative and the distribution of its property.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Due diligence

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A liquidator is not liable under this Part if the
  liquidator exercised the care, diligence and skill that a reasonably
  prudent person would have exercised in comparable circumstances,
  including reliance in good faith on

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} financial statements of the cooperative represented
    to the liquidator by an officer of the cooperative or in a written
    report of the auditor of the cooperative fairly to reflect the
    financial condition of the cooperative; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a report of a person whose profession lends
    credibility to a statement made by the professional person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to court

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a liquidator has reason to believe that any
  property of the cooperative is in the possession or control of a
  person or that anyone has concealed, withheld or misappropriated any
  property of the cooperative, the liquidator may apply to the court for
  an order requiring the person to appear before the court at the time
  and place designated in the order and to be examined.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power of court

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the examination discloses that the person has
  concealed, withheld or misappropriated property of the cooperative,
  the court may order the person to restore it or pay compensation to
  the liquidator.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 321
- 2001, c. 14, s. 214
- 2011, c. 21, s. 109(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-321-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 321"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Costs of liquidation

- **[[322]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-322 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  liquidator must pay the costs of liquidation out of the property of
  the cooperative and must pay or make adequate provision for all claims
  against it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Final accounts

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No later than one year after appointment, and after
  paying or making adequate provision for all claims against the
  cooperative, the liquidator must apply to the court for

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} approval of the final accounts of the liquidator
    and, subject to the articles and Parts 20 and 21, an order
    permitting a distribution in money or in kind of the remaining
    property of the cooperative to the members and shareholders, if any,
    according to their respective rights; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an extension of time, setting out the reasons for
    the extension.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a liquidator fails to make the application
  required by subsection (2), a member or shareholder may apply to the
  court for an order for the liquidator to show cause why a final
  accounting and distribution should not be made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A liquidator must give notice of their intention to
  make an application under subsection (2) to the Director, to each
  inspector appointed under section 316, to each member or shareholder
  and to any person who provided a security, a fidelity bond or fidelity
  insurance for the liquidation, and must publish the notice in a
  newspaper published or distributed in the place where the cooperative
  has its registered office, in any manner set out in the by-laws or as
  otherwise directed by the court.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Final order

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the court approves the final accounts rendered by
  a liquidator, the court must make an order

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} directing the Director to issue a certificate of
    dissolution;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} directing the custody or disposal of the documents
    of the cooperative; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} discharging the liquidator, subject to the
    remaining duty required by subsection (6).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delivery of order

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The liquidator must send a certified copy of the
  order described in subsection (5) to the Director without delay.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of dissolution

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of the order described in subsection (5),
  the Director must issue a certificate of dissolution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of certificate

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} The cooperative ceases to exist on the date shown in
  the certificate of dissolution.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 322
- 2011, c. 21, s. 110(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-322-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 322"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to distribution in money

**[[323]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-323 .sectionLabel}** A member or shareholder
may apply to the court for an order requiring the distribution of the
property of the cooperative to be in money if, in the course of the
liquidation of the cooperative, the members and shareholders resolve or
the liquidator proposes to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} exchange all or substantially all the property of the
  cooperative for securities of another body corporate that are to be
  distributed to the members and shareholders, if any; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} distribute all or part of the property of the
  cooperative to the members and shareholders, if any, in kind.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of court

**[[324]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-324 .sectionLabel}** On an application under
section 323, the court, subject to the articles and Parts 20 and 21, may
order

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} that all the property of the cooperative be converted
  into and distributed in money; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} that the claims of a member or shareholder applying
  under this section be satisfied by a distribution in money, in which
  case subsections 302(19) and (20) apply.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Custody of documents

**[[325]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-325 .sectionLabel}** A person who has been
granted custody of the documents of a dissolved cooperative remains
liable to produce those documents until the end of the prescribed period
or of any other shorter period fixed by an order made under subsection
322(5).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 325
- [2018, c. 8, s. 83](#1037745-1373939){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037745-1373939.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-325-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 325"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Heirs and representatives

- **[[326]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-326 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, "member" and "shareholder" include their heirs and
  personal representatives.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuation of actions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite the dissolution of a cooperative under this
  Act,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a civil, criminal, administrative, investigative or
    other action or proceeding commenced by or against the cooperative
    before its dissolution may be continued as if it had not been
    dissolved;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a civil, criminal, administrative, investigative or
    other action or proceeding may be brought against the cooperative
    within two years after its dissolution as if it had not been
    dissolved; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any property that would have been available to
    satisfy a judgment or order if the cooperative had not been
    dissolved remains available for those purposes.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Service

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Service of a document on a cooperative after its
  dissolution may be effected by serving the document on a person named
  in the last notice sent under section 81 or 91.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reimbursement

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite the dissolution of a cooperative under this
  Act, a member or shareholder to whom any of its property has been
  distributed is liable to any person claiming under subsection (2) to
  the extent of the amount distributed to that member or shareholder. An
  action to enforce the liability may be brought no later than two years
  after the date of the dissolution of the cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representative action

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A court may order an action referred to in
  subsection (4) to be brought against the persons who were members or
  shareholders as a class, subject to any conditions that the court
  thinks fit, and, if the plaintiff establishes a claim, the court may
  refer the proceedings to a referee or other officer of the court who
  may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} add as a party to the proceedings each member or
    shareholder who was found by the plaintiff;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} determine, subject to subsection (4), the amount
    that each of those members and shareholders must contribute towards
    satisfaction of the plaintiff's claim; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} direct payment of the amounts so determined.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 326
- 2001, c. 14, s. 215
- 2011, c. 21, s. 111(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-326-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 326"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unknown claimants

- **[[327]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-327 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On
  the dissolution of a cooperative under this Act, the portion of the
  property to be distributed to a creditor, member or shareholder who
  cannot be found must be converted into money and paid to the Receiver
  General.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed satisfaction

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A payment under subsection (1) is deemed to be in
  satisfaction of a debt or claim of the creditor, member or
  shareholder.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recovery

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If at any time a person establishes their entitlement
  to any money paid to the Receiver General under this Act, the Receiver
  General must pay the person an equivalent amount out of the
  Consolidated Revenue Fund.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vesting in Crown

- **[[328]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-328
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection 326(2) and
  section 327, property of a cooperative that has not been disposed of
  at the date of its dissolution under this Act vests in Her Majesty in
  right of Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of property on revival

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a cooperative is revived as a cooperative under
  section 308, any property, other than money, that vested in Her
  Majesty under subsection (1) and that has not been disposed of must be
  returned to the cooperative, and there must be paid to the cooperative
  out of the Consolidated Revenue Fund

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an amount equal to any money received by Her
    Majesty under subsection (1); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if property other than money vested in Her Majesty
    under subsection (1) and the property has been disposed of, an
    amount equal to the lesser of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the value of the property at the date it vested
      in Her Majesty, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the amount realized by Her Majesty from the
      disposition of the property.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2001, c. 14, s. 216\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 328
- 2001, c. 14, s. 216
:::

## [PART 18]{.HLabel1}[Investigations]{.HTitleText1} {#h-48374 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Investigation

- **[[329]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-329 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  interested person may apply, without notice or on any notice that the
  court may require, to a court having jurisdiction in the place where
  the cooperative has its registered office for an order directing an
  investigation to be made of the cooperative and any of its affiliates.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Grounds

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The court may order an investigation to be made of
  the cooperative and any of its affiliates if, on an application under
  subsection (1), it appears to the court that the application is
  neither frivolous nor vexatious and that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cooperative is not organized, operated or
    carrying on business on a cooperative basis;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the business or the affairs of the cooperative are
    not being carried out or conducted in accordance with

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the restrictions contained in its articles,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} its by-laws,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a unanimous agreement, or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} this Act;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the business of the cooperative or any of its
    affiliates is or has been carried on with intent to defraud a
    person;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the business or affairs of the cooperative or any
    of its affiliates are or have been carried on or conducted, or the
    powers of the directors are or have been exercised, in a manner that
    is oppressive or unfairly prejudicial to, or that unfairly
    disregards the interests of, a member or a security holder;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the cooperative or any of its affiliates was formed
    for a fraudulent or unlawful purpose or is to be dissolved for a
    fraudulent or unlawful purpose; or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} persons concerned with the formation, business or
    affairs of the cooperative or any of its affiliates have, in
    connection with the formation, business or affairs of the
    cooperative, acted fraudulently or dishonestly.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No security for costs

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An applicant under this section is not required to
  give security for costs.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 329
- 2001, c. 14, s. 217(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of court

- **[[330]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-330 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  connection with an investigation under this Part, the court may make
  any order it thinks fit, including an order

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to investigate;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} appointing an inspector, who may be the Director,
    and fixing their remuneration, or replacing an inspector;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} determining the notice to be given to any
    interested person, or dispensing with notice to any person;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} authorizing an inspector to enter any premises in
    which the court is satisfied there might be relevant information and
    to examine any thing and make copies of any document found on the
    premises;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} requiring any person to produce documents to an
    inspector;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} authorizing an inspector to conduct a hearing,
    administer oaths and examine any person on oath, and setting out
    rules for the conduct of hearings;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} requiring any person to attend a hearing conducted
    by an inspector and to give evidence on oath;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} giving directions to an inspector or any other
    interested person on any matter arising in the investigation;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} requiring an inspector to make an interim or final
    report to the court;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} determining whether a report of an inspector should
    be published and, if so, ordering its publication in whole or in
    part or that copies of it be sent to any person the court
    designates;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} requiring an inspector to discontinue the
    investigation;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} if the cooperative is incorporated with membership
    capital, requiring the cooperative to be continued under the [Canada
    Business Corporations Act](/eng/acts/C-44), or if it is incorporated
    without membership capital, requiring it to be dissolved;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} determining any matter that relates to the
    relationship between a member and the cooperative; and

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} requiring the cooperative to pay the costs of the
    investigation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copy of report

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An inspector must send the Director a copy of every
  report made by the inspector under this Part.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of inspector

- **[[331]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-331 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  inspector appointed under this Part has the powers set out in the
  order appointing the inspector.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exchange of information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In addition to the powers set out in the order
  appointing an inspector, the inspector may provide information to, or
  exchange information and otherwise cooperate with, any public official
  in Canada or elsewhere who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is authorized to exercise investigatory powers; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is investigating, in respect of the cooperative,
    any allegation of improper conduct that is the same as or similar to
    the conduct described in subsection 329(2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Court order

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An inspector must, on the request of an interested
  person, produce a copy of any order made under subsection 330(1).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hearing in private

- **[[332]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-332 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  interested person may apply to the court for an order that a hearing
  under this Part be heard in private and for directions on any matter
  arising in the investigation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to counsel

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person whose conduct is being investigated or who
  is being examined at a hearing conducted under this Part has the right
  to be represented by counsel.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incriminating statements

**[[333]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-333 .sectionLabel}** No person is excused
from attending and giving evidence and producing documents to an
inspector under this Part by reason only that the evidence tends to
incriminate the person or subject the person to a proceeding or penalty,
but no such evidence may be used or is receivable against the person in
any later proceeding instituted under an Act of Parliament, other than a
prosecution for perjury in giving the evidence or a prosecution under
section 132 or 136 of the [Criminal Code](/eng/acts/C-46) in respect of
the evidence.
