[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Absolute privilege --- defamation

**[[334]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-334 .sectionLabel}** Any oral or written
statement or report made by an inspector or other person in an
investigation under this Part has absolute privilege.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Membership shares included

- **[[335]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-335 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of this section, a security includes a membership share
  or an interest or right in one.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information respecting ownership and
  control

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Director is satisfied that, for the purposes
  of Part 9 or 10 or for the purposes of enforcing any regulation made
  under section 130, there is reason to inquire into the ownership or
  control of a security of a cooperative or any of its affiliates, the
  Director may require any person that the Director reasonably believes
  has or has had an interest or right in the security or acts or has
  acted on behalf of a person with such an interest or right to report
  to the Director or to any designated person,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} information that the person has or can reasonably
    be expected to obtain as to present and past interests or rights in
    the security; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the names and addresses of the persons who have or
    have had such an interest or right and of any person who acts or has
    acted in relation to the security on behalf of such persons.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed interest or right in securities

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (2), a person is
  deemed to hold an interest or right in a security if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a membership share, the person is or
    is entitled to be entered in the records of the cooperative as the
    owner of the membership share; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an investment share,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person has a right to vote or to acquire or
      dispose of the investment share or an interest or right in it,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the person's consent is necessary for the
      exercise of the rights or privileges of any other person with an
      interest or right in the investment share, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} any other person with an interest or right in
      the investment share can be required or is accustomed to exercise
      rights or privileges attached to it in accordance with that
      person's instructions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Director must publish, in a publication generally
  available to the public, the particulars of information obtained under
  this section if the particulars

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} are required by this Act or the regulations to be
    disclosed; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} have not previously been so disclosed.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 335
- 2011, c. 21, s. 112
- 2018, c. 8, s. 84(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-335-20111129.html "Link to previous version of section 335"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Solicitor-client privilege or
professional secrecy

**[[336]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-336 .sectionLabel}** Nothing in this Part
may be construed as affecting solicitor-client privilege or, in Quebec,
the professional secrecy of advocates and notaries.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 336
- 2011, c. 21, s. 113(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-336-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 336"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inquiries

**[[337]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-337 .sectionLabel}** The Director may make
inquiries of any person relating to compliance with this Act.

## [PART 18.1]{.HLabel1}[Apportioning Award of Damages]{.HTitleText1} {#h-48457 .Part}

### [Interpretation and Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-48458 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[337.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-337.1 .sectionLabel}** The definitions in
this section apply in this Part.

[[financial interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[financial interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, with respect to a
    cooperative, includes

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a security;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a title to or an interest in capital, assets,
      property, profits, earnings or royalties;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an option or other interest in, or a subscription
      to, a security;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an agreement under which the interest of the
      purchaser is valued for purposes of conversion or surrender by
      reference to the value of a proportionate interest in a specified
      portfolio of assets;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} an agreement providing that money received will
      be repaid or treated as a subscription for shares, units or
      interests at the option of any person or the cooperative;

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a profit-sharing agreement or certificate;

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a lease, claim or royalty in oil, natural gas or
      mining, or an interest in the lease, claim or royalty;

    - [(h)]{.lawlabel} an income or annuity contract that is not issued
      by an insurance company governed by an Act of Parliament or a law
      of a province;

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an investment contract; and

    - [(j)]{.lawlabel} anything that is prescribed to be a financial
      interest. ([intérêt financier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[financial loss]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[financial loss]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a financial loss
    arising out of an error, omission or misstatement in financial
    information concerning a cooperative that is required under this Act
    or the regulations. ([perte financière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[third party]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[third party]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes any subsequent party
    that is joined in proceedings before a court. ([Version anglaise
    seulement]{.DefinitionOtherLangOnly})

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 218
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of Part

- **[[337.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-337.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This Part applies to the
  apportionment of damages awarded to a plaintiff for financial loss
  after a court has found more than one defendant or third party
  responsible for the financial loss.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of Part

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} This Part does not apply to an award of damages to
  any of the following plaintiffs:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Her Majesty in right of Canada or of a province;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an agent of Her Majesty in right of Canada or of a
    province or a federal or provincial Crown corporation or government
    agency, unless a substantial part of its activities involves
    trading, including making investments in, securities or other
    financial instruments;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a charitable organization, private foundation or
    public foundation within the meaning of subsection 149.1(1) of the
    [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3); or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an unsecured creditor in respect of goods or
    services that the creditor provided to a cooperative.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 218
:::

### [Apportionment of Damages]{.HTitleText2} {#h-48487 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Degree of responsibility

- **[[337.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-337.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this section and sections
  337.4 to 337.6, every defendant or third party who has been found
  responsible for a financial loss is liable to the plaintiff only for
  the portion of the damages that corresponds to their degree of
  responsibility for the loss.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Uncollectable amounts

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If any part of the damages awarded against a
  responsible defendant or third party is uncollectable, the court may,
  on the application of the plaintiff, reallocate that amount to the
  other responsible defendants or third parties, if the application is
  made within one year after the date that the judgment was made
  enforceable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reallocation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The amount that may be reallocated to each of the
  other responsible defendants or third parties under subsection (2) is
  calculated by multiplying the uncollectable amount by the percentage
  that corresponds to the degree of responsibility of that defendant or
  third party for the total financial loss.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum amount

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The maximum amount determined under subsection (3),
  in respect of any responsible defendant or third party, may not be
  more than fifty per cent of the amount originally awarded against that
  responsible defendant or third party.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 218
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- fraud

- **[[337.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-337.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The plaintiff may recover the whole
  amount of the damages awarded by the court from any defendant or third
  party who has been held responsible for a financial loss if it was
  established that the defendant or third party acted fraudulently or
  dishonestly.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contribution

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The defendant or third party referred to in
  subsection (1) is entitled to claim contribution from any other
  defendant or third party who is held responsible for the loss.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 218
:::

### [Joint and Several, or Solidary, Liability]{.HTitleText2} {#h-48506 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Individual or personal body corporate

- **[[337.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-337.5
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Defendants and third parties
  referred to in subsection 337.2(1) are jointly and severally, or
  solidarily, liable for the damages awarded to a plaintiff who is an
  individual or a personal body corporate and who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} had a financial interest in a cooperative on the
    day that an error, omission or misstatement in financial information
    concerning the cooperative occurred, or acquired a financial
    interest in the period between the day that the error, omission or
    misstatement occurred and the day, as determined by the court, that
    it was generally disclosed; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has established that the value of the plaintiff's
    total financial interest in the cooperative was not more than the
    prescribed amount at the close of business on the day that the
    error, omission or misstatement occurred or at the close of business
    on any day that the plaintiff acquired a financial interest in the
    period referred to in paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply when the plaintiff
  brings the action as a member of a partnership or other association or
  as a trustee in bankruptcy, liquidator, receiver or sequestrator of a
  body corporate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a personal body corporate is a body corporate that
    is not actively engaged in any financial, commercial or industrial
    business and that is controlled by an individual, or by a group of
    individuals who are connected by marriage, common-law partnership or
    any legal parent-child relationship or are connected indirectly by a
    combination of those relationships, whether or not the individuals
    through whom they are connected are members of the group; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a common-law partnership is a relationship between
    two persons who are cohabiting with each other in a conjugal
    relationship and have done so for a period of at least one year.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 218
- 2005, c. 33, s. 6
- 2011, c. 21, s. 114(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-337.5-20050720.html "Link to previous version of section 337.5"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Equitable grounds

- **[[337.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-337.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the value of the plaintiff's
  total financial interest referred to in subsection 337.5(1) is greater
  than the prescribed amount, a court may nevertheless determine that
  the defendants and third parties are jointly and severally, or
  solidarily, liable if the court considers that it is just and
  reasonable to do so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Factors

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may establish factors that
  the court shall take into account in deciding whether to hold the
  defendants and third parties jointly and severally, or solidarily,
  liable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Statutory Instruments
  Act](/eng/acts/S-22)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The [Statutory Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22) does
  not apply to the factors referred to in subsection (2), but the
  factors shall be published in Part I of the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 218
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Value of security

- **[[337.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-337.7
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When, in order to establish the
  value of the total financial interest referred to in subsection
  337.5(1), it is necessary to determine the value of a security that is
  traded on an organized market, the value of the security is, on the
  day specified in subsection (3),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the closing price of that class of security;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if no closing price is given, the average of the
    highest and lowest prices of that class of security; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the security was not traded, the average of the
    bid and ask prices of that class of security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Court may adjust value

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The court may adjust the value of a security that has
  been determined under subsection (1) when the court considers it
  reasonable to do so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Valuation day

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The value of the security is to be determined as of
  the day that the error, omission or misstatement occurred. If the
  security was acquired in the period between that day and the day, as
  determined by the court, that the error, omission or misstatement was
  generally disclosed, the value is to be determined as of the day that
  it was acquired.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[organized
  market]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[organized
  market]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a recognized exchange for a class
  of securities or a market that regularly publishes the price of that
  class of securities in a publication that is generally available to
  the public.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 218
- 2018, c. 8, s. 85(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-337.7-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 337.7"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Court determines value

- **[[337.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-337.8
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The court shall determine the value
  of all or any part of a financial interest that is subject to resale
  restrictions or for which there is no organized market.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Factors

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may establish factors that
  the court may take into account in determining value under subsection
  (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Statutory Instruments
  Act](/eng/acts/S-22)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The [Statutory Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22) does
  not apply to the factors referred to in subsection (2), but the
  factors shall be published in Part I of the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 218
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to determine value

**[[337.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-337.9 .sectionLabel}** The plaintiff may,
by application made at any time before or during the course of the
proceedings, request the court to determine the value of the plaintiff's
financial interest for the purpose of subsection 337.5(1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 218
:::

## [PART 19]{.HLabel1}[Remedies, Offences and Punishment]{.HTitleText1} {#h-48556 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[338]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-338 .sectionLabel}** The definitions in this
section apply in this Part.

[[action]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[action]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an action taken under this
    Act. ([action]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[complainant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[complainant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a member or former member;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a registered holder or beneficial owner, or a
      former registered holder or beneficial owner, of a security of a
      cooperative or any of its affiliates;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a director or an officer, or a former director or
      officer, of a cooperative or any of its affiliates; or

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2001, c. 14, s. 219\]]{.Repealed}

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any other person who, in the opinion of the
      court, is a proper person to make an application under this
      Part. ([plaignant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 338
- 2001, c. 14, s. 219
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Commencement of derivative action

- **[[339]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-339
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), a
  complainant may apply to the court for leave to bring an action in the
  name and on behalf of a cooperative or any of its subsidiaries, or to
  intervene in an action to which the cooperative or any of its
  subsidiaries is a party, for the purpose of prosecuting, defending or
  discontinuing the action on behalf of the cooperative or subsidiary.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Condition precedent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person may bring an action and no person may
  intervene in an action brought under subsection (1) unless the court
  is satisfied that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the directors of the cooperative or its
    subsidiary do not bring, diligently prosecute, defend or discontinue
    the action, the complainant has given notice to the directors of the
    cooperative or its subsidiary of the complainant's intention to
    apply to the court under subsection (1) not less than fourteen days
    before bringing the application, or as otherwise ordered by the
    court;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the complainant is acting in good faith; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it appears to be in the interests of the
    cooperative or its subsidiary to bring, prosecute, defend or
    discontinue the action.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of court

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In an action brought or intervened in under this
  section, the court may make any order it considers appropriate,
  including an order

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} authorizing the complainant or any other person to
    control the conduct of the action;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} giving directions for the conduct of the action;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} directing that an amount adjudged payable by a
    defendant in the action be paid, in whole or in part, directly to a
    former or present member or to a former or present security holder
    of the cooperative or its subsidiary instead of to the cooperative
    or its subsidiary; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} requiring the cooperative or its subsidiary to pay
    reasonable costs incurred by the complainant in connection with the
    action.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 339
- 2001, c. 14, s. 220
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to court re oppression

- **[[340]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-340 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  complainant may apply to the court for an order, including an
  alternate order, under this section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Grounds

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the court receives an application under
  subsection (1) and is satisfied that an act or omission of a
  cooperative effects a result, that the business or affairs of the
  cooperative are or have been carried on or conducted in a manner, or
  that the powers of the director are or have been exercised in a
  manner, that is oppressive or unfairly prejudicial to or that unfairly
  disregards the interests of a member or other security holder,
  creditor, director or officer of the cooperative, the court may order
  the rectification of the matters complained of.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Types of order

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection (2), the court may make
  any order that it considers appropriate, including an order

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} restraining the conduct complained of;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} appointing a receiver or receiver-manager;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} requiring the cooperative to amend an agreement
    with members generally or with a member;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} regulating the affairs of the cooperative by
    amending its articles or by-laws or creating or amending a unanimous
    agreement;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} directing an issue or exchange of securities;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} directing changes in the directors;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} determining whether a person is or is qualified to
    be a member;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} determining any matter in regard to the relations
    between the cooperative and a member;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (6), directing the
    cooperative or any other person to purchase securities of a security
    holder;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (6), directing the
    cooperative or any other person to pay to a security holder any part
    of the money paid by the security holder for securities;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (6), directing the
    cooperative to redeem membership shares, repay member loans or to
    pay to a member any other amount standing to the member's credit in
    the records of the cooperative;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} varying or setting aside a transaction or contract
    to which the cooperative is a party and compensating the cooperative
    or any other party to the transaction or contract;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} directing the production and delivery within a
    specified time of financial statements of the cooperative;

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} directing an accounting;

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} compensating an aggrieved person;

  - [(p)]{.lawlabel} directing rectification of the registers or other
    records of the cooperative under section 342;

  - [(q)]{.lawlabel} liquidating and dissolving the cooperative;

  - [(r)]{.lawlabel} directing a special audit or an investigation under
    section 329; or

  - [(s)]{.lawlabel} requiring the trial of an issue.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty of directors and members

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If an order made under this section directs an
  amendment of the articles or by-laws of a cooperative,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the directors, members and shareholders must comply
    with subsection 303(5); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} no other amendment to the articles or by-laws may
    be made without the consent of the court, until the court orders
    otherwise.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exclusion

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A member or shareholder is not entitled to dissent
  under section 302 if an amendment to the articles is effected under
  this section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} No cooperative may make a payment to a member or
  shareholder under an order of the court if there are reasonable
  grounds to believe that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cooperative is, or would after that payment be,
    unable to pay its liabilities as they become due; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the realizable value of the cooperative's assets
    after the payment would be less than the total of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} its liabilities, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the amount that would be required to pay the
      holders of securities who have a right to be paid, on a redemption
      or liquidation, rateably with or in priority to the holders of the
      securities to be purchased or redeemed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternate order

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} An applicant under this section may apply for an
  order under section 313 instead of the order under this section.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 340
- 2001, c. 14, s. 221(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Evidence of member or shareholder
approval not decisive

- **[[341]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-341 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  application made and no action brought or intervened in under this
  Part is to be stayed or dismissed by reason only that it is shown that
  an alleged breach of a right or duty owed to the cooperative or any of
  its subsidiaries has been or may be approved by the members or
  shareholders, but evidence of approval by the members or shareholders
  must be taken into account by the court in making an order under
  section 313 or this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Court approval to discontinue

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No application made and no action brought or
  intervened in under this Part is to be stayed, discontinued, settled
  or dismissed for want of prosecution or, in Quebec, failure to respect
  the agreement between the parties as to the conduct of the proceeding
  without the approval of the court given on any terms that the court
  considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the court determines that the interests of a
  complainant may be substantially affected by a stay, discontinuance,
  settlement or dismissal mentioned in subsection (2), the court may
  order any party to the application or action to give notice of the
  application or action to the complainant.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No security for costs

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A complainant is not required to give security for
  costs in an application made or action brought or intervened in under
  this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interim costs

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} In an application made or an action brought or
  intervened in under this Part, the court may at any time order the
  cooperative or its subsidiary to pay to the complainant interim costs,
  including legal fees and disbursements, but the complainant may be
  held accountable for any interim costs so paid on the final
  disposition of the application or action.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 341
- 2011, c. 21, s. 115
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-341-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 341"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to court to rectify records

- **[[342]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-342 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the name of a person is alleged to be or to have been wrongly entered
  or retained in or wrongly deleted or omitted from the registers or
  other records of a cooperative, the cooperative, a security holder of
  the cooperative or any aggrieved person may apply to the court for an
  order that the registers or records be rectified.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power of court

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On an application made under this section, the court
  may make any order it considers appropriate, including an order

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} requiring the registers or records of the
    cooperative to be rectified;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} restraining the cooperative from calling or holding
    a meeting or allocating or paying a dividend or interest on shares
    or a patronage refund before rectification of the registers or
    records;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} determining the right of a party to the proceedings
    to have the party's name entered or retained in or deleted or
    omitted from the registers or records of the cooperative, whether
    the issue arises between two or more members or security holders, or
    alleged members or security holders, or between the cooperative and
    a member or security holder or alleged member or security holder; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} compensating a party who has incurred a loss by
    reason of the wrongful entry, retention, deletion or omission.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for directions

**[[343]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-343 .sectionLabel}** The Director may apply
to a court for directions in respect of any matter concerning the
Director's duties under this Act and, on the application, the court may
give any directions that it thinks fit.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of refusal by Director

- **[[344]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-344 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Director must file all documents that are required to be sent to him
  or her under this Act. If the Director refuses to file any of those
  documents, the Director must, no later than twenty days after the
  later of the receipt of the document and the receipt of any approval
  that may be required under any other law, give written notice of the
  refusal to the person who sent the document, giving reasons for the
  refusal.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed refusal

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Director does not file or give written reasons
  of refusal to file the documents within the twenty days provided for
  in subsection (1), the Director is deemed to have refused to file the
  document.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal from Director's decision

**[[345]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-345 .sectionLabel}** A person who feels
aggrieved by a decision of the Director referred to in any of paragraphs
(a) to (f) may apply to a court for an order, including an order
requiring the Director to change the decision:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} to refuse to file in the form submitted any articles
  or other documents required by this Act to be sent;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} to give a name, change or revoke a name or refuse to
  reserve, accept, change or revoke a name under this Act;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} to grant, or to refuse to grant, an exemption that
  may be granted under this Act and the regulations;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} to refuse to issue a certificate of discontinuance or
  a certificate attesting that as of a certain date the cooperative
  exists under section 375;

- [(d.1)]{.lawlabel} to correct, or to refuse to correct, articles, a
  notice, a certificate or other document under section 376.1;

- [(d.2)]{.lawlabel} to cancel, or to refuse to cancel, the articles and
  related certificate under section 376.2;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} to issue, or refuse to issue, a certificate of
  revival or the decision with respect to the terms for revival imposed
  by the Director; or

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} to dissolve a cooperative under section 311.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 345
- 2001, c. 14, s. 222
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restraining or compliance order

**[[346]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-346 .sectionLabel}** If a cooperative or any
director, officer, employee, agent or mandatary, auditor, trustee,
receiver, receiver-manager, sequestrator or liquidator of a cooperative
does not comply with this Act, the regulations, the articles, the
by-laws or a unanimous agreement, a complainant or the Director may, in
addition to any other right, apply to a court for an order directing any
such person to comply with or restraining them from acting in breach of
it. On the application, the court may order compliance and make any
further order it thinks fit.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 346
- 2011, c. 21, s. 116(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-346-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 346"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Summary application to court

**[[347]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-347 .sectionLabel}** When this Act states
that a person may apply to a court, the application may be made in any
summary manner by petition, originating notice of motion or otherwise as
the rules of court provide and subject to any order respecting notice to
interested parties or costs or any other order the court thinks fit.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal of final order

- **[[348]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-348 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  appeal lies to the court of appeal of a province from any final order
  made by a court of that province under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal on leave

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An appeal lies to the court of appeal of a province
  from any other order made by a court of that province, only with leave
  of the court of appeal in accordance with the rules of that court.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offences with respect to this Act

- **[[349]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-349
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who knowingly
  contravenes section 31, 32 or 33, subsection 165(1), section 166, 168,
  169, 172, 251 or 252, subsection 260(2), 264(1) or (3) or 272(5),
  section 325 or 335 or any other provision of this Act or who does not
  fulfil a duty imposed under this Act is guilty of an offence
  punishable on summary conviction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offences with respect to the
  regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who, without reasonable cause,
  contravenes a provision of the regulations or does not fulfil a duty
  imposed by the regulations is guilty of an offence punishable on
  summary conviction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offences with respect to reports

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person is guilty of an offence punishable on
  summary conviction if the person knowingly makes or assists in making
  a report, return, notice or other document required by this Act or the
  regulations to be sent to the Director or to any other person that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} contains an untrue statement of a material fact; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} omits to state a material fact required in it or
    necessary to make a statement contained in it not misleading in the
    light of the circumstances in which it was made.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order to comply

- **[[350]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-350 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  person is guilty of an offence under this Act or the regulations, the
  court in which proceedings in respect of the offence are taken may, in
  addition to any punishment it may impose, order the person to comply
  with the provisions of this Act or the regulations for the
  contravention of which the person has been convicted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A prosecution of an offence under this Act may be
  instituted at any time within but not later than two years after the
  time when the subject-matter of the complaint arose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Civil remedy not affected

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No civil remedy for an act or omission is suspended
  or affected by reason that the act or omission is an offence under
  this Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternative resolution

**[[351]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-351 .sectionLabel}** The Minister of
Agriculture and Agri-Food may, in accordance with any regulations,
provide assistance with respect to the alternative resolution of any
dispute relating to the affairs of a cooperative.

## [PART 20]{.HLabel1}[Additional Provisions Respecting Non-profit Housing Cooperatives]{.HTitleText1} {#h-48696 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

**[[352]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-352 .sectionLabel}** This Part applies to
cooperatives for which articles are issued to carry on business as
non-profit housing cooperatives.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement of articles

**[[353]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-353 .sectionLabel}** The articles of a
non-profit housing cooperative must provide that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the non-profit housing cooperative
  include the words "non-profit", "not-for-profit", "not for profit",
  "sans but lucratif" or "à but non lucratif" and "housing cooperative",
  "housing co-operative", "housing co-op", "coopérative d'habitation",
  "coop d'habitation", "co-op d'habitation", "coopérative de logement",
  "coop de logement" or "co-op de logement"; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the business of the cooperative is restricted to that
  of primarily providing housing to its members.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special limitations

**[[354]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-354 .sectionLabel}** The following
restrictions apply to a non-profit housing cooperative:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} it may not issue investment shares;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} it may only issue membership shares on a par-value
  basis;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} it must in each year conduct at least fifty per cent
  of its business with its members;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection 357(2), its business must be
  carried on without the purpose of gain for its members; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} on its dissolution, and after the payment of its
  liabilities, its remaining property is to be transferred to or
  distributed among one or more non-profit housing cooperatives,
  cooperatives incorporated in a province that have similar objectives
  and limitations, or charitable organizations.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}By-laws

- **[[355]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-355 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  by-laws of a non-profit housing cooperative must include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any obligation of a member to provide capital to
    the cooperative and the manner in which the capital is to be
    contributed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any obligation of a member to contribute to charges
    of the cooperative and the manner in which the charges are to be
    determined and paid;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the procedure for determining disputes between
    members and between members and the cooperative;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (2), the manner in which the
    membership of a member may be terminated;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the manner in which a member's equity is to be
    determined on the member's withdrawal from the cooperative, or on
    the termination of their membership and on a dissolution of the
    cooperative and the manner in which that equity is to be repaid;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a provision for the establishment of adequate
    reserves and the maintenance of adequate insurance to protect the
    cooperative from loss; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a provision for the establishment of the rules
    governing charges by the cooperative for the occupation, leasing,
    subletting and subleasing of housing units of the cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Termination of membership

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the by-laws provide that membership is to be
  terminated by the directors, the by-laws must include a right of
  appeal to the members. Despite section 40, the by-laws may require
  that an appeal from the decision of the directors may only be launched
  within seven days after the member receives notice of the termination
  of membership.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other provisions in by-laws

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The by-laws of a non-profit housing cooperative may
  provide

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the rules by which the directors may establish a
    system of providing subsidies to members for housing charges,
    provided that the administration of the rules by the directors is
    fair as among the members; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any rules in addition to those specified in
    paragraph (1)(g) relating to the occupancy of a housing unit of the
    cooperative.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Member occupancy rights

- **[[356]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-356 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who is a member is entitled to occupy the housing unit
  allocated to them by the non-profit housing cooperative while they are
  a member, although the directors may, subject to the by-laws, by
  notice, require the member to occupy an alternate housing unit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A member to whom a notice has been given by the
  cooperative to occupy an alternate housing unit may appeal that
  decision in the same manner as that provided for appeals of decisions
  by the directors to terminate the membership of a member.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unsuccessful appeal

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If an appeal by the member is not successful and the
  member does not relocate to the alternate housing unit within the time
  provided by the directors, their membership is deemed to have been
  terminated and despite section 40 and subsection 356(4), the member
  has no further appeal.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Occupancy during appeal

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite section 40, if, in accordance with the
  by-laws, the directors terminate a member's membership and the member
  appeals the termination, the member has the right to occupy the
  housing unit allocated to them until the termination has been
  confirmed by the members.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Confirmation on appeal

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A decision of the directors to terminate the
  membership of a member is confirmed if the members, at a duly called
  meeting of the members, do not reverse the directors' decision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed confirmation

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a meeting of the members is duly called to
  consider the appeal of a terminated member and a quorum of members is
  not available for the meeting, the decision of the directors is deemed
  to be confirmed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regaining possession of unit

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If a member's membership has been terminated and the
  member no longer has the right to occupy a housing unit of the
  cooperative, the cooperative regains possession of the housing unit by
  consent or by appropriate legal proceedings.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compensation for occupation past
  termination date

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} The cooperative is entitled to compensation for a
  housing unit that is occupied by a terminated member until it is
  vacated.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cooperative not to take other property

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} The cooperative may not take the property of a member
  to satisfy amounts due to the cooperative except by consent or by
  legal proceedings.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inclusion in by-laws

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of section 16, the provisions of
  this section must be included in the by-laws of a non-profit housing
  cooperative.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-profit requirements

- **[[357]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-357
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), but
  despite any other provision of this Act, a non-profit housing
  cooperative may not distribute or pay any of its assets to a member.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permitted payments

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The cooperative may pay a member

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} dividends on membership shares paid at a rate that
    is not more than eight per cent per year and interest on member
    loans at a rate that is not more than ten per cent per year;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the member withdraws from the cooperative or
    their membership in the cooperative is terminated, or on the
    dissolution of the cooperative, a sum equal to the member's
    investment in membership shares or member loans; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a reasonable amount for property or services
    provided by the member to the cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No other payments permitted

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person may pay or accept compensation for the
  withdrawal from membership of a member of a non-profit housing
  cooperative or for any person giving up possession of a housing unit
  of the cooperative, except as is provided in subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limit on compensation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} No person may give or accept, in connection with the
  allocation or use of a housing unit of the cooperative, compensation
  that exceeds the housing charges for the housing unit determined in
  accordance with the by-laws.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limit on compensation

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} No person may give or accept, in connection with the
  allocation or use of a part of a housing unit of the cooperative,
  compensation that exceeds the amount that, having regard to the
  portion of the housing unit, would be a reasonable share of the
  housing charges for the housing unit determined in accordance with the
  by-laws.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If contravention of subsection (3), (4)
  or (5)

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A person who accepts compensation in contravention of
  subsection (3), (4) or (5) must pay the amount of the compensation to
  the cooperative.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Resolution if proposed fundamental change

**[[358]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-358 .sectionLabel}** A non-profit housing
cooperative may not be a party to a fundamental change to which Part 16
applies unless the change is authorized by a vote of not less than
ninety per cent of the members.

## [PART 21]{.HLabel1}[Additional Provisions Respecting Worker Cooperatives]{.HTitleText1} {#h-48765 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[worker
cooperative]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[359]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-359 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of this Part, [[worker cooperative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  means a cooperative whose prime objectives are to provide employment
  to its members and to operate an enterprise in which control rests
  with the members.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements of articles

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The articles of a worker cooperative must provide
  that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} no person may be admitted to membership unless the
    person is an individual and an employee of the cooperative; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the maximum membership investment payable by a
    person to be a member may not be more than fifty per cent of their
    expected annual salary during the first year of their membership,
    unless any amount in excess of that amount is also paid equally by
    all persons who are members.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-member employees

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), a worker cooperative may
  provide employment to non-members if, not later than five years after
  the incorporation of the cooperative or the acquisition of a business
  by the cooperative, not less than seventy-five per cent of its
  permanent employees or those of any entity controlled by it are
  members.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permanent employees

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection (3), the following
  persons are not permanent employees:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person who is employed on a probationary period
    of less than three years; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person who is under contract for a period of less
    than two years.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}By-laws

- **[[360]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-360 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  by-laws of a worker cooperative must include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any obligation of a member to provide capital to
    the cooperative, which capital, if required, must be applied fairly
    to all members;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsections (2) and (3), the manner in
    which the membership of a member may be terminated;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the procedure for allocating, crediting or
    distributing any surplus earnings of the cooperative, including that
    not less than fifty per cent of those earnings must be paid on the
    basis of the remuneration earned by the members from the cooperative
    or the labour contributed by the members to the cooperative;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the period of probation of an applicant for
    membership, which may not be longer than three years;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} how work is to be allocated;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a provision for the laying off or suspending of
    members when there is a lack of work; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a provision for the recall of members to work.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Termination of membership

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the by-laws provide that membership is to be
  terminated by the directors, the by-laws must include a right of
  appeal to the members. Despite section 40, the by-laws may require
  that an appeal from the decision of the directors may only be launched
  within seven days after the member receives notice of the termination
  of membership.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Laid-off member

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A temporary lay-off of a member does not result in
  termination of their membership. After a member has been laid off and
  two years have elapsed after the date of the lay-off without the
  member having resumed employment with the cooperative, the directors
  or the members may, in accordance with the by-laws, terminate the
  membership of the member.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Confirmation on appeal

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A decision of the directors to terminate the
  membership of a member is confirmed if the members, at a duly called
  meeting of the members, do not reverse the directors' decision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed confirmation

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a meeting of the members is duly called to
  consider the appeal of a terminated member and a quorum of members is
  not available for the meeting, the directors must call a second
  meeting to be held, despite section 52, not later than seven days
  after the first meeting. If, at the second meeting, there is no quorum
  of members, the decision of the directors is deemed to be confirmed.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Name

- **[[361]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-361 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  articles of a worker cooperative must provide that the name of the
  worker cooperative include the words "worker cooperative", "worker
  co-operative", "worker co-op", "coopérative de travailleurs", "coop de
  travailleurs", "co-op de travailleurs", "coopérative de travail",
  "coop de travail" or "co-op de travail".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Directors

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite anything in this Act,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} not less than eighty per cent of the directors of a
    worker cooperative must be members that are employees of the
    cooperative; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the general manager of a worker cooperative may be
    a director.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reorganization

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A worker cooperative may not be a party to a
  fundamental change to which Part 16 applies unless the change is
  authorized by a vote of not less than ninety per cent of the members.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dissolution

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Unless otherwise provided in the articles, on
  dissolution of a worker cooperative, not less than twenty per cent of
  the surplus of the cooperative, after the payment of its liabilities,
  must be distributed to another cooperative, a non-profit entity or a
  charitable entity before any distribution is made to a member or
  shareholder.

## [PART 21.1]{.HLabel1}[Documents in Electronic or Other Form]{.HTitleText1} {#h-48808 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[361.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-361.1 .sectionLabel}** The definitions in
this section apply in this Part.

[[electronic document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[electronic document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, except in section
    361.6, any form of representation of information or of concepts
    fixed in any medium in or by electronic, optical or other similar
    means and that can be read or perceived by a person or by any
    means. ([document électronique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[information system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[information system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a system used to
    generate, send, receive, store or otherwise process an electronic
    document. ([système d'information]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 223
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

**[[361.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-361.2 .sectionLabel}** This Part does not
apply to a notice, document or other information sent to or issued by
the Director pursuant to this Act or to any prescribed notice, document
or other information.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 223
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use not mandatory

- **[[361.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-361.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in this Act or the
  regulations requires a person to create or provide an electronic
  document.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consent and other requirements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite anything in this Part, a requirement under
  this Act or the regulations to provide a person with a notice,
  document or other information is not satisfied by the provision of an
  electronic document unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the addressee has consented, in the manner
    prescribed, and has designated an information system for the receipt
    of the electronic document; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the electronic document is provided to the
    designated information system, unless otherwise prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation of consent

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An addressee may revoke the consent referred to in
  paragraph (2)(a) in the manner prescribed.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 223
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Creation and provision of information

**[[361.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-361.4 .sectionLabel}** A requirement under
this Act or the regulations that a notice, document or other information
be created or provided is satisfied by the creation or provision of an
electronic document if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the by-laws or the articles of the cooperative do not
  provide otherwise; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the regulations, if any, have been complied with.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 223
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Creation of information in writing

- **[[361.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-361.5
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A requirement under this Act or the
  regulations that a notice, document or other information be created in
  writing is satisfied by the creation of an electronic document if, in
  addition to the conditions in section 361.4,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information in the electronic document is
    accessible so as to be usable for subsequent reference; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the regulations pertaining to this subsection, if
    any, have been complied with.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provision of information in writing

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A requirement under this Act or the regulations that
  a notice, document or other information be provided in writing is
  satisfied by the provision of an electronic document if, in addition
  to the conditions set out in section 361.4,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information in the electronic document is
    accessible by the addressee and capable of being retained by the
    addressee, so as to be usable for subsequent reference; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the regulations pertaining to this subsection, if
    any, have been complied with.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A requirement under this Act or the regulations for
  one or more copies of a document to be provided to a single addressee
  at the same time is satisfied by the provision of a single version of
  the electronic document.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registered mail

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A requirement under this Act or the regulations to
  provide a document by registered mail is not satisfied by the sending
  of an electronic document unless prescribed.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 223
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statutory declarations and affidavits

- **[[361.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-361.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A statutory declaration or an
  affidavit required under this Act or the regulations may be created or
  provided in an electronic document if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person who makes the statutory declaration or
    affidavit signs it with his or her secure electronic signature;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the authorized person before whom the statutory
    declaration or affidavit is made signs it with his or her secure
    electronic signature; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the requirements of sections 361.3 to 361.5 are
    complied with.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, "electronic
  document" and "secure electronic signature" have the same meaning as
  in subsection 31(1) of the [Personal Information Protection and
  Electronic Documents Act](/eng/acts/P-8.6).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clarification

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of complying with paragraph (1)(c),
  the references to an "electronic document" in sections 361.3 to 361.5
  are to be read as references to an "electronic document" as defined in
  subsection 31(1) of the [Personal Information Protection and
  Electronic Documents Act](/eng/acts/P-8.6).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 223
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Signatures

**[[361.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-361.7 .sectionLabel}** A requirement under
this Act or the regulations for a signature or for a document to be
executed, except with respect to a statutory declaration or an
affidavit, is satisfied if, in relation to an electronic document, the
prescribed requirements pertaining to this section, if any, are met and
if the signature results from the application by a person of a
technology or a process that permits the following to be proven:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the signature resulting from the use by a person of
  the technology or process is unique to the person;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the technology or process is used by a person to
  incorporate, attach or associate the person's signature to the
  electronic document; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the technology or process can be used to identify the
  person using the technology or process.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 223
:::

## [PART 22]{.HLabel1}[General]{.HTitleText1} {#h-48870 .Part}

### [Notices]{.HTitleText2} {#h-48871 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to members, shareholders and
directors

- **[[362]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-362 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  notice or document required by this Act, the regulations, the
  articles, the by-laws or a unanimous agreement to be sent to a member,
  shareholder or director may be sent to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the member at the member's latest address as shown
    in the records of the cooperative;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the shareholder at the shareholder's latest address
    as shown in the records of the cooperative or its transfer agent;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the director at the director's latest address as
    shown in the records of the cooperative or in the last notice sent
    under section 81 or 91.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A director named in a notice sent by a cooperative to
  the Director under section 81 or 91 is presumed for the purposes of
  this Act to be a director of the cooperative referred to in the
  notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed receipt

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A notice or document sent in accordance with
  subsection (1) to a member, shareholder or director is deemed to have
  been received seven days after it was sent unless there are reasonable
  grounds to believe that the member, shareholder or director did not
  receive the notice or document at that time or at all.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Undelivered notice

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a cooperative sends a notice or document to a
  member or shareholder in accordance with subsection (1) and the notice
  or document is returned on two consecutive occasions because the
  member or shareholder cannot be found, the cooperative is not required
  to send any further notices or documents to the member or shareholder
  until the cooperative is informed in writing of their new address.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 362
- 2001, c. 14, s. 224(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to a cooperative

- **[[363]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-363 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  notice or document that is required to be sent to a cooperative may be
  sent to the cooperative's registered office shown in the last notice
  of registered office filed with the Director and, if so sent, is
  deemed to have been received seven days after it was sent unless there
  are reasonable grounds to believe that the cooperative did not receive
  the notice or document at that time or at all.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Service on a cooperative

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A notice or document that is required to be served on
  a cooperative may be served on it at the cooperative's registered
  office shown in the last notice of registered office filed with the
  Director.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Waiver of notice

**[[364]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-364 .sectionLabel}** When a notice or
document is required by this Act or the regulations to be sent, the
sending of the notice or document may be waived or the time for the
notice or document may be waived or abridged at any time with the
consent in writing of the person who is entitled to it.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 364
- 2001, c. 14, s. 225
:::

### [Certificates]{.HTitleText2} {#h-48895 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of Director

- **[[365]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-365 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  this Act requires or authorizes the Director to issue a certificate or
  to certify a fact, the certificate must be signed by the Director or
  by a Deputy Director authorized under section 371.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Evidence

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Except in a proceeding under section 312 to dissolve
  a cooperative, a certificate referred to in subsection (1) or a
  certified copy of one, when introduced as evidence in a civil,
  criminal, administrative, investigative or other action or proceeding,
  is conclusive proof of the facts so certified without proof of the
  signature or official character of the person appearing to have signed
  it.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of cooperative

- **[[366]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-366 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  certificate that is issued on behalf of a cooperative stating a fact
  that is set out in the articles, the by-laws, a unanimous agreement or
  the minutes of the meetings of the directors, a committee of
  directors, the members or the shareholders, or in a trust indenture or
  other contract to which the cooperative is a party, may be signed by a
  director, officer or transfer agent of the cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When introduced as evidence in a civil, criminal,
  administrative, investigative or other action or proceeding, the
  following things are, in the absence of evidence to the contrary,
  proof of the facts so certified without proof of the signature or
  official character of the person appearing to have signed the
  certificate:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a fact stated in a certificate referred to in
    subsection (1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a certified extract from a securities register or a
    members' register of a cooperative; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a certified copy of minutes or certified extract
    from minutes of a meeting of members, shareholders, directors or a
    committee of directors.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security certificate

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An entry in a securities register of, or a security
  certificate issued by, a cooperative is, in the absence of evidence to
  the contrary, proof that the person in whose name the security is
  registered is the owner of the securities described in the register or
  certificate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Membership certificate

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An entry in a members' register of, or a certificate
  of membership or a membership share certificate issued by, a
  cooperative is, in the absence of evidence to the contrary, proof that
  the person whose name appears in it or on it is a member or owns the
  membership shares as set out in the membership share certificate or
  the register.

### [Notices and Documents]{.HTitleText2} {#h-48913 .Subheading}

- **[[367]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-367 .sectionLabel}** [(1) to
  (3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2018, c. 8, s. 86\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Execution or signature of documents

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Any articles, notice, resolution, requisition,
  statement or other document required or permitted to be executed or,
  in Quebec, signed by more than one individual for the purposes of this
  Act may be executed or signed in several documents of like form, each
  of which is executed or signed by one or more of the individuals. The
  documents, when duly executed or signed by all individuals required or
  permitted, as the case may be, to do so, are deemed to constitute one
  document for the purposes of this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 367
- 2001, c. 14, s. 226
- 2011, c. 21, s. 117(E)
- 2018, c. 8, s. 86
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-367-20111129.html "Link to previous version of section 367"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies

- **[[368]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-368 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  a notice or document is required to be sent to the Director under this
  Act, the Director may accept a photostatic or photographic copy of it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content and form of documents

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Director may establish the requirements for the
  content and fix the form, including electronic or other forms, of
  notices and other documents sent to or issued by the Director under
  this Act and, in so doing, the Director may specify, among other
  things,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the notices and documents that may be transmitted
    in electronic or other form;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the persons or classes of persons who may sign or
    transmit the notices and other documents;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} their signature in electronic or other form, or the
    actions that are to have the same effect for the purposes of this
    Act as their signature; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the time and circumstances when electronic notices
    and documents are to be considered to be sent or received, and the
    place where they are considered to have been sent or received.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2018, c. 8, s. 87\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage of documents or information in
  electronic or other form

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, a document or information
  that is received by the Director under this Act in electronic or other
  form may be entered or recorded by an information storage device,
  including a system of mechanical or electronic data processing, that
  is capable of reproducing stored documents or information in
  intelligible form within a reasonable time.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 368
- 2018, c. 8, s. 87
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-368-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 368"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When notices or documents need not be
sent

**[[369]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-369 .sectionLabel}** The Director may, on
any conditions that the Director considers appropriate, determine that
notices or documents or classes of notices or documents need not be sent
to the Director under this Act if they are notices or documents that
contain information similar to information contained in notices or
documents required to be made public under any other Act of Parliament
or under an Act of the legislature of a province.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof required by Director

- **[[370]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-370 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Director may require that a document or a fact stated in a document
  required by this Act or the regulations to be sent to the Director be
  verified in accordance with subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form of proof

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A document or fact that the Director requires to be
  verified may be verified by affidavit or by statutory declaration
  under the [Canada Evidence Act](/eng/acts/C-5) before any commissioner
  for oaths or for taking affidavits.

### [Appointment of Director]{.HTitleText2} {#h-48941 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appointment

**[[371]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-371 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may
appoint a Director and one or more Deputy Directors to perform the
duties and exercise the powers of the Director under this Act.

### [Regulations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-48944 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[372]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-372 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} providing for anything that by this Act is to be
    prescribed or provided for by the regulations;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} defining, enlarging or restricting the meaning of
    any word or expression used but not defined in this Act;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} requiring the payment of a fee in respect of the
    receipt, examination, filing, issuance or copying of any document,
    or in respect of any action that the Director is required or
    authorized to take under this Act, and prescribing the amount of the
    fee or the manner of determining the fee;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} respecting the payment of any fees, including the
    time when and the manner in which the fees are to be paid, the
    additional fees that may be charged for the late payment of fees and
    the circumstances in which any fees previously paid may be refunded
    in whole or in part;

  - [(d.1)]{.lawlabel} prescribing, for the purposes of subsection
    58(2.1), a manner of determining the number of investment shares
    required for a person to be eligible to make a proposal, including
    the time and manner of determining a value or percentage of the
    outstanding investment shares;

  - [(d.2)]{.lawlabel} prescribing, for the purposes of paragraph
    58(4)(d), the minimum amount of support required in relation to the
    number of times the person has submitted substantially the same
    proposal within the prescribed period;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} prescribing rules with respect to exemptions
    permitted by this Act;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} respecting the powers that may be granted by a
    shareholder in a form of proxy;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} prescribing any matter necessary for the purposes
    of the application of Part 21.1, including the time and
    circumstances when an electronic document is to be considered to
    have been provided or received and the place where it is considered
    to have been provided or received;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the manner of, and conditions for,
    participating in a meeting by means of a telephonic, electronic or
    other communication facility that permits all participants to
    communicate adequately with each other during the meeting; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} prescribing, for the purposes of subsection 65(3),
    the manner of, and conditions for, voting at a meeting of a
    cooperative by means of a telephonic, electronic or other
    communication facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incorporation by reference

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The regulations may incorporate any material by
  reference, regardless of its source and either as it exists on a
  particular date or as amended from time to time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incorporated material is not a
  regulation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Material does not become a regulation for the
  purposes of the [Statutory Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22) because it
  is incorporated by reference.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 372
- 2001, c. 14, s. 227
- 2018, c. 8, s. 89
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-372-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 372"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment of fees

**[[372.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-372.1 .sectionLabel}** A fee in respect of
the receipt or copying of any document must be paid to the Director when
it is received or copied, and a fee in respect of the examination,
filing or issuance of any document or in respect of any action that the
Director is required or authorized to take must be paid to the Director
before the document is examined, filed or issued or the action is taken.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 228
- 2018, c. 8, s. 90
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-372.1-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 372.1"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Statements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-48969 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
[[statement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[373]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-373 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, [[statement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a statement of
  intent to dissolve and a statement of revocation of intent to dissolve
  referred to in section 310.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sending of articles and statements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If this Act requires that articles or a statement
  relating to a cooperative be sent to the Director, on receiving the
  articles or statement in the form that the Director fixes, any other
  required documents and the required fees, the Director shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} record the date of receipt;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} issue the appropriate certificate;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} send the certificate, or a copy, image or
    photographic, electronic or other reproduction of the certificate,
    to the cooperative or its agent or mandatary; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} publish a notice of the issuance of the certificate
    in a publication generally available to the public.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date of certificate

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A certificate referred to in subsection (2) that is
  issued by the Director may be dated as of the date of the receipt of
  the articles, statement or court order pursuant to which the
  certificate was issued or as of any later date specified by the court
  or person who signed the articles or statement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Signature

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A signature required on a certificate issued by the
  Director under this Act may be printed or otherwise reproduced on the
  certificate or may be made in accordance with the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date of certificate

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (3), a certificate of
  discontinuance may be dated as of the day a cooperative amalgamates
  under another Act or is continued.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 373
- 2001, c. 14, s. 229
- 2011, c. 21, s. 118(E)
- 2018, c. 8, s. 91
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-373-20111129.html "Link to previous version of section 373"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication

**[[373.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-373.1 .sectionLabel}** The Director must
publish, in a publication generally available to the public, a notice of
any decision made by the Director granting an application made under
subsection 4(4) or 167(1), section 248 or subsection 263(2) or 267(2).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 8, s. 92
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Annual return

**[[374]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-374 .sectionLabel}** Every cooperative must
send the Director an annual return in the form and within the period
that the Director fixes.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 374
- 2018, c. 8, s. 92
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-374-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 374"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate

- **[[375]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-375 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Director may provide any person with a certificate that a cooperative

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has sent to the Director a document required to be
    sent;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has paid all required fees; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} exists as of a certain date.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Director may refuse to issue
  certificate of existence

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the Director may refuse to
  issue a certificate under paragraph (1)(c) if the Director has
  knowledge that the cooperative is in default of sending a document
  required to be sent or is in default of paying a required fee.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 375
- 2001, c. 14, s. 230
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alteration

**[[376]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-376 .sectionLabel}** The Director may alter
a notice or document, other than an affidavit or statutory declaration,
if authorized to do so by the person who sent the document or by their
representative.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 376
- 2001, c. 14, s. 230
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Corrections at request of Director

- **[[376.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-376.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If there is an error in articles, a
  notice, a certificate or other document, the directors, members or
  shareholders must, on the request of the Director, pass the
  resolutions and send to the Director the documents required to comply
  with this Act, and take any other steps that the Director may
  reasonably require so that the Director may correct the document.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No prejudice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Before proceeding under subsection (1), the Director
  must be satisfied that the correction would not prejudice any of the
  members, shareholders or creditors of the cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Corrections at the request of the
  cooperative

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Director may, at the request of the cooperative
  or of any other interested person, accept a correction to any of the
  documents referred to in subsection (1) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the correction is approved by the directors of the
    cooperative, unless the error is obvious or was made by the
    Director; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Director is satisfied that the correction would
    not prejudice any of the members, shareholders or creditors of the
    cooperative and that the correction reflects the original intention
    of the cooperative or the incorporators, as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to court

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If, in the view of the Director, of the cooperative
  or of any interested person who wishes a correction, a correction to
  any of the documents referred to in subsection (1) would prejudice any
  of the members, shareholders or creditors of a cooperative, the
  Director, the cooperative or the person, as the case may be, may apply
  to the court for an order that the document be corrected and for an
  order determining the rights of the members, shareholders or
  creditors.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to Director

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} An applicant under subsection (4) must give the
  Director notice of the application, and the Director is entitled to
  appear and to be heard in person or by counsel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Director may require surrender of
  document

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Director may demand the surrender of the original
  document, and may issue a corrected certificate or file the corrected
  articles, notice or other document.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date of corrected document

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} A corrected document must bear the date of the
  document it replaces unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the correction is made with respect to the date of
    the document, in which case the document must bear the corrected
    date; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the court decides otherwise.
