- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} If a corrected certificate materially amends the
  terms of the original certificate, the Director must without delay
  give notice of the correction in a publication generally available to
  the public.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 230
- 2018, c. 8, s. 93(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-376.1-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 376.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation of articles by Director

- **[[376.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-376.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In the prescribed circumstances, the
  Director may cancel the articles and related certificate of a
  cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No prejudice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Before proceeding under subsection (1), the Director
  must be satisfied that the cancellation would not prejudice any of the
  members, shareholders or creditors of the cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request to Director to cancel articles

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the prescribed circumstances, the Director may, at
  the request of a cooperative or of any other interested person, cancel
  the articles and related certificate of the cooperative if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cancellation is approved by the directors of
    the cooperative; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Director is satisfied that the cancellation
    would not prejudice any of the members, shareholders or creditors of
    the cooperative and that the cancellation reflects the original
    intention of the cooperative or the incorporators, as the case may
    be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to court

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If, in the view of the Director, of the cooperative
  or of any interested person who wishes a cancellation, a cancellation
  of articles and a related certificate would prejudice any of the
  members, shareholders or creditors of a cooperative, the Director, the
  cooperative or the person, as the case may be, may apply to the court
  for an order that the articles and certificate be cancelled and for an
  order determining the rights of the members, shareholders or
  creditors.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to Director

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} An applicant under subsection (4) must give the
  Director notice of the application, and the Director is entitled to
  appear and to be heard in person or by counsel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of certificate

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Director may demand the surrender of a cancelled
  certificate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 230
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inspection

- **[[377]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-377 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who has paid the required fee is entitled during usual business
  hours to examine and make copies of or take extracts from a document,
  except a report sent to the Director under subsection 330(2), that is
  required to be sent to the Director under this Act, or that was
  required to be sent to a person performing a similar function under
  prior legislation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies and extracts

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Director must, on request, provide any person
  with a copy, extract, certified copy or certified extract of a
  document that may be examined under subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 377
- 2001, c. 14, s. 231
- [2018, c. 8, s. 94](#1037749-1373784){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037749-1373784.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-377-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 377"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Records]{.HTitleText2} {#h-49056 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Director's obligation to keep documents

- **[[378]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-378
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Documents that are received and
  accepted by the Director under this Act or that were received and
  accepted by a person performing a similar function under prior
  legislation must be kept by the Director in any form.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation to furnish

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If documents are kept by the Director otherwise than
  in written form,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Director must provide any copy required to be
    provided under subsection 377(2) in intelligible written form; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a report reproduced from those documents, if it is
    certified by the Director, is admissible in evidence to the same
    extent as the original documents would have been.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time period for keeping and producing
  documents

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Director is not required to keep or produce any
  document or class of documents --- other than a certificate and any
  attached articles or statement received under section 373 and other
  prescribed documents or prescribed class of documents --- after the
  end of the prescribed period for the keeping or production of the
  document or class of documents.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form of publication

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Information or notices required by this Act to be
  summarized in a publication generally available to the public or to be
  published by the Director may be made available to the public or
  published by a system of mechanical or electronic data processing or
  any other information storage device that is capable of reproducing
  any required information or notice in intelligible form within a
  reasonable time.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1998, c. 1, s. 378
- 2001, c. 14, s. 232
- 2018, c. 8, s. 95
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-378-20180501.html "Link to previous version of section 378"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 23]{.HLabel1}[Continuance]{.HTitleText1} {#h-49070 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuance under this Act

- **[[379]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-379
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to paragraphs 7(3)(b) and
  (c), on the coming into force of this subsection,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} each former Act cooperative is deemed to be a
    cooperative incorporated under this Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any provision contained in the articles of
    association and the charter by-laws of a former Act cooperative that
    is required to be contained in the articles of incorporation of a
    cooperative under this Act is deemed to be contained in the articles
    of that cooperative; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any share that has been issued by the cooperative
    that is not a membership share is deemed to be an investment share.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendment to articles

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Each former Act cooperative must, not later than five
  years after the coming into force of this subsection, amend its
  articles so that they comply with section 11 and file them with the
  Director.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Failure to comply

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a cooperative referred to in subsection (1) does
  not comply with subsection (2), the Director may, after giving not
  less than one hundred and eighty days notice after the end of the five
  years to the cooperative, dissolve the cooperative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No member or shareholder rights

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Any amendment to the articles of a cooperative to
  comply with subsection (2) does not give rise to any rights by a
  member or shareholder under section 302.

an Act enacted by this Act comes into force on a day or days to be fixed
by order of the Governor in Council.

## RELATED PROVISIONS {#related-provisions .scheduleTitleText}

- --- 2011, c. 25, s. 42

  - ###### Submission to Minister {#submission-to-minister .MarginalNote}

    - [42]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Corporation must submit an
      application for continuance under one of the following Acts for
      the Minister's approval:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the [Canada Business Corporations
        Act](/eng/acts/C-44);

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the [Canada Cooperatives Act](/eng/acts/C-1.7);
        or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the [Canada Not-for-profit Corporations
        Act](/eng/acts/C-7.75).

    - ###### Deadline {#deadline .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} The application must be submitted to the Minister
      within four years, or any shorter period specified by the
      Minister, after the day on which this Part comes into force.

    - ###### Submission to applicable authorities {#submission-to-applicable-authorities .MarginalNote}

      [(3)]{.lawlabel} Once the application has been approved by the
      Minister, the Corporation must submit the application to the
      applicable authorities under the relevant Act.

    - ###### Application not invalid {#application-not-invalid .MarginalNote}

      [(4)]{.lawlabel} The application is not invalid solely because the
      Corporation is incorporated by an Act of Parliament.
:::
::::

:::: section
::: ScheduleNIF
## AMENDMENTS NOT IN FORCE {#amendments-not-in-force .scheduleTitleText}

- --- 2018, c. 8, s. 63

  - [63]{.lawlabel} Subsection 169(1) of the Act is replaced by the
    following:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - ###### Duty of intermediary {#duty-of-intermediary .MarginalNote}

      - [169]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Shares of a cooperative that
        are registered in the name of an intermediary or their nominee
        and not beneficially owned by the intermediary must not be voted
        unless the intermediary, without delay after receipt of the
        prescribed documents, sends a copy of those documents to the
        beneficial owner and, except when the intermediary has received
        written voting instructions from the beneficial owner, a written
        request for those instructions.
    :::
    ::::

- --- 2018, c. 8, s. 71

  - [71]{.lawlabel} Section 251 of the Act is replaced by the following:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - ###### Copies to members and shareholders {#copies-to-members-and-shareholders .MarginalNote}

      [251]{.lawlabel} A cooperative shall send, within a prescribed
      period, a copy of the prescribed documents relating to financial
      disclosure to the prescribed shareholders, prescribed members and
      other prescribed persons.
    :::
    ::::

- --- 2018, c. 8, s. 88

  - [88]{.lawlabel} The Act is amended by adding the following after
    section 369:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - ###### Exemption {#exemption .MarginalNote}

      [369.1]{.lawlabel} The Director may, in the prescribed
      circumstances and on any conditions that the Director considers
      appropriate, exempt any cooperative or any other person from a
      requirement set out in subsection 52(1), 165(1), 166(1) or 169(1)
      or section 251 to send any notices or other documents, or classes
      of notices or other documents.
    :::
    ::::
:::
